01792: function computeRecentBichoMetrics(draws, groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
01793:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
01794:   const out = new Map();
01795: 
01796:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
01797:     out.set(g, {
01798:       recentTop5: 0,
01799:       recentFirst: 0,
01800:       recentDupDraws: 0,
01801:       recentLast1Top5: 0,
01802:       recentLast2Top5: 0,
01803:       recentLast3Top5: 0,
01804:     });
01805:   }
01806: 
01807:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
01808:   const last1 = list.length ? [list[list.length - 1]] : [];
01809:   const last2 = list.slice(-2);
01810:   const last3 = list.slice(-3);
01811: 
01812:   for (const d of list) {
01813:     const items = getAllTop5Groups(d);
01814:     const perDrawCounts = new Map();
01815: 
01816:     for (const it of items) {
01817:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
01818:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
01819: 
01820:       const row = out.get(g);
01821:       row.recentTop5 += 1;
01822: 
01823:       if (Number(it.pos) === 1) {
01824:         row.recentFirst += 1;
01825:       }
01826: 
01827:       perDrawCounts.set(g, Number(perDrawCounts.get(g) || 0) + 1);
01828:     }
01829: 
01830:     for (const [g, c] of perDrawCounts.entries()) {
01831:       if (Number(c) >= 2) {
01832:         out.get(Number(g)).recentDupDraws += 1;
01833:       }
01834:     }
01835:   }
01836: 
01837:   for (const d of last1) {
01838:     const seen = new Set();
01839:     for (const it of getAllTop5Groups(d)) {
01840:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
01841:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k || seen.has(g)) continue;
01842:       seen.add(g);
01843:       out.get(g).recentLast1Top5 += 1;
01844:     }
01845:   }
01846: 
01847:   for (const d of last2) {
01848:     const seen = new Set();
01849:     for (const it of getAllTop5Groups(d)) {
01850:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
01851:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k || seen.has(g)) continue;
01852:       seen.add(g);
01853:       out.get(g).recentLast2Top5 += 1;
01854:     }
01855:   }
01856: 
01857:   for (const d of last3) {
01858:     const seen = new Set();
01859:     for (const it of getAllTop5Groups(d)) {
01860:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
01861:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k || seen.has(g)) continue;
01862:       seen.add(g);
01863:       out.get(g).recentLast3Top5 += 1;
01864:     }
01865:   }
01866: 
01867:   return out;
01868: }
01869: 
01870: function getDayDrivenWeights(mode) {
01871:   if (mode === "REPEAT") {
01872:     return {
01873:       transition: 0.25,
01874:       pair: 0.25,
01875:       memory: 0.2,
01876:       recent: 0.15,
01877:       structural: 0.1,
01878:       late: 0.05,
01879:     };
01880:   }
01881: 
01882:   if (mode === "SPREAD") {
01883:     return {
01884:       transition: 0.2,
01885:       pair: 0.1,
01886:       memory: 0.1,
01887:       recent: 0.2,
01888:       structural: 0.3,
01889:       late: 0.1,
01890:     };
01891:   }
01892: 
01893:   return {
01894:     transition: 0.3,
01895:     pair: 0.15,
01896:     memory: 0.15,
01897:     recent: 0.15,
01898:     structural: 0.2,
01899:     late: 0.05,
01900:   };
01901: }
01902: 
01903: function normalizeWeights(weights) {
01904:   const safe = {
01905:     transition: Number(weights?.transition || 0),
01906:     pair: Number(weights?.pair || 0),
01907:     memory: Number(weights?.memory || 0),
01908:     recent: Number(weights?.recent || 0),
01909:     structural: Number(weights?.structural || 0),
01910:     late: Number(weights?.late || 0),
01911:   };
01912: 
01913:   const total = Object.values(safe).reduce((acc, n) => acc + n, 0);
01914:   if (!Number.isFinite(total) || total <= 0) {
01915:     return {
01916:       transition: 0.3,
01917:       pair: 0.15,
01918:       memory: 0.15,
01919:       recent: 0.15,
01920:       structural: 0.2,
01921:       late: 0.05,
01922:     };
01923:   }
01924: 
01925:   return {
01926:     transition: safe.transition / total,
01927:     pair: safe.pair / total,
01928:     memory: safe.memory / total,
01929:     recent: safe.recent / total,
01930:     structural: safe.structural / total,
01931:     late: safe.late / total,
01932:   };
01933: }
01934: 
01935: function computeComparableLastSeenByGrupo(
01936:   draws,
01937:   lotteryKey,
01938:   targetHour,
01939:   targetDow,
01940:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
01941: ) {
01942:   const list = getComparableDrawsForTargetContext(
01943:     draws,
01944:     lotteryKey,
01945:     targetHour,
01946:     targetDow
01947:   );
01948: 
01949:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
01950:   const last = new Map();
01951: 
01952:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
01953:     last.set(g, Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY);
01954:   }
01955: 
01956:   for (const d of list) {
01957:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
01958:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
01959:     const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
01960:     const items = getAllTop5Groups(d);
01961: 
01962:     for (const it of items) {
01963:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
01964:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
01965: 
01966:       const prev = Number(last.get(g));
01967:       if (!Number.isFinite(prev) || ts > prev) {
01968:         last.set(g, ts);
01969:       }
01970:     }
01971:   }
01972: 
01973:   return last;
01974: }
01975: 
01976: function buildDayContext(drawsToday = []) {
01977:   const list = sortDrawsAsc(Array.isArray(drawsToday) ? drawsToday : []);
01978:   const freq = new Map();
01979:   const firstFreq = new Map();
01980:   const seq = [];
01981: 
01982:   for (const d of list) {
01983:     const g = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(d);
01984:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(g)) || Number(g) < 1 || Number(g) > 25) continue;
01985: 
01986:     const gg = Number(g);
01987:     seq.push(gg);
01988:     freq.set(gg, Number(freq.get(gg) || 0) + 1);
01989: 
01990:     const items = getAllTop5Groups(d);
01991:     if (items.some((it) => Number(it.grupo) === gg && Number(it.pos) === 1)) {
01992:       firstFreq.set(gg, Number(firstFreq.get(gg) || 0) + 1);
01993:     }
01994:   }
01995: 
01996:   const total = seq.length;
01997:   const unique = new Set(seq).size;
01998:   const diversidade = total > 0 ? unique / total : 0;
01999: 
02000:   let dominante = null;
02001:   let dominanteCount = -1;
02002:   for (const [g, n] of freq.entries()) {
02003:     if (Number(n) > dominanteCount) {
02004:       dominante = Number(g);
02005:       dominanteCount = Number(n);
02006:     }
02007:   }
02008: 
02009:   const last1 = total >= 1 ? seq[total - 1] : null;
02010:   const last2 = total >= 2 ? seq[total - 2] : null;
02011:   const repeatNow =
02012:     Number.isFinite(Number(last1)) &&
02013:     Number.isFinite(Number(last2)) &&
02014:     Number(last1) === Number(last2);
02015: 
02016:   return {
02017:     total,
02018:     unique,
02019:     diversidade,
02020:     dominante,
02021:     dominanteCount: Math.max(0, dominanteCount),
02022:     repeatNow,
02023:     freq,
02024:     firstFreq,
02025:     seq,
02026:   };
02027: }
02028: 
02029: function buildPairSequenceDistribution({
02030:   drawsRange,
02031:   lotteryKey,
02032:   targetHour,
02033:   targetDow,
02034:   targetDayOfMonth,
02035:   prevPair,
02036:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
02037:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
02038:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
02039:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K,
02040: }) {
02041:   const seq = buildComparableFirstSequence(
02042:     Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? drawsRange : [],
02043:     lotteryKey,
02044:     targetHour,
02045:     targetDow
02046:   );
02047: 
02048:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
02049:   const outFreq = new Map();
02050: 
02051:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) outFreq.set(g, 0);
02052: 
02053:   const pair = Array.isArray(prevPair) ? prevPair : [];
02054:   if (pair.length < 2) {
02055:     return {
02056:       prob: buildZeroProbMap(k),
02057:       samples: 0,
02058:       weightedSamples: 0,
02059:       exactDomSamples: 0,
02060:       freq: outFreq,
02061:     };
02062:   }
02063: 
02064:   const pA = Number(pair[0]);
02065:   const pB = Number(pair[1]);
02066:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
02067: 
02068:   let matchedSamples = 0;
02069:   let weightedSamples = 0;
02070:   let exactDomSamples = 0;
02071: 
02072:   for (let i = 2; i < seq.length; i += 1) {
02073:     const a = Number(seq[i - 2]?.grupo);
02074:     const b = Number(seq[i - 1]?.grupo);
02075:     const g = Number(seq[i]?.grupo);
02076:     const y = safeStr(seq[i]?.ymd);
02077: 
02078:     if (!Number.isFinite(a) || !Number.isFinite(b) || !Number.isFinite(g)) continue;
02079:     if (a !== pA || b !== pB) continue;
02080:     if (!isYMD(y)) continue;
02081: 
02082:     matchedSamples += 1;
02083: 
02084:     let weight = 1;
02085: 
02086:     if (key !== "FEDERAL") {
02087:       const dom = Number(getDayOfMonth(y));
02088:       if (Number.isFinite(dom) && Number(dom) === Number(targetDayOfMonth)) {
02089:         weight = 1.35;
02090:         exactDomSamples += 1;
02091:       }
02092:     }
02093: 
02094:     weightedSamples += weight;
02095:     outFreq.set(g, Number(outFreq.get(g) || 0) + weight);
02096:   }
02097: 
02098:   const prob = new Map();
02099:   const denom = Math.max(1, weightedSamples);
02100: 
02101:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
02102:     prob.set(g, Number(outFreq.get(g) || 0) / denom);
02103:   }
02104: 
02105:   return {
02106:     prob,
02107:     samples: matchedSamples,
02108:     weightedSamples,
02109:     exactDomSamples,
02110:     freq: outFreq,
02111:   };
02112: }
02113: 
02114: function buildSeq2FirstDistribution(
02115:   comparableSeq,
02116:   currentState,
02117:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
02118: ) {
02119:   const seq = Array.isArray(comparableSeq) ? comparableSeq : [];
02120:   const state = Array.isArray(currentState) ? currentState : [];
02121:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
02122: 
02123:   const freq = new Map();
02124:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) freq.set(g, 0);
02125: 
02126:   if (state.length < 2) {
02127:     const prob = new Map();
02128:     for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) prob.set(g, 0);
02129:     return { prob, freq, samples: 0, prev2: null, prev1: null };
02130:   }
02131: 
02132:   const prev1 = Number(state[0]);
02133:   const prev2 = Number(state[1]);
02134: 
02135:   let samples = 0;
02136: 
02137:   for (let i = 2; i < seq.length; i += 1) {
02138:     const gA = Number(seq[i - 2]?.grupo);
02139:     const gB = Number(seq[i - 1]?.grupo);
02140:     const gN = Number(seq[i]?.grupo);
02141: 
02142:     if (!Number.isFinite(gA) || !Number.isFinite(gB) || !Number.isFinite(gN)) continue;
02143:     if (gA !== prev2 || gB !== prev1) continue;
02144:     if (gN < 1 || gN > k) continue;
02145: 
02146:     samples += 1;
02147:     freq.set(gN, Number(freq.get(gN) || 0) + 1);
02148:   }
02149: 
02150:   const prob = new Map();
02151:   const denom = Math.max(1, samples);
02152: 
02153:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
02154:     prob.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) / denom);
02155:   }
02156: 
02157:   return { prob, freq, samples, prev2, prev1 };
02158: }
02159: 
02160: function buildRecentRepeatBoostMap(
02161:   comparableSeq,
02162:   targetTs,
02163:   windowSize = 4,
02164:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
02165: ) {
02166:   const seq = getComparableRecentDrawsBeforeTs(
02167:     Array.isArray(comparableSeq) ? comparableSeq : [],
02168:     targetTs,
02169:     windowSize
02170:   );
02171: 
02172:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
02173:   const counts = new Map();
02174:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) counts.set(g, 0);
02175: 
02176:   for (const item of seq) {
02177:     const g = Number(item?.grupo);
02178:     if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
02179:     counts.set(g, Number(counts.get(g) || 0) + 1);
02180:   }
02181: 
02182:   const boost = new Map();
02183:   const maxCount = Math.max(1, ...Array.from(counts.values()).map((n) => Number(n || 0)));
02184: 
02185:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
02186:     const c = Number(counts.get(g) || 0);
02187:     boost.set(g, c >= 2 ? c / maxCount : 0);
02188:   }
02189: 
02190:   return {
02191:     boost,
02192:     counts,
02193:     windowSize,
02194:     considered: Array.isArray(seq) ? seq.length : 0,
02195:   };
02196: }
02197: 
02198: /* =========================
02199:    Motor principal V2 (FOCO = BICHO)
02200: ========================= */
02201: 
02202: export function computeConditionalNextTop3V2({
02203:   lotteryKey,
02204:   drawsRange,
02205:   drawLast,
02206:   drawsToday,
02207:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
02208:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
02209:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
02210:   topN = 3,
02211:   targetYmdOverride = "",
02212:   targetHourOverride = "",
02213: }) {
02214:   const list = Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? drawsRange : [];
02215:   if (!list.length || !drawLast) {
02216:     return { top: [], meta: null };
02217:   }
02218: 
02219:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
02220: 
02221:   const lastY = pickDrawYMD(drawLast);
02222:   const lastH = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(drawLast));
02223:   const prevGrupo = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(drawLast);
02224: 
02225:   if (
02226:     !isYMD(lastY) ||
02227:     !lastH ||
02228:     !Number.isFinite(Number(prevGrupo)) ||
02229:     Number(prevGrupo) <= 0
02230:   ) {
02231:     return { top: [], meta: null };
02232:   }
02233: 
02234:   const nextSlot = getNextSlotForLottery({
02235:     lotteryKey,
02236:     ymd: lastY,
02237:     hourBucket: lastH,
02238:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
02239:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
02240:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
02241:   });
02242: 
02243:   const forcedTargetY = safeStr(targetYmdOverride);
02244:   const forcedTargetH = toHourBucket(targetHourOverride);
02245: 
02246:   const targetY = isYMD(forcedTargetY) && forcedTargetH
02247:     ? forcedTargetY
02248:     : safeStr(nextSlot?.ymd);
02249: 
02250:   const targetH = isYMD(forcedTargetY) && forcedTargetH
02251:     ? forcedTargetH
02252:     : toHourBucket(nextSlot?.hour);
02253:   const targetDow = getDowKey(targetY);
02254:   const targetDayOfMonth = getDayOfMonth(targetY);
02255: 
02256:   if (!isYMD(targetY) || !targetH || !Number.isFinite(Number(targetDow))) {
02257:     return { top: [], meta: null };
02258:   }
02259: 
02260:   const targetTs = ymdHourToTs(targetY, targetH);
02261: 
02262:   const dayContext = buildDayContext(drawsToday);
02263:   const prevPair =
02264:     Array.isArray(dayContext?.seq) && dayContext.seq.length >= 2
02265:       ? dayContext.seq.slice(-2)
02266:       : [];
02267: 
02268:   const pairOut = buildPairSequenceDistribution({
02269:     drawsRange: list,
02270:     lotteryKey: key,
02271:     targetHour: targetH,
02272:     targetDow,
02273:     targetDayOfMonth,
02274:     prevPair,
02275:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
02276:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
02277:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
02278:     groupsK: TOP3_GROUPS_K,
02279:   });
02280: 
02281:   const pairConfidence = clamp01(
02282:     (confidenceFromSamples(pairOut?.samples || 0, 6) * 0.7) +
02283:     (confidenceFromSamples(pairOut?.exactDomSamples || 0, 3) * 0.3)
02284:   );
02285: 
02286:   const layerOut = buildConditionalLayerDistribution({
02287:     lotteryKey,
02288:     drawsRange: list,
02289:     targetHour: targetH,
02290:     targetDow,
02291:     targetDayOfMonth,
02292:     prevHour: lastH,
02293:     prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
02294:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
02295:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
02296:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
02297:   });
02298: 
02299:   const chosen = layerOut?.chosen || null;
02300:   const condSamples = Number(chosen?.samples || 0);
02301:   const condFirstFreq = chosen?.firstFreq || new Map();
02302: 
02303:   const pTransitionFirst = new Map();
02304:   for (let g = 1; g <= safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25); g += 1) {
02305:     pTransitionFirst.set(
02306:       g,
02307:       Number(condFirstFreq.get(g) || 0) / Math.max(1, condSamples)
02308:     );
02309:   }
02310: 
02311:   const structuralFirst = computeStructuralFirstDistribution(
02312:     list,
02313:     key,
02314:     targetH,
02315:     targetDow,
02316:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
02317:   );
02318:   const pStructuralFirst = structuralFirst?.prob || new Map();
02319: 
02320:   const structuralTop5 = computeComparableTop5Distribution(
02321:     list,
02322:     key,
02323:     targetH,
02324:     targetDow,
02325:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
02326:   );
02327:   const pStructuralTop5 = structuralTop5?.prob || new Map();
02328: 
02329:   const duplication = computeComparableDuplicationDistribution(
02330:     list,
02331:     key,
02332:     targetH,
02333:     targetDow,
02334:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
02335:   );
02336:   const pDuplication = duplication?.prob || new Map();
02337: 
02338:   const comparableSeq = buildComparableFirstSequence(
02339:     list,
02340:     key,
02341:     targetH,
02342:     targetDow
02343:   );
02344: 
02345:   const currentState = getStateFromComparableSequenceBeforeTs(
02346:     comparableSeq,
02347:     targetTs,
02348:     3
02349:   );
02350: 
02351:   const memoryOut = buildMemoryStateDistribution({
02352:     drawsRange: list,
02353:     lotteryKey: key,
02354:     targetHour: targetH,
02355:     targetDow,
02356:     currentState,
02357:     groupsK: TOP3_GROUPS_K,
02358:   });
02359: 
02360:   const pMemory = memoryOut?.prob || new Map();
02361: 
02362:   const seq2Out = buildSeq2FirstDistribution(
02363:     comparableSeq,
02364:     currentState,
02365:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
02366:   );
02367:   const pSeq2 = seq2Out?.prob || new Map();
02368: 
02369:   const repeatBoostOut = buildRecentRepeatBoostMap(
02370:     comparableSeq,
02371:     targetTs,
02372:     4,
02373:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
02374:   );
02375:   const repeatBoostMap = repeatBoostOut?.boost || new Map();
02376: 
02377:   const regimeInfo = detectRegimeFromComparableSequence(
02378:     getComparableRecentDrawsBeforeTs(comparableSeq, targetTs, 6)
02379:   );
02380: 
02381:   const regime = safeStr(regimeInfo?.regime || "neutral");
02382: 
02383:   const mode =
02384:     dayContext.repeatNow || Number(dayContext?.diversidade || 0) < 0.5
02385:       ? "REPEAT"
02386:       : Number(dayContext?.diversidade || 0) > 0.75
02387:         ? "SPREAD"
02388:         : "NEUTRAL";
02389: 
02390:   const weights = getDayDrivenWeights(mode);
02391: 
02392:   const recentContextDraws = getComparableContextDrawsBeforeTs(
02393:     list,
02394:     key,
02395:     targetH,
02396:     targetDow,
02397:     targetTs,
02398:     8
02399:   );
02400:   const recentMetrics = computeRecentBichoMetrics(recentContextDraws, TOP3_GROUPS_K);
02401: 
02402:   const recentMaxTop5 = Math.max(
02403:     1,
02404:     ...Array.from(recentMetrics.values()).map((x) => Number(x.recentTop5 || 0))
02405:   );
02406:   const recentMaxFirst = Math.max(
02407:     1,
02408:     ...Array.from(recentMetrics.values()).map((x) => Number(x.recentFirst || 0))
02409:   );
02410:   const recentMaxDup = Math.max(
02411:     1,
02412:     ...Array.from(recentMetrics.values()).map((x) => Number(x.recentDupDraws || 0))
02413:   );
02414: 
02415:   const lateSeen = computeComparableLastSeenByGrupo(
02416:     list,
02417:     key,
02418:     targetH,
02419:     targetDow,
02420:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
02421:   );
02422: 
02423:   const finiteSeen = Array.from(lateSeen.values()).filter((x) =>
02424:     Number.isFinite(Number(x))
02425:   );
02426:   const minSeenTs = finiteSeen.length ? Math.min(...finiteSeen) : targetTs;
02427:   const maxGapMsBase =
02428:     Number.isFinite(targetTs) && Number.isFinite(minSeenTs)
02429:       ? Math.max(1, targetTs - minSeenTs)
02430:       : 1;
02431: 
02432:   const dayFlowConfidence = confidenceFromSamples(dayContext?.total || 0, 4);
02433:   const pairStrong = Number(pairOut?.samples || 0) >= 4;
02434: 
02435:   const confidence =
02436:     (pairConfidence * 0.3) +
02437:     (confidenceFromSamples(condSamples, 20) * 0.4) +
02438:     (confidenceFromSamples(memoryOut?.matchedSamples || 0, 15) * 0.3);
02439: 
02440:   const adjustedWeights = normalizeWeights({
02441:     transition: pairStrong ? weights.transition * 0.7 : weights.transition,
02442:     pair: pairStrong ? weights.pair * 1.4 : weights.pair,
02443:     memory: weights.memory,
02444:     recent: weights.recent,
02445:     structural: weights.structural,
02446:     late: weights.late,
02447:   });
02448: 
02449:   const ranked = Array.from({ length: safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25) }, (_, idx) => {
02450:     const grupo = idx + 1;
02451: 
02452:     const pT = Number(pTransitionFirst.get(grupo) || 0);
02453:     const pSF = Number(pStructuralFirst.get(grupo) || 0);
02454:     const pST = Number(pStructuralTop5.get(grupo) || 0);
02455:     const pM = Number(pMemory.get(grupo) || 0);
02456:     const pD = Number(pDuplication.get(grupo) || 0);
02457:     const pSeq = Number(pSeq2.get(grupo) || 0);
02458:     const repeatBoost = Number(repeatBoostMap.get(grupo) || 0);
02459:     const pPair = Number(pairOut?.prob?.get?.(grupo) || 0);
02460: 
02461:     const rm = recentMetrics.get(grupo) || {
02462:       recentTop5: 0,
02463:       recentFirst: 0,
02464:       recentDupDraws: 0,
02465:       recentLast1Top5: 0,
02466:       recentLast2Top5: 0,
02467:       recentLast3Top5: 0,
02468:     };
02469: 
02470:     const recentTop5Norm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentTop5, recentMaxTop5);
02471:     const recentFirstNorm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentFirst, recentMaxFirst);
02472:     const recentDupNorm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentDupDraws, recentMaxDup);
02473:     const recentLast1Norm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentLast1Top5, 1);
02474:     const recentLast2Norm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentLast2Top5, 2);
02475: 
02476:     const recentComposite =
02477:       (recentFirstNorm * 0.34) +
02478:       (recentTop5Norm * 0.24) +
02479:       (recentDupNorm * 0.18) +
02480:       (recentLast2Norm * 0.14) +
02481:       (recentLast1Norm * 0.1);
02482: 
02483:     const dominanceScore =
02484:       (pT * 0.42) +
02485:       (pSF * 0.26) +
02486:       (pST * 0.18) +
02487:       (pD * 0.14);
02488: 
02489:     const seenTs = Number(lateSeen.get(grupo));
02490:     const gapMs =
02491:       Number.isFinite(targetTs) && Number.isFinite(seenTs)
02492:         ? Math.max(0, targetTs - seenTs)
02493:         : maxGapMsBase;
02494: 
02495:     const lateNorm = Math.max(
02496:       0,
02497:       Math.min(1, gapMs / Math.max(1, maxGapMsBase))
02498:     );
02499: 
02500:     const dayFreq = Number(dayContext?.freq?.get?.(grupo) || 0);
02501:     const dayFirstFreq = Number(dayContext?.firstFreq?.get?.(grupo) || 0);
02502:     const isDominantToday =
02503:       Number.isFinite(Number(dayContext?.dominante)) &&
02504:       Number(dayContext.dominante) === Number(grupo);
02505: 
02506:     let dayFlowRaw = 0;
02507: 
02508:     if (dayFreq >= 2) dayFlowRaw += 32;
02509:     if (dayFreq >= 3) dayFlowRaw += 18;
02510:     if (dayFirstFreq >= 1) dayFlowRaw += 20;
02511:     if (dayFirstFreq >= 2) dayFlowRaw += 14;
02512:     if (isDominantToday) dayFlowRaw += 24;
02513: 
02514:     if (Number(dayContext?.diversidade || 0) < 0.5) {
02515:       dayFlowRaw += dayFreq > 0 ? 12 : -8;
02516:     } else if (Number(dayContext?.diversidade || 0) > 0.75) {
02517:       dayFlowRaw += dayFreq === 0 ? 10 : 0;
02518:     }
02519: 
02520:     if (dayContext?.repeatNow && dayFreq > 0) {
02521:       dayFlowRaw += 10;
02522:     }
02523: 
02524:     const dayFlowScore = dayFlowRaw * dayFlowConfidence;
02525: 
02526:     const pairSequenceScore =
02527:       Number(pairOut?.samples || 0) > 0
02528:         ? ((pPair * 260) + Math.min(70, Number(pairOut.samples || 0) * 8)) * pairConfidence
02529:         : 0;
02530: 
02531:     const structuralBlend =
02532:       (pSF * 0.45) +
02533:       (pST * 0.35) +
02534:       (pD * 0.2);
02535: 
02536:     const memoryBlend =
02537:       (pM * 0.75) +
02538:       (pSeq * 0.25);
02539: 
02540:     const recentBlend =
02541:       (recentComposite * 0.85) +
02542:       (repeatBoost * 0.15);
02543: 
02544:     const prob =
02545:       (pT * adjustedWeights.transition) +
02546:       (pPair * adjustedWeights.pair * pairConfidence) +
02547:       (memoryBlend * adjustedWeights.memory) +
02548:       (recentBlend * adjustedWeights.recent) +
02549:       (structuralBlend * adjustedWeights.structural) +
02550:       (lateNorm * adjustedWeights.late);
02551: 
02552:     const score =
02553:       (prob * 1000) +
02554:       (dominanceScore * 180) +
02555:       (pairSequenceScore * 0.6) +
02556:       (dayFlowScore * 0.8) +
02557:       (confidence * 50);
02558: 
02559:     return {
02560:       grupo,
02561:       prob,
02562:       scoreProb: prob,
02563:       score,
02564:       probTransition: pT,
02565:       probStructuralFirst: pSF,
02566:       probStructuralTop5: pST,
02567:       probMemory: pM,
02568:       probDuplication: pD,
02569:       probSeq2: pSeq,
02570:       probPair: pPair,
02571:       pairProb: pPair,
02572:       pairConfidence,
02573:       repeatBoost,
02574:       dominanceScore,
02575:       recentComposite,
02576:       recentTop5: rm.recentTop5,
02577:       recentFirst: rm.recentFirst,
02578:       recentDupDraws: rm.recentDupDraws,
02579:       recentLast1Top5: rm.recentLast1Top5,
02580:       recentLast2Top5: rm.recentLast2Top5,
02581:       recentLast3Top5: rm.recentLast3Top5,
02582:       lateNorm,
02583:       gapMs,
02584:       condFirstCount: Number(condFirstFreq.get(grupo) || 0),
02585:       structuralFirstCount: Number(structuralFirst?.firstFreq?.get(grupo) || 0),
02586:       structuralTop5Count: Number(structuralTop5?.freq?.get(grupo) || 0),
02587:       duplicationCount: Number(duplication?.freq?.get(grupo) || 0),
02588:       memoryWeight: Number(memoryOut?.freq?.get(grupo) || 0),
02589:       dayFreq,
02590:       dayFirstFreq,
02591:       isDominantToday,
02592:       dayFlowConfidence,
02593:       dayFlowScore,
02594:       pairSamples: Number(pairOut?.samples || 0),
02595:       pairWeightedSamples: Number(pairOut?.weightedSamples || 0),
02596:       pairExactDomSamples: Number(pairOut?.exactDomSamples || 0),
02597:       pairCount: Number(pairOut?.freq?.get?.(grupo) || 0),
02598:       pairSequenceScore,
02599:     };
02600:   })
02601:     .sort((a, b) => {
02602:       if (b.score !== a.score) return b.score - a.score;
02603:       if (b.scoreProb !== a.scoreProb) return b.scoreProb - a.scoreProb;
02604:       if (b.dominanceScore !== a.dominanceScore) return b.dominanceScore - a.dominanceScore;
02605:       if (b.probTransition !== a.probTransition) return b.probTransition - a.probTransition;
02606:       if (b.probPair !== a.probPair) return b.probPair - a.probPair;
02607:       if (b.probDuplication !== a.probDuplication) return b.probDuplication - a.probDuplication;
02608:       if (b.probStructuralFirst !== a.probStructuralFirst) return b.probStructuralFirst - a.probStructuralFirst;
02609:       if (b.probStructuralTop5 !== a.probStructuralTop5) return b.probStructuralTop5 - a.probStructuralTop5;
02610:       return a.grupo - b.grupo;
02611:     })
02612:     .slice(0, Math.max(1, Number(topN || 3)));
02613: 
02614:   if (typeof window !== "undefined") {
02615:     console.table(
02616:       ranked.map((x) => ({
02617:         slot: `${targetY} ${targetH}`,
02618:         grupo: x.grupo,
02619:         score: Number(x.score || 0).toFixed(2),
02620:         final: (Number(x.scoreProb || 0) * 100).toFixed(2),
02621:         transicao: (Number(x.probTransition || 0) * 100).toFixed(2),
02622:         estrutural1: (Number(x.probStructuralFirst || 0) * 100).toFixed(2),
02623:         estruturalTop5: (Number(x.probStructuralTop5 || 0) * 100).toFixed(2),
02624:         memoria: (Number(x.probMemory || 0) * 100).toFixed(2),
02625:         par: (Number(x.probPair || 0) * 100).toFixed(2),
02626:         duplicacao: (Number(x.probDuplication || 0) * 100).toFixed(2),
02627:         recencia: (Number(x.recentComposite || 0) * 100).toFixed(2),
02628:         atraso: (Number(x.lateNorm || 0) * 100).toFixed(2),
02629:         dayFreq: x.dayFreq,
02630:         dayFirst: x.dayFirstFreq,
02631:         dominante: x.isDominantToday,
02632:         pairSamples: x.pairSamples,
02633:         condFirstCount: x.condFirstCount,
02634:         structuralFirstCount: x.structuralFirstCount,
02635:         structuralTop5Count: x.structuralTop5Count,
02636:       }))
02637:     );
02638: 
02639:     console.log("[TOP3 V2 CONTEXTO]", {

02679:

02722:         `Probabilidade final BICHO=${(x.scoreProb * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,

02736:         explain: {

02738:           mode,

02742:           weights: adjustedWeights,
02743:           layerKey: chosen?.key || "NONE",
02744:           layerLabel: chosen?.label || "—",

02753:           seq2Prev1: Number(seq2Out?.prev1 || 0),

02766:           dayFlowConfidence,

02797:       explain: {
02798:         engine: "V2_BICHO",
02799:         mode,
02800:         regime,
02801:         repeatRate: Number(regimeInfo?.repeatRate || 0),
02802:         uniqueRate: Number(regimeInfo?.uniqueRate || 0),
02803:         weights: adjustedWeights,
02804:         layerKey: chosen?.key || "NONE",
02805:         layerLabel: chosen?.label || "—",
02806:         layerSamples: condSamples,
02807:         structuralFirstSamples: Number(structuralFirst?.totalSamples || 0),
02808:         structuralTop5Samples: Number(structuralTop5?.totalSamples || 0),
02809:         duplicationSamples: Number(duplication?.totalSamples || 0),
02810:         memoryMatchedSamples: Number(memoryOut?.matchedSamples || 0),

02824:           key: layer.key,

02833: 
02834: 
02835: /* =========================
02836:    Motor principal V3 (ESTATISTICO AUDITAVEL)
02837: ========================= */
02838: 
02839: function incMap(map, key, value = 1) {
02840:   const k = Number(key);
02841:   if (!Number.isFinite(k) || k < 1 || k > TOP3_GROUPS_K) return;
02842:   map.set(k, Number(map.get(k) || 0) + Number(value || 0));
02843: }
02844: 
02845: function emptyGroupMap() {
02846:   const out = new Map();
02847:   for (let g = 1; g <= safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25); g += 1) out.set(g, 0);
02848:   return out;
02849: }
02850: 
02851: function getTop5GroupSet(draw) {
02852:   return new Set(
02853:     getAllTop5Groups(draw)
02854:       .map((x) => Number(x.grupo))
02855:       .filter((g) => Number.isFinite(g) && g >= 1 && g <= TOP3_GROUPS_K)
02856:   );
02857: }
02858: 
02859: function layerProbability(freqMap, samples, alpha = TOP3_SMOOTH_ALPHA) {
02860:   const out = new Map();
02861:   const k = safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25);
02862:   const a = Math.max(0, Number(alpha || 0));
02863:   const base = Math.max(0, Number(samples || 0));
02864:   const denom = Math.max(1, base + (a * k));
02865: 
02866:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
02867:     const count = Math.max(0, Number(freqMap?.get?.(g) || 0));
02868:     out.set(g, (count + a) / denom);
02869:   }
02870: 
02871:   return out;
02872: }
02873: 
02874: function sampleConfidence(samples, fullAt = 30) {
02875:   const n = Number(samples || 0);
02876:   const lim = Math.max(1, Number(fullAt || 30));
02877:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, n / lim));
02878: }
02879: 
02880: export function computeStatisticalTop3V3({
02881:   lotteryKey,
02882:   drawsRange,
02883:   drawLast,
02884:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
02885:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
02886:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
02887:   topN = 3,
02888:   targetYmdOverride = "",
02889:   targetHourOverride = "",
02890: }) {
02891:   const list = sortDrawsAsc(Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? drawsRange : []);
02892:   if (!list.length || !drawLast) return { top: [], meta: null };
02893: 
02894:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
02895: 
02896:   const lastY = pickDrawYMD(drawLast);
02897:   const lastH = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(drawLast));
02898:   const prevGrupo = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(drawLast);
02899: 
02900:   if (
02901:     !isYMD(lastY) ||
02902:     !lastH ||
02903:     !Number.isFinite(Number(prevGrupo)) ||
02904:     Number(prevGrupo) < 1 ||
02905:     Number(prevGrupo) > TOP3_GROUPS_K
02906:   ) {
02907:     return { top: [], meta: null };
02908:   }
02909: 
02910:   const nextSlot = getNextSlotForLottery({
02911:     lotteryKey: key,
02912:     ymd: lastY,
02913:     hourBucket: lastH,
02914:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
02915:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
02916:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
02917:   });
02918: 
02919:   const forcedTargetY = safeStr(targetYmdOverride);
02920:   const forcedTargetH = toHourBucket(targetHourOverride);
02921: 
02922:   const targetY = isYMD(forcedTargetY) && forcedTargetH
02923:     ? forcedTargetY
02924:     : safeStr(nextSlot?.ymd);
02925: 
02926:   const targetH = isYMD(forcedTargetY) && forcedTargetH
02927:     ? forcedTargetH
02928:     : toHourBucket(nextSlot?.hour);
02929:   const targetDow = getDowKey(targetY);
02930:   const targetDayOfMonth = getDayOfMonth(targetY);
02931:   const targetTs = ymdHourToTs(targetY, targetH);
02932: 
02933:   if (!isYMD(targetY) || !targetH || !Number.isFinite(Number(targetDow))) {
02934:     return { top: [], meta: null };
02935:   }
02936: 
02937:   const history = list.filter((d) => {
02938:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
02939:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
02940:     const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
02941:     return isYMD(y) && h && Number.isFinite(ts) && ts < targetTs;
02942:   });
02943: 
02944:   const layers = {
02945:     hour: { label: `frequência no horário ${targetH}`, samples: 0, first: emptyGroupMap(), top5: emptyGroupMap(), weight: 0.33 },
02946:     dowHour: { label: `frequência no dia da semana + horário`, samples: 0, first: emptyGroupMap(), top5: emptyGroupMap(), weight: 0.27 },
02947:     dayMonth: { label: `frequência no dia ${String(targetDayOfMonth).padStart(2, "0")}`, samples: 0, first: emptyGroupMap(), top5: emptyGroupMap(), weight: 0.04 },
02948:     transition: { label: `transição G${String(prevGrupo).padStart(2, "0")} @ ${lastH} → ${targetH}`, samples: 0, first: emptyGroupMap(), top5: emptyGroupMap(), weight: 0.26 },
02949:     recent: { label: `recência comparável`, samples: 0, first: emptyGroupMap(), top5: emptyGroupMap(), weight: 0.10 },
02950:   };
02951: 
02952:   for (const d of history) {
02953:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
02954:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
02955:     const dow = getDowKey(y);
02956:     const dom = getDayOfMonth(y);
02957:     const g1 = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(d);
02958:     const top5Set = getTop5GroupSet(d);
02959: 
02960:     const addToLayer = (layer) => {
02961:       layer.samples += 1;
02962:       incMap(layer.first, g1, 1);
02963:       for (const g of top5Set) incMap(layer.top5, g, 1);
02964:     };
02965: 
02966:     if (h === targetH) addToLayer(layers.hour);
02967:     if (h === targetH && Number(dow) === Number(targetDow)) addToLayer(layers.dowHour);
02968:     if (Number(dom) === Number(targetDayOfMonth)) addToLayer(layers.dayMonth);
02969:   }
02970: 
02971:   const indexed = indexDrawsByYmdHour(history);
02972: 
02973:   for (const prev of history) {
02974:     const py = pickDrawYMD(prev);
02975:     const ph = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(prev));
02976:     const pg = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(prev);
02977: 
02978:     if (!isYMD(py) || !ph || Number(pg) !== Number(prevGrupo)) continue;
02979: 
02980:     const ns = getNextSlotForLottery({
02981:       lotteryKey: key,
02982:       ymd: py,
02983:       hourBucket: ph,
02984:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
02985:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
02986:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
02987:     });
02988: 
02989:     if (toHourBucket(ns?.hour) !== targetH) continue;
02990:     if (ph !== lastH) continue;
02991: 
02992:     const nextDraw = indexed.get(`${safeStr(ns?.ymd)}|${toHourBucket(ns?.hour)}`) || null;
02993:     if (!nextDraw) continue;
02994: 
02995:     const g1 = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(nextDraw);
02996:     const top5Set = getTop5GroupSet(nextDraw);
02997: 
02998:     layers.transition.samples += 1;
02999:     incMap(layers.transition.first, g1, 1);
03000:     for (const g of top5Set) incMap(layers.transition.top5, g, 1);
03001:   }
03002: 
03003:   const recentComparable = history
03004:     .filter((d) => {
03005:       const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
03006:       const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
03007:       return h === targetH && Number(getDowKey(y)) === Number(targetDow);
03008:     })
03009:     .slice(-20);
03010: 
03011:   for (const d of recentComparable) {
03012:     const g1 = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(d);
03013:     const top5Set = getTop5GroupSet(d);
03014: 
03015:     layers.recent.samples += 1;
03016:     incMap(layers.recent.first, g1, 1);
03017:     for (const g of top5Set) incMap(layers.recent.top5, g, 1);
03018:   }
03019: 
03020:   const currentScene = buildSceneFromDraw(drawLast);
03021:   const sceneRanking = buildHistoricalSceneRanking(history, currentScene, 80);
03022:   const sceneHypothesis = buildSceneHypothesisDistribution(sceneRanking, TOP3_GROUPS_K);
03023:   const sceneWeight =
03024:     sampleConfidence(sceneHypothesis?.samples || 0, TOP3_SCENE_SAMPLE_TARGET) *
03025:     TOP3_SCENE_WEIGHT;
03026: 
03027: 
03028:   const activeWeights = {};
03029:   let totalWeight = 0;
03030: 
03031:   for (const [keyLayer, layer] of Object.entries(layers)) {
03032:     const conf =
03033:       keyLayer === "transition"
03034:         ? sampleConfidence(layer.samples, 8)
03035:         : keyLayer === "recent"
03036:           ? sampleConfidence(layer.samples, 12)
03037:           : sampleConfidence(layer.samples, 30);
03038: 
03039:     const w = layer.samples > 0 ? layer.weight * conf : 0;
03040:     activeWeights[keyLayer] = w;
03041:     totalWeight += w;
03042:   }
03043: 
03044:   if (totalWeight <= 0) {
03045:     activeWeights.hour = 1;
03046:     totalWeight = 1;
03047:   }
03048: 
03049:   for (const keyLayer of Object.keys(activeWeights)) {
03050:     activeWeights[keyLayer] = activeWeights[keyLayer] / totalWeight;
03051:   }
03052: 
03053:   const ranked = Array.from({ length: safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25) }, (_, idx) => {
03054:     const grupo = idx + 1;
03055: 
03056:     let scoreProb = 0;
03057:     const details = {};
03058: 
03059:     for (const [keyLayer, layer] of Object.entries(layers)) {
03060:       const pFirst = Number(layerProbability(layer.first, layer.samples).get(grupo) || 0);
03061:       const pTop5 = Number(layerProbability(layer.top5, layer.samples).get(grupo) || 0);
03062: 
03063:       const pLayer = (pFirst * 0.75) + (pTop5 * 0.25);
03064:       const w = Number(activeWeights[keyLayer] || 0);
03065: 
03066:       scoreProb += pLayer * w;
03067: 
03068:       details[keyLayer] = {
03069:         label: layer.label,
03070:         samples: layer.samples,
03071:         firstCount: Number(layer.first.get(grupo) || 0),
03072:         top3Count: Number(layer.top5.get(grupo) || 0),
03073:         probability: pLayer,
03074:         weight: w,
03075:       };
03076:     }
03077: 
03078:     const pSceneRaw = Number(sceneHypothesis?.prob?.get?.(grupo) || 0);
03079:     const pScene =
03080:       (pSceneRaw * TOP3_SCENE_BLEND_SCENE) +
03081:       ((1 / TOP3_GROUPS_K) * TOP3_SCENE_BLEND_UNIFORM);
03082: 
03083:     if (sceneWeight > 0) {
03084:       scoreProb = (scoreProb * (1 - sceneWeight)) + (pScene * sceneWeight);
03085:     }
03086: 
03087:     details.scene = {
03088:       label: "analogia histórica de cena",
03089:       samples: Number(sceneHypothesis?.samples || 0),
03090:       firstCount: Number(sceneHypothesis?.freq?.get?.(grupo) || 0),
03091:       top3Count: 0,
03092:       probability: pScene,
03093:       weight: sceneWeight,
03094:     };
03095: 
03096:     return {
03097:       grupo,
03098:       scoreProb,
03099:       score: scoreProb * 1000,
03100:       details,
03101:     };
03102:   })
03103:     .sort((a, b) => {
03104:       if (b.score !== a.score) return b.score - a.score;
03105:       return a.grupo - b.grupo;
03106:     })
03107:     .slice(0, Math.max(1, Number(topN || 3)));
03108: 
03109:   const rankedScored = scoreRanking(
03110:     ranked,
03111:     {
03112:       lotteryKey,
03113:       targetYmd: targetY,
03114:       targetHour: targetH,
03115:       previousGroup: prevGrupo,
03116:       totalDraws: history.length,
03117:     }
03118:   );
03119: 
03120:   const top = rankedScored.map((x, idx) => {
03121:     const g2 = String(x.grupo).padStart(2, "0");
03122:     const rawScoreProb = Number(x.scoreProb || 0);
03123: 
03124:     const strongest = Object.values(x.details)
03125:       .filter((d) => Number(d.probability || 0) > 0)
03126:       .sort((a, b) => (b.probability * b.weight) - (a.probability * a.weight))
03127:       .slice(0, 3);
03128: 
03129:     return {
03130:       rank: idx + 1,
03131:       title:
03132:         idx === 0
03133:           ? "Mais provável"
03134:           : idx === 1
03135:             ? "2º mais provável"
03136:             : "3º mais provável",
03137:       grupo: x.grupo,
03138:       scoreProb: Number(rawScoreProb || 0),
03139:       rawScoreProb: Number(rawScoreProb || 0),
03140:       probCond: Number(x.details.transition?.probability || 0),
03141:       probBase: Number(x.details.hour?.probability || 0),
03142:       lateBonus: 0,
03143:       freq: Number(x.details.hour?.firstCount || 0),
03144:       freqCond: Number(x.details.transition?.firstCount || 0),
03145:       freqBase: Number(x.details.hour?.firstCount || 0),
03146:       freqZeroWhy: "",
03147:       reasons: [
03148:         `Motor: V3_STATISTICAL`,
03149:         `Alvo: ${targetY} ${targetH} | DOW=${targetDow} | dia=${String(targetDayOfMonth).padStart(2, "0")}`,
03150:         `Base: G${String(prevGrupo).padStart(2, "0")} @ ${lastH}`,
03151:         `Probabilidade final G${g2}: ${(Number(rawScoreProb || 0) * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
03152:         ...strongest.map((d) =>
03153:           `${d.label}: ${d.firstCount}x em 1º | ${d.top3Count}x no TOP3 | amostras=${d.samples} | peso=${(d.weight * 100).toFixed(0)}%`
03154:         ),
03155:       ],
03156:       meta: {
03157:         trigger: {
03158:           ymd: lastY,
03159:           hour: lastH,
03160:           grupo: Number(prevGrupo),
03161:         },
03162:         next: {
03163:           ymd: safeStr(targetY),
03164:           hour: safeStr(targetH),
03165:         },
03166:         samples: history.length,
03167:         period: {
03168:           from: pickDrawYMD(history[0]) || "",
03169:           to: pickDrawYMD(history[history.length - 1]) || "",
03170:         },
03171:         scenario: "V3_STATISTICAL",
03172:         explain: {
03173:           engine: "V3_STATISTICAL",
03174:           targetDow,
03175:           targetDayOfMonth,
03176:           prevHour: lastH,
03177:           prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
03178:           activeWeights,
03179:           scene: {
03180:             samples: Number(sceneHypothesis?.samples || 0),
03181:             totalWeight: Number(sceneHypothesis?.totalWeight || 0),
03182:             weight: Number(sceneWeight || 0),
03183:             currentSignature: currentScene?.signature || "",
03184:           },
03185:           layers: Object.fromEntries(
03186:             Object.entries(layers).map(([k, v]) => [
03187:               k,
03188:               {
03189:                 label: v.label,
03190:                 samples: v.samples,
03191:                 weight: activeWeights[k],
03192:               },
03193:             ])
03194:           ),
03195:           details: x.details,
03196:         },
03197:       },
03198:     };
03199:   });
03200: 
03201:   return {
03202:     top,
03203:     meta: {
03204:       trigger: {
03205:         ymd: lastY,
03206:         hour: lastH,
03207:         grupo: Number(prevGrupo),
03208:       },
03209:       next: {
03210:         ymd: safeStr(targetY),
03211:         hour: safeStr(targetH),
03212:       },
03213:       samples: history.length,
03214:       scenario: "V3_STATISTICAL",
03215:       explain: {
03216:         engine: "V3_STATISTICAL",
03217:         targetDow,
03218:         targetDayOfMonth,
03219:         prevHour: lastH,
03220:         prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
03221:         activeWeights,
03222:         layers: Object.fromEntries(
03223:           Object.entries(layers).map(([k, v]) => [
03224:             k,
03225:             {
03226:               label: v.label,
03227:               samples: v.samples,
03228:               weight: activeWeights[k],
03229:             },
03230:           ])
03231:         ),
03232:       },
03233:     },
03234:   };
03235: }
03236: 
03237: 
03238: 
03239: /* =========================
03240:    TOP3 SCENE READER
03241: ========================= */
03242: 
03243: function scenePickMilhar4(prize) {
03244:   const candidates = [
03245:     prize?.milhar,
03246:     prize?.milhar4,
03247:     prize?.numero,
03248:     prize?.number,
03249:     prize?.value,
03250:     prize?.result,
03251:     prize?.resultado,
03252:     prize?.premio,
03253:   ];
03254: 
03255:   for (const v of candidates) {
03256:     const dig = String(v ?? "").replace(/\D+/g, "");
03257:     if (dig) return dig.length >= 4 ? dig.slice(-4) : dig.padStart(4, "0");
03258:   }
03259: 
03260:   return "";
03261: }
03262: 
03263: function sceneGrupoFromMilhar4(m4) {
03264:   const s = String(m4 || "").replace(/\D+/g, "").padStart(4, "0").slice(-4);
03265:   if (!/^\d{4}$/.test(s)) return null;
03266: 
03267:   const dzRaw = Number(s.slice(-2));
03268:   if (!Number.isFinite(dzRaw)) return null;
03269: 
03270:   const dz = dzRaw === 0 ? 100 : dzRaw;
03271:   const grupo = Math.ceil(dz / 4);
03272: 
03273:   return grupo >= 1 && grupo <= 25 ? grupo : null;
03274: }
03275: 
03276: function buildSceneFromDraw(draw) {
03277:   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
03278: 
03279:   const rows = prizes
03280:     .map((p) => {
03281:       const pos = Number(guessPrizePos(p));
03282:       const milhar = scenePickMilhar4(p);
03283:       const grupo = sceneGrupoFromMilhar4(milhar);
03284: 
03285:       return {
03286:         pos,
03287:         milhar: String(milhar || "").replace(/\D+/g, "").padStart(4, "0").slice(-4),
03288:         centena: getCentena3(milhar),
03289:         dezena: getDezena2(milhar),
03290:         grupo,
03291:       };
03292:     })
03293:     .filter((x) => Number.isFinite(x.pos) && x.pos >= 1 && x.pos <= 7)
03294:     .sort((a, b) => a.pos - b.pos);
03295: 
03296:   const grupos = rows.map((x) => x.grupo).filter((x) => Number.isFinite(x));
03297:   const dezenas = rows.map((x) => x.dezena).filter(Boolean);
03298:   const centenas = rows.map((x) => x.centena).filter(Boolean);
03299:   const milhares = rows.map((x) => x.milhar).filter(Boolean);
03300: 
03301:   const countMap = (arr) => {
03302:     const m = new Map();
03303:     for (const x of arr) m.set(String(x), (m.get(String(x)) || 0) + 1);
03304:     return Array.from(m.entries())
03305:       .filter(([, n]) => n > 1)
03306:       .map(([value, count]) => ({ value, count }));
03307:   };
03308: 
03309:   return {
03310:     ymd: pickDrawYMD(draw),
03311:     hour: toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw)),
03312:     firstGrupo: grupos[0] ?? null,
03313:     grupos,
03314:     dezenas,
03315:     centenas,
03316:     milhares,
03317:     repeatedGroups: countMap(grupos),
03318:     repeatedDezenas: countMap(dezenas),
03319:     repeatedCentenas: countMap(centenas),
03320:     rows,
03321:     signature: grupos.map((g) => String(g).padStart(2, "0")).join("-"),
03322:   };
03323: }
03324: 
03325: 
03326: 
03327: function compareScenes(a, b, explain = false) {
03328:   if (!a || !b) return explain ? { score: 0, details: {} } : 0;
03329: 
03330:   let score = 0;
03331:   const details = {};
03332: 
03333:   const add = (key, value) => {
03334:     if (!value) return;
03335:     score += value;
03336:     details[key] = value;
03337:   };
03338: 
03339:   const ag = Array.isArray(a.grupos) ? a.grupos : [];
03340:   const bg = Array.isArray(b.grupos) ? b.grupos : [];
03341: 
03342:   const ad = Array.isArray(a.dezenas) ? a.dezenas : [];
03343:   const bd = Array.isArray(b.dezenas) ? b.dezenas : [];
03344: 
03345:   const ac = Array.isArray(a.centenas) ? a.centenas : [];
03346:   const bc = Array.isArray(b.centenas) ? b.centenas : [];
03347: 
03348:   if (Number(a.firstGrupo) === Number(b.firstGrupo))
03349:     add("firstGrupo",45);
03350: 
03351:   for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(ag.length,bg.length,7); i++) {
03352:     if (Number(ag[i]) === Number(bg[i]))
03353:       add("order_"+i, i===0 ? 20 : 10);
03354:   }
03355: 
03356:   add(
03357:     "groupOverlap",
03358:     new Set(ag.filter(g => bg.includes(g))).size * 6
03359:   );
03360: 
03361:   add(
03362:     "dezenaOverlap",
03363:     new Set(ad.filter(d => bd.includes(d))).size * 3
03364:   );
03365: 
03366:   add(
03367:     "centenaOverlap",
03368:     new Set(ac.filter(c => bc.includes(c))).size * 1.5
03369:   );
03370: 
03371:   const repA = Array.isArray(a.repeatedGroups) ? a.repeatedGroups.length : 0;
03372:   const repB = Array.isArray(b.repeatedGroups) ? b.repeatedGroups.length : 0;
03373: 
03374:   if (repA > 0 && repB > 0)
03375:     add("repeatExists",8);
03376: 
03377:   if (repA === repB && repA > 0)
03378:     add("repeatEqual",6);
03379: 
03380:   if (String(a.signature||"") === String(b.signature||""))
03381:     add("signature",60);
03382: 
03383:   if (a.hour === b.hour)
03384:     add("hour",8);
03385: 
03386:   return explain ? { score, details } : score;
03387: }
03388: 
03389: 
03390: 
03391: 
03392: function buildHistoricalSceneRanking(draws, currentScene, limit = 100) {
03393:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
03394:   if (!currentScene) return [];
03395: 
03396:   const ordered = list
03397:     .map((draw) => ({
03398:       draw,
03399:       scene: buildSceneFromDraw(draw),
03400:     }))
03401:     .filter((x) => x.scene)
03402:     .sort((a, b) => {
03403:       const ta = ymdHourToTs(a.scene?.ymd, a.scene?.hour);
03404:       const tb = ymdHourToTs(b.scene?.ymd, b.scene?.hour);
03405:       return Number(ta || 0) - Number(tb || 0);
03406:     });
03407: 
03408:   return ordered
03409:     .map((item, idx) => {
03410:       const next = ordered[idx + 1] || null;
03411:       const score = compareScenes(currentScene, item.scene);
03412: 
03413:       return {
03414:         draw: item.draw,
03415:         scene: item.scene,
03416:         nextDraw: next?.draw || null,
03417:         nextScene: next?.scene || null,
03418:         score,
03419:       };
03420:     })
03421:     .filter((x) => x.scene && x.nextDraw && Number(x.score) > 0)
03422:     .sort((a, b) => {
03423:       if (Number(b.score) !== Number(a.score)) {
03424:         return Number(b.score) - Number(a.score);
03425:       }
03426: 
03427:       const ta = ymdHourToTs(a.scene?.ymd, a.scene?.hour);
03428:       const tb = ymdHourToTs(b.scene?.ymd, b.scene?.hour);
03429:       return Number(tb || 0) - Number(ta || 0);
03430:     })
03431:     .slice(0, Math.max(1, Number(limit || 100)));
03432: }
03433: 
03434: 
03435: 
03436: 
03437: function buildSceneHypothesisDistribution(sceneRanking, groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
03438:   const ranking = Array.isArray(sceneRanking) ? sceneRanking : [];
03439:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
03440: 
03441:   const freq = new Map();
03442:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) freq.set(g, 0);
03443: 
03444:   let totalWeight = 0;
03445: 
03446:   for (const item of ranking) {
03447:     const score = Math.max(0, Number(item?.score || 0));
03448:     const nextGrupo = Number(item?.nextDraw ? pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(item.nextDraw) : null);
03449: 
03450:     if (!Number.isFinite(nextGrupo) || nextGrupo < 1 || nextGrupo > k) continue;
03451:     if (score <= 0) continue;
03452: 
03453:     freq.set(nextGrupo, Number(freq.get(nextGrupo) || 0) + score);
03454:     totalWeight += score;
03455:   }
03456: 
03457:   const prob = new Map();
03458:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
03459:     prob.set(g, totalWeight > 0 ? Number(freq.get(g) || 0) / totalWeight : 0);
03460:   }
03461: 
03462:   return {
03463:     freq,
03464:     prob,
03465:     samples: ranking.length,
03466:     totalWeight,
03467:   };
03468: }
03469: 
03470: 
03471: function logTop3SceneDebug(label, scene, extra = {}) {
03472:   if (typeof window === "undefined") return;
03473:   if (!scene) return;
03474: 
03475:   console.log(`[TOP3 SCENE] ${label}`, {
03476:     ...extra,
03477:     ymd: scene.ymd,
03478:     hour: scene.hour,
03479:     firstGrupo: scene.firstGrupo,
03480:     signature: scene.signature,
03481:     grupos: scene.grupos,
03482:     dezenas: scene.dezenas,
03483:     centenas: scene.centenas,
03484:     milhares: scene.milhares,
03485:     repeatedGroups: scene.repeatedGroups,
03486:     repeatedDezenas: scene.repeatedDezenas,
03487:     repeatedCentenas: scene.repeatedCentenas,
03488:     rows: scene.rows,
03489:   });
03490: }
03491: 
03492: 
03493: /* =========================
03494:    16/20 milhares (por grupo) — POR TERMINAÇÃO (CORRETO)
03495: ========================= */
03496: 
03497: function getDezenasFixasFromGrupo(grupo2) {
03498:   const g = Number(grupo2);
03499:   if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > 25) return [];
03500: 
03501:   const start = (g - 1) * 4 + 1;
03502:   const out = [];
03503: 
03504:   for (let i = 0; i < 4; i += 1) {
03505:     out.push(wrapToDezena2(start + i));
03506:   }
03507: 
03508:   return out.filter(Boolean);
03509: }
03510: 
03511: 
03512: export function buildMilharesForGrupo({
03513:   rangeDraws,
03514:   analysisHourBucket,
03515:   schedule,
03516:   grupo2,
03517:   count = 20,
03518:   targetYmd = "",
03519: }) {
03520:   const list = Array.isArray(rangeDraws) ? rangeDraws : [];
03521:   const target = toHourBucket(analysisHourBucket);
03522:   const schSet = scheduleSet(schedule);
03523:   const targetDay = safeStr(targetYmd);
03524:   const targetDow = isYMD(targetDay) ? getDowKey(targetDay) : null;
03525: 
03526:   const N = Number.isFinite(Number(count))
03527:     ? Math.max(4, Math.trunc(Number(count)))
03528:     : 20;
03529: 
03530:   const grupoNum = Number(grupo2);
03531: 
03532:   if (!Number.isFinite(grupoNum) || grupoNum < 1 || grupoNum > 25 || !list.length) {
03533:     return { dezenas: [], slots: [] };
03534:   }
03535: 
03536:   const dezenasFixas = getDezenasFixasFromGrupo(grupoNum);
03537:   if (!dezenasFixas.length) return { dezenas: [], slots: [] };
03538: 
03539:   const perDezena = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(N / dezenasFixas.length));
03540:   const byMilhar = new Map();
03541: 
03542:   for (const d of list) {
03543:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
03544:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
03545:     if (!h) continue;
03546: 
03547:     const isTargetHour = target && h === target;
03548:     const isScheduleHour = schSet.size && schSet.has(h);
03549:     const isSameDow = targetDow !== null && Number(getDowKey(y)) === Number(targetDow);
03550:     const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
03551: 
03552:     const ps = Array.isArray(d?.prizes) ? d.prizes : [];
03553: 
03554:     for (const prize of ps) {
03555:       const pos = Number(guessPrizePos(prize));
03556:       if (!Number.isFinite(pos) || pos < 1 || pos > 7) continue;
03557: 
03558:       const g = Number(guessPrizeGrupo(prize));
03559:       if (g !== grupoNum) continue;
03560: 
03561:       const m4 = String(pickPrizeMilhar4(prize) || "").replace(/\D+/g, "").padStart(4, "0").slice(-4);
03562:       if (!/^\d{4}$/.test(m4)) continue;
03563: 
03564:       const dz = getDezena2(m4);
03565:       const c3 = getCentena3(m4);
03566: 
03567:       if (!dz || !c3 || !dezenasFixas.includes(dz)) continue;
03568: 
03569:       const current = byMilhar.get(m4) || {
03570:         milhar: m4,
03571:         dezena: dz,
03572:         centena: c3,
03573:         freq: 0,
03574:         score: 0,
03575:         targetHits: 0,
03576:         scheduleHits: 0,
03577:         sameDowHits: 0,
03578:         firstPrizeHits: 0,
03579:         lastTs: 0,
03580:       };
03581: 
03582:       let inc = 1;
03583: 
03584:       if (isTargetHour) {
03585:         inc += 8;
03586:         current.targetHits += 1;
03587:       }
03588: 
03589:       if (isScheduleHour) {
03590:         inc += 3;
03591:         current.scheduleHits += 1;
03592:       }
03593: 
03594:       if (isSameDow) {
03595:         inc += 2;
03596:         current.sameDowHits += 1;
03597:       }
03598: 
03599:       if (pos === 1) {
03600:         inc += 3;
03601:         current.firstPrizeHits += 1;
03602:       } else if (pos <= 3) {
03603:         inc += 1;
03604:       }
03605: 
03606:       current.freq += 1;
03607:       current.score += inc;
03608: 
03609:       if (Number.isFinite(ts)) {
03610:         current.lastTs = Math.max(Number(current.lastTs || 0), ts);
03611:       }
03612: 
03613:       byMilhar.set(m4, current);
03614:     }
03615:   }
03616: 
03617:   const ranked = Array.from(byMilhar.values())
03618:     .map((m) => {
03619:       const freq = Number(m.freq || 0);
03620:       const targetHits = Number(m.targetHits || 0);
03621:       const scheduleHits = Number(m.scheduleHits || 0);
03622:       const sameDowHits = Number(m.sameDowHits || 0);
03623:       const firstPrizeHits = Number(m.firstPrizeHits || 0);
03624:       const recencyBoost = Number(m.lastTs || 0) > 0 ? 1 : 0;
03625: 
03626:       return {
03627:         ...m,
03628:         score:
03629:           freq * 100 +
03630:           targetHits * 70 +
03631:           scheduleHits * 35 +
03632:           sameDowHits * 25 +
03633:           firstPrizeHits * 45 +
03634:           recencyBoost * 5,
03635:       };
03636:     })
03637:     .sort((a, b) => {
03638:       if (Number(b.score) !== Number(a.score)) return Number(b.score) - Number(a.score);
03639:       if (Number(b.targetHits) !== Number(a.targetHits)) return Number(b.targetHits) - Number(a.targetHits);
03640:       if (Number(b.freq) !== Number(a.freq)) return Number(b.freq) - Number(a.freq);
03641:       if (Number(b.lastTs) !== Number(a.lastTs)) return Number(b.lastTs) - Number(a.lastTs);
03642:       return milharCompareAsc(a.milhar, b.milhar);
03643:     });
03644: 
03645:   const centenaStats = new Map();
03646: 
03647:   for (const item of ranked) {
03648:     const centena = String(item?.centena || "").trim();
03649:     if (!centena) continue;
03650: 
03651:     const current = centenaStats.get(centena) || {
03652:       centena,
03653:       freq: 0,
03654:       score: 0,
03655:       targetHits: 0,
03656:       sameDowHits: 0,
03657:       firstPrizeHits: 0,
03658:       lastTs: 0,
03659:     };
03660: 
03661:     current.freq += Number(item.freq || 0);
03662:     current.score += Number(item.score || 0);
03663:     current.targetHits += Number(item.targetHits || 0);
03664:     current.sameDowHits += Number(item.sameDowHits || 0);
03665:     current.firstPrizeHits += Number(item.firstPrizeHits || 0);
03666:     current.lastTs = Math.max(Number(current.lastTs || 0), Number(item.lastTs || 0));
03667: 
03668:     centenaStats.set(centena, current);
03669:   }
03670: 
03671:   const centenaScoreMap = new Map();
03672: 
03673:   for (const c of centenaStats.values()) {
03674:     centenaScoreMap.set(
03675:       c.centena,
03676:       Number(c.score || 0) +
03677:         Number(c.freq || 0) * 25 +
03678:         Number(c.targetHits || 0) * 60 +
03679:         Number(c.sameDowHits || 0) * 30 +
03680:         Number(c.firstPrizeHits || 0) * 40 +
03681:         (Number(c.lastTs || 0) > 0 ? 10 : 0)
03682:     );
03683:   }
03684: 
03685:   const usedMilhares = new Set();
03686:   const slots = [];
03687: 
03688:   function pickDiversifiedMilharesForDezena(dz, limit) {
03689:     const candidates = ranked
03690:       .filter((x) => x.dezena === dz && !usedMilhares.has(x.milhar))
03691:       .map((x) => {
03692:         const milhar = String(x.milhar || "").padStart(4, "0").slice(-4);
03693:         const prefix = milhar.slice(0, 1);
03694:         const centena = milhar.slice(1);
03695:         const centenaScore = Number(centenaScoreMap.get(centena) || 0);
03696: 
03697:         return {
03698:           ...x,
03699:           milhar,
03700:           prefix,
03701:           centena,
03702:           centenaScore,
03703:           adjustedScore:
03704:             centenaScore * 2 +
03705:             Number(x.score || 0) -
03706:             (prefix === "0" ? 80 : 0),
03707:         };
03708:       });
03709: 
03710:     const byCentena = new Map();
03711: 
03712:     for (const item of candidates) {
03713:       if (!byCentena.has(item.centena)) byCentena.set(item.centena, []);
03714:       byCentena.get(item.centena).push(item);
03715:     }
03716: 
03717:     const centenaGroups = Array.from(byCentena.entries())
03718:       .map(([centena, items]) => {
03719:         const sortedItems = items.slice().sort((a, b) => {
03720:           if (Number(b.adjustedScore) !== Number(a.adjustedScore)) {
03721:             return Number(b.adjustedScore) - Number(a.adjustedScore);
03722:           }
03723:           if (Number(b.score) !== Number(a.score)) return Number(b.score) - Number(a.score);
03724:           if (Number(b.targetHits) !== Number(a.targetHits)) return Number(b.targetHits) - Number(a.targetHits);
03725:           if (Number(b.freq) !== Number(a.freq)) return Number(b.freq) - Number(a.freq);
03726:           return milharCompareAsc(a.milhar, b.milhar);
03727:         });
03728: 
03729:         const top = sortedItems[0] || {};
03730: 
03731:         return {
03732:           centena,
03733:           centenaScore: Number(top.centenaScore || 0),
03734:           bestScore: Number(top.adjustedScore || 0),
03735:           items: sortedItems,
03736:         };
03737:       })
03738:       .sort((a, b) => {
03739:         if (Number(b.centenaScore) !== Number(a.centenaScore)) {
03740:           return Number(b.centenaScore) - Number(a.centenaScore);
03741:         }
03742:         if (Number(b.bestScore) !== Number(a.bestScore)) {
03743:           return Number(b.bestScore) - Number(a.bestScore);
03744:         }
03745:         return String(a.centena).localeCompare(String(b.centena));
03746:       });
03747: 
03748:     const picked = [];
03749:     const usedPrefixes = new Set();
03750:     const usedCentenas = new Set();
03751: 
03752:     // Fase 1: uma melhor milhar por centena forte, evitando repetir prefixo.
03753:     for (const group of centenaGroups) {
03754:       if (picked.length >= limit) break;
03755: 
03756:       const item = group.items.find((x) => !usedPrefixes.has(x.prefix));
03757:       if (!item) continue;
03758: 
03759:       picked.push(item);
03760:       usedPrefixes.add(item.prefix);
03761:       usedCentenas.add(item.centena);
03762:     }
03763: 
03764:     // Fase 2: se faltar, pega novas centenas fortes mesmo repetindo prefixo.
03765:     for (const group of centenaGroups) {
03766:       if (picked.length >= limit) break;
03767:       if (usedCentenas.has(group.centena)) continue;
03768: 
03769:       const item = group.items.find((x) => !picked.some((p) => p.milhar === x.milhar));
03770:       if (!item) continue;
03771: 
03772:       picked.push(item);
03773:       usedPrefixes.add(item.prefix);
03774:       usedCentenas.add(item.centena);
03775:     }
03776: 
03777:     // Fase 3: se ainda faltar, permite mais de uma milhar da mesma centena.
03778:     for (const group of centenaGroups) {
03779:       if (picked.length >= limit) break;
03780: 
03781:       for (const item of group.items) {
03782:         if (picked.length >= limit) break;
03783:         if (picked.some((p) => p.milhar === item.milhar)) continue;
03784: 
03785:         picked.push(item);
03786:       }
03787:     }
03788: 
03789:     return picked.slice(0, limit);
03790:   }
03791: 
03792:   for (const dz of dezenasFixas) {
03793:     const items = pickDiversifiedMilharesForDezena(dz, perDezena);
03794: 
03795:     let pushed = 0;
03796: 
03797:     for (const item of items) {
03798:       usedMilhares.add(item.milhar);
03799:       slots.push({
03800:         dezena: dz,
03801:         milhar: item.milhar,
03802:         score: item.score,
03803:         adjustedScore: item.adjustedScore,
03804:         centena: item.centena,
03805:         centenaScore: item.centenaScore,
03806:         freq: item.freq,
03807:         targetHits: item.targetHits,
03808:         scheduleHits: item.scheduleHits,
03809:         sameDowHits: item.sameDowHits,
03810:         firstPrizeHits: item.firstPrizeHits,
03811:       });
03812:       pushed += 1;
03813:     }
03814: 
03815:     while (pushed < perDezena) {
03816:       slots.push({ dezena: dz, milhar: "" });
03817:       pushed += 1;
03818:     }
03819:   }
03820: 
03821:   while (slots.length < N) slots.push({ dezena: "", milhar: "" });
03822: 
03823:   return { dezenas: dezenasFixas, slots: slots.slice(0, N) };
03824: }
03825: 
03826: export function build16MilharesForGrupo(args) {
03827:   return buildMilharesForGrupo({ ...(args || {}), count: 16 });
03828: }
03829: 
03830: export function build20MilharesForGrupo(args) {
03831:   return buildMilharesForGrupo({ ...(args || {}), count: 20 });
03832: }
03833: 
03834: /* =========================
03835:    TIMELINE DO DIA (CADEIA PREDITIVA)
03836: ========================= */
03837: 
03838: 
03839: 
03840: export function auditTop3Timeline({
03841:   timeline,
03842:   lotteryKey = "",
03843: }) {
03844:   const rows = Array.isArray(timeline) ? timeline : [];
03845: 
03846:   const validated = rows.filter((slot) => {
03847:     const status = String(slot?.status || "").toLowerCase();
03848:     return status === "validated" && Number.isFinite(Number(slot?.resultGrupo));
03849:   });
03850: 
03851:   const total = validated.length;
03852: 
03853:   const top1Hits = validated.filter((slot) => {
03854:     const result = Number(slot.resultGrupo);
03855:     const first = Number(slot?.top3?.[0]?.grupo);
03856:     return Number.isFinite(first) && first === result;
03857:   }).length;
03858: 
03859:   const top3Hits = validated.filter((slot) => {
03860:     const result = Number(slot.resultGrupo);
03861:     return (Array.isArray(slot?.top3) ? slot.top3 : [])
03862:       .slice(0, 3)
03863:       .some((item) => Number(item?.grupo) === result);
03864:   }).length;
03865: 
03866:   function pct(n, d) {
03867:     if (!d) return 0;
03868:     return Number(((Number(n || 0) / Number(d || 1)) * 100).toFixed(2));
03869:   }
03870: 
03871:   function groupBy(keyFn) {
03872:     const map = new Map();
03873: 
03874:     for (const slot of validated) {
03875:       const key = keyFn(slot);
03876:       const current = map.get(key) || {
03877:         key,
03878:         total: 0,
03879:         top1Hits: 0,
03880:         top3Hits: 0,
03881:       };
03882: 
03883:       const result = Number(slot.resultGrupo);
03884:       const first = Number(slot?.top3?.[0]?.grupo);
03885:       const picks = Array.isArray(slot?.top3) ? slot.top3.slice(0, 3) : [];
03886: 
03887:       current.total += 1;
03888:       if (Number.isFinite(first) && first === result) current.top1Hits += 1;
03889:       if (picks.some((item) => Number(item?.grupo) === result)) current.top3Hits += 1;
03890: 
03891:       map.set(key, current);
03892:     }
03893: 
03894:     return Array.from(map.values())
03895:       .map((x) => ({
03896:         ...x,
03897:         top1Rate: pct(x.top1Hits, x.total),
03898:         top3Rate: pct(x.top3Hits, x.total),
03899:       }))
03900:       .sort((a, b) => {
03901:         if (b.top3Rate !== a.top3Rate) return b.top3Rate - a.top3Rate;
03902:         return b.total - a.total;
03903:       });
03904:   }
03905: 
03906:   return {
03907:     lotteryKey,
03908:     total,
03909:     top1Hits,
03910:     top3Hits,
03911:     top1Rate: pct(top1Hits, total),
03912:     top3Rate: pct(top3Hits, total),
03913:     byHour: groupBy((slot) => String(slot?.targetHour || "")),
03914:     byDate: groupBy((slot) => String(slot?.targetYmd || "")),
03915:     rows: validated.map((slot) => ({
03916:       ymd: slot.targetYmd,
03917:       hour: slot.targetHour,
03918:       baseYmd: slot.baseYmd,
03919:       baseHour: slot.baseHour,
03920:       resultGrupo: slot.resultGrupo,
03921:       top3: (Array.isArray(slot.top3) ? slot.top3 : [])
03922:         .slice(0, 3)
03923:         .map((x) => Number(x?.grupo)),
03924:       top1Hit:
03925:         Number(slot?.top3?.[0]?.grupo) === Number(slot.resultGrupo),
03926:       top3Hit: (Array.isArray(slot.top3) ? slot.top3 : [])
03927:         .slice(0, 3)
03928:         .some((x) => Number(x?.grupo) === Number(slot.resultGrupo)),
03929:     })),
03930:   };
03931: }
03932: 
03933: 
03934: function buildTimelineForDate({
03935:   ymd,
03936:   drawsToday,
03937:   drawsRange,
03938:   lotteryKey,
03939:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
03940:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
03941:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
03942: }) {
03943:   const targetYmd = safeStr(ymd);
03944:   const day = Array.isArray(drawsToday) ? [...drawsToday] : [];
03945:   const range = Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? [...drawsRange] : [];
03946: 
03947:   if (!isYMD(targetYmd) || !range.length) return [];
03948: 
03949:   const schedule = getScheduleForLottery({
03950:     lotteryKey,
03951:     ymd: targetYmd,
03952:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
03953:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
03954:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
03955:   });
03956: 
03957:   const normalizedSchedule = (Array.isArray(schedule) ? schedule : [])
03958:     .map(toHourBucket)
03959:     .filter(Boolean);
03960: 
03961:   if (!normalizedSchedule.length) return [];
03962: 
03963:   const daySorted = day
03964:     .filter((d) => isYMD(pickDrawYMD(d)) && toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d)))
03965:     .sort((a, b) => {
03966:       const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
03967:       const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
03968:       return ta - tb;
03969:     });
03970: 
03971:   const rangeSorted = range
03972:     .filter((d) => isYMD(pickDrawYMD(d)) && toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d)))
03973:     .sort((a, b) => {
03974:       const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
03975:       const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
03976:       return ta - tb;
03977:     });
03978: 
03979:   const realizedTodayMap = new Map();
03980: 
03981:   for (const d of daySorted) {
03982:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
03983:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
03984:     if (!isYMD(y) || !h) continue;
03985:     if (y !== targetYmd) continue;
03986:     realizedTodayMap.set(`${y}|${h}`, d);
03987:   }
03988: 
03989: 
03990:   const chainContextDraws = [];
03991:   const timeline = [];
03992:   let pendingPredictionAlreadyShown = false;
03993: 
03994:   for (const slotHour of normalizedSchedule) {
03995:     const currentDraw = realizedTodayMap.get(`${targetYmd}|${slotHour}`) || null;
03996:     const slotTs = ymdHourToTs(targetYmd, slotHour);
03997: 
03998: 
03999:     if (!currentDraw && pendingPredictionAlreadyShown) {
04000:       timeline.push({
04001:         targetYmd,
04002:         targetHour: slotHour,
04003:         baseYmd: "",
04004:         baseHour: "",
04005:         top3: [],
04006:         resultGrupo: null,
04007:         hit: null,
04008:         status: "pending",
04009:       });
04010:       continue;
04011:     }
04012: 
04013:     let baseDraw = null;
04014: 
04015:     const priorChainDraw = [...chainContextDraws]
04016:       .filter((d) => {
04017:         const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
04018:         const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
04019:         const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
04020:         return isYMD(y) && h && Number.isFinite(ts) && ts < slotTs;
04021:       })
04022:       .sort((a, b) => {
04023:         const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
04024:         const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
04025:         return tb - ta;
04026:       })[0] || null;
04027: 
04028:     if (priorChainDraw) baseDraw = priorChainDraw;
04029: 
04030:     if (!baseDraw) {
04031:       const prevSameDay = daySorted
04032:         .filter((d) => {
04033:           const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
04034:           const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
04035:           const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
04036:           return y === targetYmd && h && Number.isFinite(ts) && ts < slotTs;
04037:         })
04038:         .sort((a, b) => {
04039:           const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
04040:           const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
04041:           return tb - ta;
04042:         })[0] || null;
04043: 
04044:       if (prevSameDay) baseDraw = prevSameDay;
04045:     }
04046: 
04047:     if (!baseDraw) {
04048:       for (let i = rangeSorted.length - 1; i >= 0; i -= 1) {
04049:         const d = rangeSorted[i];
04050:         const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
04051:         const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
04052:         const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
04053: 
04054:         if (!Number.isFinite(ts) || ts >= slotTs) continue;
04055: 
04056:         baseDraw = d;
04057:         break;
04058:       }
04059:     }
04060: 
04061:     if (!baseDraw) {
04062:       baseDraw = findPreviousValidDraw(rangeSorted, targetYmd, slotHour);
04063:     }
04064: 
04065:     if (!baseDraw) {
04066:       timeline.push({
04067:         targetYmd,
04068:         targetHour: slotHour,
04069:         baseYmd: "",
04070:         baseHour: "",
04071:         top3: [],
04072:         resultGrupo: null,
04073:         hit: null,
04074:         status: "pending",
04075:       });
04076:       continue;
04077:     }
04078: 
04079:     const baseTs = ymdHourToTs(
04080:       pickDrawYMD(baseDraw),
04081:       toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(baseDraw))
04082:     );
04083: 
04084:     const usableHistory = rangeSorted.filter((d) => {
04085:       const ts = ymdHourToTs(
04086:         pickDrawYMD(d),
04087:         toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d))
04088:       );
04089:       return Number.isFinite(ts) && ts <= baseTs;
04090:     });
04091: 
04092:     const usableTodayContext = [...chainContextDraws]
04093:       .filter((d) => {
04094:         const ts = ymdHourToTs(
04095:           pickDrawYMD(d),
04096:           toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d))
04097:         );
04098:         return Number.isFinite(ts) && ts <= baseTs;
04099:       })
04100:       .sort((a, b) => {
04101:         const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
04102:         const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
04103:         return ta - tb;
04104:       });
04105: 
04106:     const computed = computeStatisticalTop3V3({
04107:       lotteryKey,
04108:       drawsRange: [...usableHistory, ...usableTodayContext],
04109:       drawLast: baseDraw,
04110:       drawsToday: usableTodayContext,
04111:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
04112:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
04113:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
04114:       topN: 3,
04115:       targetYmdOverride: targetYmd,
04116:       targetHourOverride: slotHour,
04117:     });
04118: 
04119: 
04120:     const computedNextYmd = safeStr(computed?.meta?.next?.ymd || "");
04121:     const computedNextHour = toHourBucket(computed?.meta?.next?.hour || "");
04122:     const computedForSlot =
04123:       computedNextYmd === targetYmd && computedNextHour === slotHour;
04124: 
04125:     const top3 =
04126:       computedForSlot && Array.isArray(computed?.top)
04127:         ? computed.top.slice(0, 3)
04128:         : [];
04129: 
04130:     const resultGrupo = currentDraw ? pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(currentDraw) : null;
04131: 
04132:     const hit =
04133:       Number.isFinite(Number(resultGrupo)) && top3.length
04134:         ? top3.some((t) => Number(t?.grupo) === Number(resultGrupo))
04135:         : null;
04136: 
04137:     timeline.push({
04138:       targetYmd,
04139:       targetHour: slotHour,
04140:       baseYmd: pickDrawYMD(baseDraw) || "",
04141:       baseHour: toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(baseDraw)) || "",
04142:       top3,
04143:       resultGrupo: Number.isFinite(Number(resultGrupo)) ? Number(resultGrupo) : null,
04144:       hit,
04145:       status: Number.isFinite(Number(resultGrupo)) ? "validated" : "pending",
04146:     });
04147: 
04148:     if (currentDraw) {
04149:       chainContextDraws.push(currentDraw);
04150:     } else {
04151:       pendingPredictionAlreadyShown = true;
04152:     }
04153:   }
04154: 
04155:   return timeline;
04156: 
04157: }
04158: 
04159: export function buildTimelineTop3(args) {
04160:   return buildTimelineForDate(args || {});
04161: }
04162: 
04163: export function auditTop3Backtest({
04164:   drawsRange,
04165:   lotteryKey,
04166:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
04167:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
04168:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
04169: }) {
04170:   const range = Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? drawsRange : [];
04171:   const dates = Array.from(
04172:     new Set(
04173:       range
04174:         .map((d) => pickDrawYMD(d))
04175:         .filter((ymd) => isYMD(ymd))
04176:     )
04177:   ).sort();
04178: 
04179:   const allTimeline = [];
04180: 
04181:   for (const ymd of dates) {
04182:     const drawsToday = range.filter((d) => pickDrawYMD(d) === ymd);
04183: 
04184:     const dayTimeline = buildTimelineForDate({
04185:       ymd,
04186:       drawsToday,
04187:       drawsRange: range,
04188:       lotteryKey,
04189:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
04190:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
04191:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
04192:     });
04193: 
04194:     allTimeline.push(...(Array.isArray(dayTimeline) ? dayTimeline : []));
04195:   }
04196: 
04197:   return auditTop3Timeline({
04198:     timeline: allTimeline,
04199:     lotteryKey,
04200:   });
04201: }

00032: function findPreviousValidDraw(draws, currentYmd, currentHour) {
00033:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
00034:   const targetY = safeStr(currentYmd);
00035:   const targetH = toHourBucket(currentHour);
00036: 
00037:   if (!isYMD(targetY) || !targetH) return null;
00038: 
00039:   const targetTs = ymdHourToTs(targetY, targetH);
00040: 
00041:   return list
00042:     .filter((draw) => Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) && draw.prizes.length > 0)
00043:     .map((draw) => {
00044:       const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
00045:       const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
00046:       return { draw, ts: ymdHourToTs(y, h) };
00047:     })
00048:     .filter((x) => Number.isFinite(x.ts) && x.ts < targetTs)
00049:     .sort((a, b) => b.ts - a.ts)[0]?.draw || null;
00050: }

00056: function grupoFromDezena2(dezena2) {
00057:   const s = safeStr(dezena2);
00058:   if (!/^\d{2}$/.test(s)) return null;
00059: 
00060:   const n = Number(s);
00061:   if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n < 0 || n > 99) return null;
00062:   if (n === 0) return 25;
00063: 
00064:   const g = Math.ceil(n / 4);
00065:   return g >= 1 && g <= 25 ? g : null;
00066: }

00068: function drawQualityScore(draw) {
00069:   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
00070:   const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
00071:   const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
00072: 
00073:   let score = 0;
00074:   if (isYMD(y)) score += 20;
00075:   if (h) score += 20;
00076:   score += Math.min(10, prizes.length) * 10;
00077: 
00078:   const hasP1 = prizes.some((p) => guessPrizePos(p) === 1);
00079:   if (hasP1) score += 15;
00080: 
00081:   return score;
00082: }
