00084: export function guessPrizePos(p) {
00085:   const pos = Number.isFinite(Number(p?.position))
00086:     ? Number(p.position)
00087:     : Number.isFinite(Number(p?.posicao))
00088:       ? Number(p.posicao)
00089:       : Number.isFinite(Number(p?.pos))
00090:         ? Number(p.pos)
00091:         : Number.isFinite(Number(p?.colocacao))
00092:           ? Number(p.colocacao)
00093:           : null;
00094: 
00095:   return pos;
00096: }

00190: function getDayOfMonth(ymd) {

00195: function ymdHourToTs(ymd, hourBucket) {
00196:   const y = safeStr(ymd);
00197:   const h = toHourBucket(hourBucket);
00198: 
00199:   if (!isYMD(y) || !h) return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
00200: 
00201:   const mins = hourToInt(h);
00202:   if (!Number.isFinite(mins) || mins < 0) {
00203:     return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
00204:   }
00205: 
00206:   const [Y, M, D] = y.split("-").map((x) => Number(x));
00207:   const base = Date.UTC(Y, M - 1, D);
00208: 
00209:   return base + mins * 60 * 1000;
00210: }

00261: export function findLastDrawInList(draws, schedule) {
00262:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
00263:   if (!list.length) return null;
00264: 
00265:   const sorted = [...list]
00266:     .filter((d) => isHourInSchedule(schedule, pickDrawHour(d)))
00267:     .sort((a, b) => {
00268:       const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
00269:       const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
00270:       return tb - ta;
00271:     });
00272: 
00273:   return sorted[0] || null;
00274: }

00276: export function findPrevDrawBeforeTargetInSameDay(
00277:   draws,
00278:   targetHourBucket,
00279:   schedule
00280: ) {
00281:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
00282:   if (!list.length) return null;
00283: 
00284:   const t = hourToInt(targetHourBucket);
00285:   if (t < 0) return null;
00286: 
00287:   const candidates = list
00288:     .filter((d) => isHourInSchedule(schedule, pickDrawHour(d)))
00289:     .map((d) => ({ d, h: hourToInt(toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d))) }))
00290:     .filter((x) => x.h >= 0 && x.h < t)
00291:     .sort((a, b) => b.h - a.h);
00292: 
00293:   return candidates.length ? candidates[0].d : null;
00294: }

00437: export function findNextExistingDrawFromSlot({
00438:   lotteryKey,
00439:   startSlot,
00440:   drawsIndex,
00441:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
00442:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
00443:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
00444:   maxSteps = TOP3_NEXTDRAW_SCAN_MAX_STEPS,
00445:   maxDays = TOP3_NEXTDRAW_SCAN_MAX_DAYS,
00446: }) {
00447:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
00448:   const y0 = safeStr(startSlot?.ymd);
00449:   const h0 = toHourBucket(startSlot?.hour);
00450: 
00451:   if (!isYMD(y0) || !h0 || !(drawsIndex instanceof Map)) {
00452:     return { slot: null, draw: null };
00453:   }
00454: 
00455:   if (key === "FEDERAL") {
00456:     let curY = y0;
00457:     let steps = 0;
00458:     let daysWalked = 0;
00459: 
00460:     while (steps < maxSteps && daysWalked <= maxDays) {
00461:       const sch = getScheduleForLottery({
00462:         lotteryKey: key,
00463:         ymd: curY,
00464:         PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
00465:         PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
00466:         FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
00467:       });
00468: 
00469:       if (Array.isArray(sch) && sch.length) {
00470:         const hh = toHourBucket(sch[0]);
00471:         const d = drawsIndex.get(`${curY}|${hh}`) || null;
00472:         if (d) return { slot: { ymd: curY, hour: hh }, draw: d };
00473:       }
00474: 
00475:       curY = addDaysYMD(curY, 1);
00476:       daysWalked += 1;
00477:       steps += 1;
00478:     }
00479: 
00480:     return { slot: null, draw: null };
00481:   }
00482: 
00483:   let curY = y0;
00484:   let curH = h0;
00485:   let steps = 0;
00486:   let daysWalked = 0;
00487: 
00488:   while (steps < maxSteps && daysWalked <= maxDays) {
00489:     const drawKey = `${curY}|${toHourBucket(curH)}`;
00490:     let d = drawsIndex.get(drawKey) || null;
00491: 
00492:     if (!d) {
00493:       const curTs = ymdHourToTs(curY, curH);
00494:       const curYLocked = curY;
00495:       d = Array.from(drawsIndex.entries())
00496:         .map(([k, v]) => {
00497:           const [ky, kh] = String(k).split("|");
00498:           return { ymd: ky, hour: kh, draw: v, ts: ymdHourToTs(ky, kh) };
00499:         })
00500:         .filter((x) => x.ymd === curYLocked)
00501:         .filter((x) => Number.isFinite(x.ts) && x.ts >= curTs)
00502:         .sort((a, b) => a.ts - b.ts)[0]?.draw || null;
00503:     }
00504: 
00505:     if (d) return { slot: { ymd: curY, hour: toHourBucket(curH) }, draw: d };
00506: 
00507:     const sch = getScheduleForLottery({
00508:       lotteryKey: key,
00509:       ymd: curY,
00510:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
00511:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
00512:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
00513:     });
00514: 
00515:     const arr = Array.isArray(sch) ? sch.map(toHourBucket) : [];
00516:     const curBucket = toHourBucket(curH);
00517:     const idx = arr.indexOf(curBucket);
00518: 
00519:     if (idx >= 0 && idx < arr.length - 1) {
00520:       curH = arr[idx + 1];
00521:     } else {
00522:       curY = addDaysYMD(curY, 1);
00523:       daysWalked += 1;
00524: 
00525:       const sch2 = getScheduleForLottery({
00526:         lotteryKey: key,
00527:         ymd: curY,
00528:         PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
00529:         PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
00530:         FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
00531:       });
00532: 
00533:       const arr2 = Array.isArray(sch2) ? sch2.map(toHourBucket) : [];
00534:       if (!arr2.length) break;
00535:       curH = arr2[0];
00536:     }
00537: 
00538:     steps += 1;
00539:   }
00540: 
00541:   return { slot: null, draw: null };
00542: }
00543: 
00544: /* =========================
00545:    Index + utilitários de grupo
00546: ========================= */
00547: 
00548: export function indexDrawsByYmdHour(draws) {
00549:   const map = new Map();
00550:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
00551: 
00552:   for (const d of list) {
00553:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
00554:     const rawH = pickDrawHour(d);
00555:     const h = toHourBucket(rawH);
00556: 
00557:     if (!isYMD(y) || !h) continue;
00558: 
00559:     const drawKey = `${y}|${h}`;
00560:     const prev = map.get(drawKey);
00561: 
00562:     if (!prev) {
00563:       map.set(drawKey, d);
00564:       continue;
00565:     }
00566: 
00567:     if (drawQualityScore(d) >= drawQualityScore(prev)) {
00568:       map.set(drawKey, d);
00569:     }
00570:   }
00571: 
00572:   return map;
00573: }
00574: 
00575: export function computeLastSeenByGrupo(draws) {
00576:   const last = new Map();
00577:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
00578: 
00579:   for (const d of list) {
00580:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
00581:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
00582:     if (!isYMD(y) || !h) continue;
00583: 
00584:     const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
00585:     const ps = Array.isArray(d?.prizes) ? d.prizes : [];
00586: 
00587:     for (const p of ps) {
00588:       const pos = guessPrizePos(p);
00589:       if (!Number.isFinite(Number(pos)) || Number(pos) < 1 || Number(pos) > 5) {
00590:         continue;
00591:       }
00592: 
00593:       const g = guessPrizeGrupo(p);
00594:       if (!Number.isFinite(Number(g)) || Number(g) <= 0) continue;
00595: 
00596:       const gg = Number(g);
00597:       const prev = last.get(gg);
00598: 
00599:       if (!Number.isFinite(prev) || ts > prev) {
00600:         last.set(gg, ts);
00601:       }
00602:     }
00603:   }
00604: 
00605:   return last;
00606: }
00607: 
00608: export function countAparicoesByGrupoInDraw(draw) {
00609:   const counts = new Map();
00610:   const ps = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
00611:   for (const p of ps) {
00612:     const pos = guessPrizePos(p);
00613:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(pos)) || Number(pos) < 1 || Number(pos) > 5) {
00614:       continue;
00615:     }
00616:     const g = guessPrizeGrupo(p);
00617:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(g)) || Number(g) <= 0) continue;
00618:     const gg = Number(g);
00619:     counts.set(gg, (counts.get(gg) || 0) + 1);
00620:   }
00621:   return counts;
00622: }
00623: 
00624: function getFirstGrupoFromDraw(draw) {
00625:   const ps = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
00626:   const p1 = ps.find((p) => guessPrizePos(p) === 1) || null;
00627:   if (p1) {
00628:     const g = guessPrizeGrupo(p1);
00629:     return Number.isFinite(Number(g)) ? Number(g) : null;
00630:   }
00631:   return pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(draw);
00632: }
00633: 
00634: function getAllTop5Groups(draw) {
00635:   const out = [];
00636:   const ps = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
00637:   for (const p of ps) {
00638:     const pos = guessPrizePos(p);
00639:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(pos)) || Number(pos) < 1 || Number(pos) > 5) {
00640:       continue;
00641:     }
00642:     const g = guessPrizeGrupo(p);
00643:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(g)) || Number(g) <= 0) continue;
00644:     out.push({ pos: Number(pos), grupo: Number(g) });
00645:   }
00646:   return out.sort((a, b) => a.pos - b.pos);
00647: }
00648: 
00649: function buildFreqAndFirstMaps(nextDraw) {
00650:   const freq = new Map();
00651:   const firstMap = new Map();
00652:   const items = getAllTop5Groups(nextDraw);
00653: 
00654:   for (const it of items) {
00655:     freq.set(it.grupo, (freq.get(it.grupo) || 0) + 1);
00656:     if (it.pos === 1) {
00657:       firstMap.set(it.grupo, (firstMap.get(it.grupo) || 0) + 1);
00658:     }
00659:   }
00660: 
00661:   return { freq, firstMap };
00662: }
00663: 
00664: function mergeMapsAdd(target, source) {
00665:   for (const [k, v] of source.entries()) {
00666:     target.set(Number(k), Number(target.get(Number(k)) || 0) + Number(v || 0));
00667:   }
00668: }
00669: 
00670: function probFromFreq(freq, samples, groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
00671:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
00672:   const denom = Math.max(1, Number(samples || 0) * 5);
00673:   const out = new Map();
00674: 
00675:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
00676:     const n = Number(freq.get(g) || 0);
00677:     out.set(g, n / denom);
00678:   }
00679: 
00680:   return out;
00681: }
00682: 
00683: function computeStructuralBaseDistribution(
00684:   draws,
00685:   lotteryKey,
00686:   targetHour,
00687:   targetDow,
00688:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
00689: ) {
00690:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
00691:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
00692:   const target = toHourBucket(targetHour);
00693:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
00694: 
00695:   const freq = new Map();
00696:   let totalSamples = 0;
00697: 
00698:   for (const d of list) {
00699:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
00700:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
00701:     if (!h || !y) continue;
00702: 
00703:     if (key === "FEDERAL") {
00704:       if (!isFederalDrawDay(y)) continue;
00705:       if (Number(getDowKey(y)) !== Number(targetDow)) continue;
00706:     } else {
00707:       if (h !== target) continue;
00708:     }
00709: 
00710:     const items = getAllTop5Groups(d);
00711:     if (!items.length) continue;
00712: 
00713:     totalSamples += 1;
00714: 
00715:     for (const it of items) {
00716:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
00717:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
00718:       freq.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) + 1);
00719:     }
00720:   }
00721: 
00722:   const out = new Map();
00723:   const denom = Math.max(1, totalSamples * 5);
00724: 
00725:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
00726:     const n = Number(freq.get(g) || 0);
00727:     out.set(g, n / denom);
00728:   }
00729: 
00730:   return { scoreProb: out, totalSamples };
00731: }
00732: 
00733: /* =========================
00734:    Recência / pressão estrutural
00735: ========================= */
00736: 
00737: function sortDrawsAsc(draws) {
00738:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
00739:   return [...list].sort((a, b) => {
00740:     const ya = pickDrawYMD(a);
00741:     const yb = pickDrawYMD(b);
00742:     const ha = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a));
00743:     const hb = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b));
00744:     const tsa = ymdHourToTs(ya, ha);
00745:     const tsb = ymdHourToTs(yb, hb);
00746:     return tsa - tsb;
00747:   });
00748: }
00749: 
00750: function getRecentDrawsBefore(draws, drawLast, count = 6) {
00751:   const list = sortDrawsAsc(draws);
00752:   const lastY = pickDrawYMD(drawLast);
00753:   const lastH = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(drawLast));
00754:   const lastTs = ymdHourToTs(lastY, lastH);
00755: 
00756:   const prev = list.filter((d) => {
00757:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
00758:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
00759:     const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
00760:     return Number.isFinite(ts) && ts < lastTs;
00761:   });
00762: 
00763:   return prev.slice(-Math.max(1, Number(count || 6)));
00764: }
00765: 
00766: function computeRecentPressureMetrics(recentDraws, groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
00767:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
00768:   const out = new Map();
00769: 
00770:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
00771:     out.set(g, {
00772:       recentTop5: 0,
00773:       recentIndirect: 0,
00774:       recentFirst: 0,
00775:       recentLast1First: 0,
00776:       recentLast2Top5: 0,
00777:     });
00778:   }
00779: 
00780:   const list = Array.isArray(recentDraws) ? recentDraws : [];
00781:   const last1 = list.length ? [list[list.length - 1]] : [];
00782:   const last2 = list.slice(-2);
00783: 
00784:   for (const d of list) {
00785:     const items = getAllTop5Groups(d);
00786: 
00787:     for (const it of items) {
00788:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
00789:       const pos = Number(it.pos);
00790:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
00791: 
00792:       const row = out.get(g);
00793:       row.recentTop5 += 1;
00794: 
00795:       if (pos >= 2 && pos <= 4) row.recentIndirect += 1;
00796:       if (pos === 1) row.recentFirst += 1;
00797:     }
00798:   }
00799: 
00800:   for (const d of last1) {
00801:     const items = getAllTop5Groups(d);
00802:     for (const it of items) {
00803:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
00804:       const pos = Number(it.pos);
00805:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
00806:       if (pos === 1) {
00807:         out.get(g).recentLast1First += 1;
00808:       }
00809:     }
00810:   }
00811: 
00812:   for (const d of last2) {
00813:     const items = getAllTop5Groups(d);
00814:     const seen = new Set();
00815:     for (const it of items) {
00816:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
00817:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
00818:       if (seen.has(g)) continue;
00819:       seen.add(g);
00820:       out.get(g).recentLast2Top5 += 1;
00821:     }
00822:   }
00823: 
00824:   return out;
00825: }
00826: 
00827: function normalizeMetric(value, maxValue) {
00828:   const v = Number(value || 0);
00829:   const m = Number(maxValue || 0);
00830:   if (!Number.isFinite(v) || !Number.isFinite(m) || m <= 0) return 0;
00831:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, v / m));
00832: }
00833: 
00834: function clamp01(v) {
00835:   const n = Number(v);
00836:   if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return 0;
00837:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, n));
00838: }
00839: 
00840: function buildZeroProbMap(groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
00841:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
00842:   return new Map(Array.from({ length: k }, (_, i) => [i + 1, 0]));
00843: }
00844: 
00845: function confidenceFromSamples(samples, fullConfidenceAt = 8) {
00846:   const n = Number(samples || 0);
00847:   const lim = Math.max(1, Number(fullConfidenceAt || 8));
00848:   return clamp01(n / lim);
00849: }

00855: const LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES = {
00856:   DOM_DOW_PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET: 3,
00857:   DOW_PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET: 5,
00858:   PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET: 8,
00859:   DOM_TARGET: 8,
00860:   DOW_TARGET: 12,
00861:   TARGET_ONLY: 20,
00862: 
00863:   FED_DOW_PREVG: 3,
00864:   FED_PREVG: 4,
00865:   FED_DOW_ONLY: 6,
00866:   FED_ONLY: 10,
00867: };
00868: 
00869: function buildLayerConfigs({
00870:   lotteryKey,
00871:   targetDayOfMonth,
00872:   targetDow,
00873:   prevHour,
00874:   prevGrupo,
00875:   targetHour,
00876: }) {
00877:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
00878: 
00879:   if (key === "FEDERAL") {
00880:     return [
00881:       {
00882:         key: "FED_DOW_PREVG",
00883:         label: "Federal: dia da semana + grupo anterior",
00884:         minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.FED_DOW_PREVG,
00885:         match: (ctx) =>
00886:           ctx.targetDow === targetDow &&
00887:           ctx.prevGrupo === prevGrupo,
00888:       },
00889:       {
00890:         key: "FED_PREVG",
00891:         label: "Federal: grupo anterior",
00892:         minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.FED_PREVG,
00893:         match: (ctx) => ctx.prevGrupo === prevGrupo,
00894:       },
00895:       {
00896:         key: "FED_DOW_ONLY",
00897:         label: "Federal: dia da semana",
00898:         minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.FED_DOW_ONLY,
00899:         match: (ctx) => ctx.targetDow === targetDow,
00900:       },
00901:       {
00902:         key: "FED_ONLY",
00903:         label: "Federal: histórico geral",
00904:         minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.FED_ONLY,
00905:         match: () => true,
00906:       },
00907:     ];
00908:   }
00909: 
00910:   return [
00911:     {
00912:       key: "DOM_DOW_PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET",
00913:       label: "dia do mês + dia da semana + hora anterior + grupo anterior -> alvo",
00914:       minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.DOM_DOW_PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET,
00915:       match: (ctx) =>
00916:         ctx.targetDayOfMonth === targetDayOfMonth &&
00917:         ctx.targetDow === targetDow &&
00918:         ctx.prevHour === prevHour &&
00919:         ctx.prevGrupo === prevGrupo &&
00920:         ctx.targetHour === targetHour,
00921:     },
00922:     {
00923:       key: "DOW_PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET",
00924:       label: "dia da semana + hora anterior + grupo anterior -> alvo",
00925:       minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.DOW_PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET,
00926:       match: (ctx) =>
00927:         ctx.targetDow === targetDow &&
00928:         ctx.prevHour === prevHour &&
00929:         ctx.prevGrupo === prevGrupo &&
00930:         ctx.targetHour === targetHour,
00931:     },
00932:     {
00933:       key: "PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET",
00934:       label: "hora anterior + grupo anterior -> alvo",
00935:       minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET,
00936:       match: (ctx) =>
00937:         ctx.prevHour === prevHour &&
00938:         ctx.prevGrupo === prevGrupo &&
00939:         ctx.targetHour === targetHour,
00940:     },
00941:     {
00942:       key: "DOM_TARGET",
00943:       label: "dia do mês + alvo",
00944:       minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.DOM_TARGET,
00945:       match: (ctx) =>
00946:         ctx.targetDayOfMonth === targetDayOfMonth &&
00947:         ctx.targetHour === targetHour,
00948:     },
00949:     {
00950:       key: "DOW_TARGET",
00951:       label: "dia da semana + alvo",
00952:       minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.DOW_TARGET,
00953:       match: (ctx) =>
00954:         ctx.targetDow === targetDow &&
00955:         ctx.targetHour === targetHour,
00956:     },
00957:     {
00958:       key: "TARGET_ONLY",
00959:       label: "alvo geral",
00960:       minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.TARGET_ONLY,
00961:       match: (ctx) => ctx.targetHour === targetHour,
00962:     },
00963:   ];
00964: }
00965: 
00966: function buildConditionalLayerDistribution({
00967:   lotteryKey,
00968:   drawsRange,
00969:   targetHour,
00970:   targetDow,
00971:   targetDayOfMonth,
00972:   prevHour,
00973:   prevGrupo,
00974:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
00975:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
00976:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
00977: }) {
00978:   const list = Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? drawsRange : [];
00979:   const drawsIndex = indexDrawsByYmdHour(list);
00980: 
00981:   const layers = buildLayerConfigs({
00982:     lotteryKey,
00983:     targetDayOfMonth,
00984:     targetDow,
00985:     prevHour,
00986:     prevGrupo,
00987:     targetHour,
00988:   });
00989: 
00990:   const layerResults = layers.map((layer) => ({
00991:     key: layer.key,
00992:     label: layer.label,
00993:     minSamples: layer.minSamples,
00994:     samples: 0,
00995:     freq: new Map(),
00996:     firstFreq: new Map(),
00997:     matchingDraws: [],
00998:   }));
00999: 
01000:   for (const d of list) {
01001:     const prevY = pickDrawYMD(d);
01002:     const prevH = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
01003:     if (!isYMD(prevY) || !prevH) continue;
01004: 
01005:     const prevG = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(d);
01006:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(prevG)) || Number(prevG) <= 0) continue;
01007: 
01008:     const ns = getNextSlotForLottery({
01009:       lotteryKey,
01010:       ymd: prevY,
01011:       hourBucket: prevH,
01012:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
01013:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
01014:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
01015:     });
01016: 
01017:     if (!ns?.ymd || !ns?.hour) continue;
01018: 
01019:     const found = findNextExistingDrawFromSlot({
01020:       lotteryKey,
01021:       startSlot: { ymd: ns.ymd, hour: ns.hour },
01022:       drawsIndex,
01023:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
01024:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
01025:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
01026:     });
01027: 
01028:     const nextDraw = found?.draw || null;
01029:     const nextSlot = found?.slot || null;
01030:     if (!nextDraw || !nextSlot?.ymd || !nextSlot?.hour) continue;
01031: 
01032:     const ctx = {
01033:       prevYmd: prevY,
01034:       prevHour: prevH,
01035:       prevGrupo: Number(prevG),
01036:       targetYmd: safeStr(nextSlot.ymd),
01037:       targetHour: toHourBucket(nextSlot.hour),
01038:       targetDow: getDowKey(nextSlot.ymd),
01039:       targetDayOfMonth: getDayOfMonth(nextSlot.ymd),
01040:     };
01041: 
01042:     const nextMaps = buildFreqAndFirstMaps(nextDraw);
01043: 
01044:     for (let i = 0; i < layers.length; i += 1) {
01045:       const layer = layers[i];
01046:       if (!layer.match(ctx)) continue;
01047: 
01048:       layerResults[i].samples += 1;
01049:       layerResults[i].matchingDraws.push(nextDraw);
01050:       mergeMapsAdd(layerResults[i].freq, nextMaps.freq);
01051:       mergeMapsAdd(layerResults[i].firstFreq, nextMaps.firstMap);
01052:     }
01053:   }
01054: 
01055:   let chosen =
01056:     layerResults.find((x) => x.samples >= x.minSamples && x.freq.size > 0) ||
01057:     layerResults.find((x) => x.samples > 0 && x.freq.size > 0) ||
01058:     null;
01059: 
01060:   if (!chosen) {
01061:     chosen = {
01062:       key: "NONE",
01063:       label: "sem amostra",
01064:       minSamples: 0,
01065:       samples: 0,
01066:       freq: new Map(),
01067:       firstFreq: new Map(),
01068:       matchingDraws: [],
01069:     };
01070:   }
01071: 
01072:   return {
01073:     chosen,
01074:     layers: layerResults,
01075:   };
01076: }
01077: 
01078: /* =========================
01079:    Motor principal V1
01080: ========================= */
01081: 
01082: export function computeConditionalNextTop3({
01083:   lotteryKey,
01084:   drawsRange,
01085:   drawLast,
01086:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
01087:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
01088:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
01089:   topN = 3,
01090:   targetYmdOverride = "",
01091:   targetHourOverride = "",
01092: }) {
01093:   const list = Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? drawsRange : [];
01094:   if (!list.length || !drawLast) {
01095:     return { top: [], meta: null };
01096:   }
01097: 
01098:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
01099: 
01100:   const lastY = pickDrawYMD(drawLast);
01101:   const lastH = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(drawLast));
01102:   const prevGrupo = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(drawLast);
01103: 
01104:   if (
01105:     !isYMD(lastY) ||
01106:     !lastH ||
01107:     !Number.isFinite(Number(prevGrupo)) ||
01108:     Number(prevGrupo) <= 0
01109:   ) {
01110:     return { top: [], meta: null };
01111:   }
01112: 
01113:   const nextSlot = getNextSlotForLottery({
01114:     lotteryKey,
01115:     ymd: lastY,
01116:     hourBucket: lastH,
01117:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
01118:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
01119:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
01120:   });
01121: 
01122:   const forcedTargetY = safeStr(targetYmdOverride);
01123:   const forcedTargetH = toHourBucket(targetHourOverride);
01124: 
01125:   const targetY = isYMD(forcedTargetY) && forcedTargetH
01126:     ? forcedTargetY
01127:     : safeStr(nextSlot?.ymd);
01128: 
01129:   const targetH = isYMD(forcedTargetY) && forcedTargetH
01130:     ? forcedTargetH
01131:     : toHourBucket(nextSlot?.hour);
01132:   const targetDow = getDowKey(targetY);
01133:   const targetDayOfMonth = getDayOfMonth(targetY);
01134:   const transition = `${lastH}->${targetH}`;
01135: 
01136:   const useFirstFocusedRanking =
01137:     key !== "FEDERAL" &&
01138:     (transition === "11h->14h" || transition === "14h->16h");
01139: 
01140:   if (!isYMD(targetY) || !targetH || !Number.isFinite(targetDow)) {
01141:     return { top: [], meta: null };
01142:   }
01143: 
01144:   const layerOut = buildConditionalLayerDistribution({
01145:     lotteryKey,
01146:     drawsRange: list,
01147:     targetHour: targetH,
01148:     targetDow,
01149:     targetDayOfMonth,
01150:     prevHour: lastH,
01151:     prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
01152:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
01153:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
01154:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
01155:   });
01156: 
01157:   const chosen = layerOut.chosen;
01158:   const samples = Number(chosen?.samples || 0);
01159:   const freq = chosen?.freq || new Map();
01160:   const firstFreq = chosen?.firstFreq || new Map();
01161:   const probCond = probFromFreq(freq, samples, TOP3_GROUPS_K);
01162: 
01163:   const structural = computeStructuralBaseDistribution(
01164:     list,
01165:     key,
01166:     targetH,
01167:     targetDow,
01168:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
01169:   );
01170:   const probBase = structural?.scoreProb || new Map();
01171: 
01172:   const condWeight =
01173:     key === "FEDERAL"
01174:       ? samples >= 8
01175:         ? 0.55
01176:         : samples >= 4
01177:           ? 0.4
01178:           : 0.25
01179:       : samples >= 12
01180:         ? 0.65
01181:         : samples >= 6
01182:           ? 0.55
01183:           : 0.45;
01184: 
01185:   const baseWeight = 1 - condWeight;
01186: 
01187:   const lastSeen = computeLastSeenByGrupo(list);
01188:   const nowTs = ymdHourToTs(lastY, lastH);
01189: 
01190:   const finiteLastSeen = Array.from(lastSeen.values()).filter((v) =>
01191:     Number.isFinite(Number(v))
01192:   );
01193:   const minLastSeenTs = finiteLastSeen.length ? Math.min(...finiteLastSeen) : nowTs;
01194:   const maxGapMsBase =
01195:     Number.isFinite(nowTs) && Number.isFinite(minLastSeenTs)
01196:       ? Math.max(1, nowTs - minLastSeenTs)
01197:       : 1;
01198: 
01199:   const recentDraws = getRecentDrawsBefore(list, drawLast, 6);
01200:   const recentMetrics = computeRecentPressureMetrics(recentDraws, TOP3_GROUPS_K);
01201: 
01202:   const recentMaxTop5 = Math.max(
01203:     1,
01204:     ...Array.from(recentMetrics.values()).map((x) => Number(x.recentTop5 || 0))
01205:   );
01206:   const recentMaxIndirect = Math.max(
01207:     1,
01208:     ...Array.from(recentMetrics.values()).map((x) => Number(x.recentIndirect || 0))
01209:   );
01210:   const recentMaxFirst = Math.max(
01211:     1,
01212:     ...Array.from(recentMetrics.values()).map((x) => Number(x.recentFirst || 0))
01213:   );
01214: 
01215:   const ranked = Array.from({ length: safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25) }, (_, idx) => {
01216:     const grupo = idx + 1;
01217:     const aparicoes = Number(freq.get(grupo) || 0);
01218:     const primeiros = Number(firstFreq.get(grupo) || 0);
01219:     const pCond = Number(probCond.get(grupo) || 0);
01220:     const pBase = Number(probBase.get(grupo) || 0);
01221:     const pFinal = (pCond * condWeight) + (pBase * baseWeight);
01222: 
01223:     const ls = lastSeen.get(grupo);
01224:     const lastSeenTs = Number.isFinite(ls) ? ls : Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
01225: 
01226:     const gapMs =
01227:       Number.isFinite(nowTs) &&
01228:       Number.isFinite(lastSeenTs) &&
01229:       lastSeenTs !== Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY
01230:         ? Math.max(0, nowTs - lastSeenTs)
01231:         : maxGapMsBase;
01232: 
01233:     const taxaPrimeiro = samples > 0 ? primeiros / samples : 0;
01234:     const lateBonusRaw = Math.max(0, gapMs) / Math.max(1, maxGapMsBase);
01235:     const lateBonus = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, lateBonusRaw));
01236: 
01237:     const rm = recentMetrics.get(grupo) || {
01238:       recentTop5: 0,
01239:       recentIndirect: 0,
01240:       recentFirst: 0,
01241:       recentLast1First: 0,
01242:       recentLast2Top5: 0,
01243:     };
01244: 
01245:     const recentTop5Norm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentTop5, recentMaxTop5);
01246:     const recentIndirectNorm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentIndirect, recentMaxIndirect);
01247:     const recentFirstNorm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentFirst, recentMaxFirst);
01248:     const recentLast1FirstNorm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentLast1First, 1);
01249:     const recentLast2Top5Norm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentLast2Top5, 2);
01250: 
01251:     const recencyCoolingFactor =
01252:       rm.recentLast1First > 0
01253:         ? 0.72
01254:         : rm.recentFirst >= 2
01255:           ? 0.84
01256:           : 1.0;
01257: 
01258:     const smartLateBoost =
01259:       lateBonus *
01260:       (0.45 + (recentIndirectNorm * 0.35) + (recentLast2Top5Norm * 0.2)) *
01261:       recencyCoolingFactor;
01262: 
01263:     const recentComposite =
01264:       (recentTop5Norm * 0.45) +
01265:       (recentIndirectNorm * 0.35) +
01266:       (recentFirstNorm * 0.2);
01267: 
01268:     const baseScore =
01269:       key === "FEDERAL"
01270:         ? (pFinal * 145) +
01271:           (pBase * 85) +
01272:           (primeiros * 42) +
01273:           (aparicoes * 18) +
01274:           (recentComposite * 26) +
01275:           (smartLateBoost * 10)
01276:         : useFirstFocusedRanking
01277:           ? (pFinal * 125) +
01278:             (aparicoes * 155) +
01279:             (primeiros * 70) +
01280:             (taxaPrimeiro * 34) +
01281:             (recentComposite * 18) +
01282:             (recentLast1FirstNorm * 10) +
01283:             (smartLateBoost * 16)
01284:           : (pFinal * 125) +
01285:             (aparicoes * 135) +
01286:             (primeiros * 62) +
01287:             (recentComposite * 24) +
01288:             (recentLast1FirstNorm * 8) +
01289:             (smartLateBoost * 18);
01290: 
01291:     return {
01292:       grupo,
01293:       aparicoes,
01294:       primeiros,
01295:       taxaPrimeiro,
01296:       scoreProb: pFinal,
01297:       probCond: pCond,
01298:       probBase: pBase,
01299:       score: baseScore,
01300:       lateBonus,
01301:       smartLateBoost,
01302:       recentTop5: rm.recentTop5,
01303:       recentIndirect: rm.recentIndirect,
01304:       recentFirst: rm.recentFirst,
01305:       recentLast1First: rm.recentLast1First,
01306:       recentLast2Top5: rm.recentLast2Top5,
01307:       recentComposite,
01308:       lastSeenTs,
01309:       gapMs,
01310:     };
01311:   })
01312:     .sort((a, b) => {
01313:       if (b.score !== a.score) return b.score - a.score;
01314:       if (b.scoreProb !== a.scoreProb) return b.scoreProb - a.scoreProb;
01315:       if (b.primeiros !== a.primeiros) return b.primeiros - a.primeiros;
01316:       if (b.aparicoes !== a.aparicoes) return b.aparicoes - a.aparicoes;
01317:       return a.grupo - b.grupo;
01318:     })
01319:     .slice(0, Math.max(1, Number(topN || 3)));
01320: 
01321:   const reasonsBase = [
01322:     `Camada usada: ${chosen?.label || "—"}`,
01323:     `Amostra histórica condicional: ${samples}`,
01324:     `Amostra estrutural: ${Number(structural?.totalSamples || 0)}`,
01325:     `Pesos: condicional=${(condWeight * 100).toFixed(0)}% | estrutural=${(baseWeight * 100).toFixed(0)}%`,
01326:     `Estado atual: prev=${String(prevGrupo).padStart(2, "0")} @ ${lastH} → alvo ${targetH}`,
01327:     `Data alvo: ${targetY} | DOW=${targetDow} | dia=${String(targetDayOfMonth).padStart(2, "0")}`,
01328:   ];
01329: 
01330:   const top = ranked.map((x, idx) => {
01331:     const g2 = String(x.grupo).padStart(2, "0");
01332:     const pct = (x.scoreProb * 100).toFixed(2);
01333:     const pctCond = (x.probCond * 100).toFixed(2);
01334:     const pctBase = (x.probBase * 100).toFixed(2);
01335: 
01336:     return {
01337:       rank: idx + 1,
01338:       title:
01339:         idx === 0
01340:           ? "Mais provável"
01341:           : idx === 1
01342:             ? "2º mais provável"
01343:             : "3º mais provável",
01344:       grupo: x.grupo,
01345:       scoreProb: Number(x.scoreProb || 0),
01346:       probCond: Number(x.probCond || 0),
01347:       probBase: Number(x.probBase || 0),
01348:       lateBonus: Number(x.lateBonus || 0),
01349:       freq: x.aparicoes,
01350:       freqCond: x.aparicoes,
01351:       freqBase: 0,
01352:       freqZeroWhy:
01353:         x.aparicoes <= 0
01354:           ? `Sem ocorrência nesta camada (${chosen?.label || "—"}).`
01355:           : "",
01356:       reasons: [
01357:         ...reasonsBase,
01358:         `Grupo G${g2}: aparições=${x.aparicoes} em ${samples} amostras do próximo sorteio`,
01359:         `Grupo G${g2}: primeiros lugares=${x.primeiros}`,
01360:         `Probabilidade final estimada no TOP3: ${pct}%`,
01361:         `Composição da probabilidade: condicional=${pctCond}% | estrutural=${pctBase}%`,
01362:         `Presença indireta recente (2º-4º): ${x.recentIndirect}`,
01363:         `Persistência curta recente: ${x.recentTop5}`,
01364:         `Bônus de atraso ajustado: ${(Number(x.smartLateBoost || 0) * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
01365:       ],
01366:       meta: {
01367:         trigger: {
01368:           ymd: lastY,
01369:           hour: lastH,
01370:           grupo: Number(prevGrupo),
01371:         },
01372:         next: {
01373:           ymd: safeStr(targetY),
01374:           hour: safeStr(targetH),
01375:         },
01376:         samples,
01377:         scenario: chosen?.key || "NONE",
01378:         explain: {
01379:           layerKey: chosen?.key || "NONE",
01380:           layerLabel: chosen?.label || "—",
01381:           layerSamples: samples,
01382:           baseSamples: Number(structural?.totalSamples || 0),
01383:           condWeight,
01384:           baseWeight,
01385:           targetDow,
01386:           targetDayOfMonth,
01387:           prevHour: lastH,
01388:           prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
01389:           recentTop5: x.recentTop5,
01390:           recentIndirect: x.recentIndirect,
01391:           recentFirst: x.recentFirst,
01392:           recentLast1First: x.recentLast1First,
01393:           recentLast2Top5: x.recentLast2Top5,
01394:           allLayers: (layerOut?.layers || []).map((layer) => ({
01395:             key: layer.key,
01396:             label: layer.label,
01397:             samples: layer.samples,
01398:             minSamples: layer.minSamples,
01399:           })),
01400:         },
01401:       },
01402:     };
01403:   });
01404: 
01405:   return {
01406:     top,
01407:     meta: {
01408:       trigger: {
01409:         ymd: lastY,
01410:         hour: lastH,
01411:         grupo: Number(prevGrupo),
01412:       },
01413:       next: {
01414:         ymd: safeStr(targetY),
01415:         hour: safeStr(targetH),
01416:       },
01417:       samples,
01418:       scenario: chosen?.key || "NONE",
01419:       explain: {
01420:         layerKey: chosen?.key || "NONE",
01421:         layerLabel: chosen?.label || "—",
01422:         layerSamples: samples,
01423:         baseSamples: Number(structural?.totalSamples || 0),
01424:         condWeight,
01425:         baseWeight,
01426:         targetDow,
01427:         targetDayOfMonth,
01428:         prevHour: lastH,
01429:         prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
01430:         allLayers: (layerOut?.layers || []).map((layer) => ({
01431:           key: layer.key,
01432:           label: layer.label,
01433:           samples: layer.samples,
01434:           minSamples: layer.minSamples,
01435:         })),
01436:       },
01437:     },
01438:   };
01439: }

01441: /* =========================
01442:    V2 — helpers focados em BICHO
01443: ========================= */
01444: 
01445: function computeStructuralFirstDistribution(
01446:   draws,
01447:   lotteryKey,
01448:   targetHour,
01449:   targetDow,
01450:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
01451: ) {
01452:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
01453:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
01454:   const target = toHourBucket(targetHour);
01455:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
01456: 
01457:   const firstFreq = new Map();
01458:   let totalSamples = 0;
01459: 
01460:   for (const d of list) {
01461:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
01462:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
01463:     if (!isYMD(y) || !h) continue;
01464: 
01465:     if (key === "FEDERAL") {
01466:       if (!isFederalDrawDay(y)) continue;
01467:       if (Number(getDowKey(y)) !== Number(targetDow)) continue;
01468:     } else {
01469:       if (h !== target) continue;
01470:     }
01471: 
01472:     const g = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(d);
01473:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(g)) || Number(g) < 1 || Number(g) > k) continue;
01474: 
01475:     totalSamples += 1;
01476:     firstFreq.set(Number(g), Number(firstFreq.get(Number(g)) || 0) + 1);
01477:   }
01478: 
01479:   const prob = new Map();
01480:   const denom = Math.max(1, totalSamples);
01481: 
01482:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
01483:     prob.set(g, Number(firstFreq.get(g) || 0) / denom);
01484:   }
01485: 
01486:   return { prob, firstFreq, totalSamples };
01487: }
01488: 
01489: function getComparableDrawsForTargetContext(
01490:   draws,
01491:   lotteryKey,
01492:   targetHour,
01493:   targetDow
01494: ) {
01495:   const list = sortDrawsAsc(draws);
01496:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
01497:   const target = toHourBucket(targetHour);
01498: 
01499:   return list.filter((d) => {
01500:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
01501:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
01502:     if (!isYMD(y) || !h) return false;
01503: 
01504:     if (key === "FEDERAL") {
01505:       return isFederalDrawDay(y) && Number(getDowKey(y)) === Number(targetDow);
01506:     }
01507: 
01508:     return h === target;
01509:   });
01510: }
01511: 
01512: function buildComparableFirstSequence(
01513:   draws,
01514:   lotteryKey,
01515:   targetHour,
01516:   targetDow
01517: ) {
01518:   return getComparableDrawsForTargetContext(
01519:     draws,
01520:     lotteryKey,
01521:     targetHour,
01522:     targetDow
01523:   )
01524:     .map((d) => {
01525:       const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
01526:       const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
01527:       const grupo = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(d);
01528:       return {
01529:         draw: d,
01530:         ymd: y,
01531:         hour: h,
01532:         ts: ymdHourToTs(y, h),
01533:         grupo: Number(grupo),
01534:       };
01535:     })
01536:     .filter((x) =>
01537:       isYMD(x.ymd) &&
01538:       x.hour &&
01539:       Number.isFinite(Number(x.ts)) &&
01540:       Number.isFinite(Number(x.grupo)) &&
01541:       Number(x.grupo) >= 1 &&
01542:       Number(x.grupo) <= 25
01543:     );
01544: }
01545: 
01546: function getStateFromComparableSequenceBeforeTs(seq, beforeTs, count = 3) {
01547:   const list = Array.isArray(seq) ? seq : [];
01548:   const n = Math.max(1, Number(count || 3));
01549: 
01550:   return list
01551:     .filter((x) => Number.isFinite(Number(x.ts)) && Number(x.ts) < Number(beforeTs))
01552:     .slice(-n)
01553:     .reverse()
01554:     .map((x) => Number(x.grupo))
01555:     .filter((g) => Number.isFinite(g) && g >= 1 && g <= 25);
01556: }
01557: 
01558: function getComparableRecentDrawsBeforeTs(seq, beforeTs, count = 6) {
01559:   const list = Array.isArray(seq) ? seq : [];
01560:   return list
01561:     .filter((x) => Number.isFinite(Number(x.ts)) && Number(x.ts) < Number(beforeTs))
01562:     .slice(-Math.max(1, Number(count || 6)));
01563: }
01564: 
01565: function countLeadingStateMatches(currentState, histState) {
01566:   const a = Array.isArray(currentState) ? currentState : [];
01567:   const b = Array.isArray(histState) ? histState : [];
01568:   const len = Math.min(a.length, b.length);
01569: 
01570:   let matched = 0;
01571:   for (let i = 0; i < len; i += 1) {
01572:     if (Number(a[i]) !== Number(b[i])) break;
01573:     matched += 1;
01574:   }
01575:   return matched;
01576: }
01577: 
01578: function buildMemoryStateDistribution({
01579:   drawsRange,
01580:   lotteryKey,
01581:   targetHour,
01582:   targetDow,
01583:   currentState,
01584:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K,
01585: }) {
01586:   const seq = buildComparableFirstSequence(
01587:     drawsRange,
01588:     lotteryKey,
01589:     targetHour,
01590:     targetDow
01591:   );
01592: 
01593:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
01594:   const freq = new Map();
01595: 
01596:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
01597:     freq.set(g, 0);
01598:   }
01599: 
01600:   let totalWeight = 0;
01601:   let matchedSamples = 0;
01602: 
01603:   for (let i = 0; i < seq.length; i += 1) {
01604:     const target = seq[i];
01605:     const histState = [];
01606: 
01607:     for (let j = i - 1; j >= 0 && histState.length < 3; j -= 1) {
01608:       histState.push(Number(seq[j].grupo));
01609:     }
01610: 
01611:     const matchDepth = countLeadingStateMatches(currentState, histState);
01612:     if (matchDepth <= 0) continue;
01613: 
01614:     const weight =
01615:       matchDepth >= 3 ? 4.0 :
01616:       matchDepth === 2 ? 2.5 :
01617:       1.0;
01618: 
01619:     const g = Number(target.grupo);
01620:     if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
01621: 
01622:     freq.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) + weight);
01623:     totalWeight += weight;
01624:     matchedSamples += 1;
01625:   }
01626: 
01627:   const prob = new Map();
01628:   const denom = Math.max(1, totalWeight);
01629: 
01630:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
01631:     prob.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) / denom);
01632:   }
01633: 
01634:   return {
01635:     prob,
01636:     freq,
01637:     totalWeight,
01638:     matchedSamples,
01639:     state: Array.isArray(currentState) ? currentState : [],
01640:     sequenceSamples: seq.length,
01641:   };
01642: }
01643: 
01644: function detectRegimeFromComparableSequence(seqRecent) {
01645:   const list = Array.isArray(seqRecent) ? seqRecent : [];
01646:   const groups = list
01647:     .map((x) => Number(x?.grupo))
01648:     .filter((g) => Number.isFinite(g) && g >= 1 && g <= 25);
01649: 
01650:   if (groups.length <= 1) {
01651:     return {
01652:       regime: "neutral",
01653:       repeatRate: 0,
01654:       uniqueRate: groups.length ? 1 : 0,
01655:       samples: groups.length,
01656:     };
01657:   }
01658: 
01659:   let repeats = 0;
01660:   for (let i = 1; i < groups.length; i += 1) {
01661:     if (groups[i] === groups[i - 1]) repeats += 1;
01662:   }
01663: 
01664:   const repeatRate = repeats / Math.max(1, groups.length - 1);
01665:   const uniqueRate = new Set(groups).size / Math.max(1, groups.length);
01666: 
01667:   let regime = "neutral";
01668: 
01669:   if (repeatRate >= 0.34 || uniqueRate <= 0.5) {
01670:     regime = "repeat";
01671:   } else if (repeatRate === 0 && uniqueRate >= 0.84) {
01672:     regime = "spread";
01673:   }
01674: 
01675:   return {
01676:     regime,
01677:     repeatRate,
01678:     uniqueRate,
01679:     samples: groups.length,
01680:   };
01681: }
01682: 
01683: function computeComparableTop5Distribution(
01684:   draws,
01685:   lotteryKey,
01686:   targetHour,
01687:   targetDow,
01688:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
01689: ) {
01690:   const list = getComparableDrawsForTargetContext(
01691:     draws,
01692:     lotteryKey,
01693:     targetHour,
01694:     targetDow
01695:   );
01696: 
01697:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
01698:   const freq = new Map();
01699:   let totalSamples = 0;
01700: 
01701:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
01702:     freq.set(g, 0);
01703:   }
01704: 
01705:   for (const d of list) {
01706:     const items = getAllTop5Groups(d);
01707:     if (!items.length) continue;
01708: 
01709:     totalSamples += 1;
01710: 
01711:     for (const it of items) {
01712:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
01713:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
01714:       freq.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) + 1);
01715:     }
01716:   }
01717: 
01718:   const prob = new Map();
01719:   const denom = Math.max(1, totalSamples * 5);
01720: 
01721:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
01722:     prob.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) / denom);
01723:   }
01724: 
01725:   return { prob, freq, totalSamples };
01726: }
01727: 
01728: function computeComparableDuplicationDistribution(
01729:   draws,
01730:   lotteryKey,
01731:   targetHour,
01732:   targetDow,
01733:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
01734: ) {
01735:   const list = getComparableDrawsForTargetContext(
01736:     draws,
01737:     lotteryKey,
01738:     targetHour,
01739:     targetDow
01740:   );
01741: 
01742:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
01743:   const freq = new Map();
01744:   let totalSamples = 0;
01745: 
01746:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
01747:     freq.set(g, 0);
01748:   }
01749: 
01750:   for (const d of list) {
01751:     const counts = countAparicoesByGrupoInDraw(d);
01752:     if (!counts.size) continue;
01753: 
01754:     totalSamples += 1;
01755: 
01756:     for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
01757:       const c = Number(counts.get(g) || 0);
01758:       if (c >= 2) {
01759:         freq.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) + 1);
01760:       }
01761:     }
01762:   }
01763: 
01764:   const prob = new Map();
01765:   const denom = Math.max(1, totalSamples);
01766: 
01767:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
01768:     prob.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) / denom);
01769:   }
01770: 
01771:   return { prob, freq, totalSamples };
01772: }
01773: 
01774: function getComparableContextDrawsBeforeTs(
01775:   draws,
01776:   lotteryKey,
01777:   targetHour,
01778:   targetDow,
01779:   beforeTs,
01780:   count = 8
01781: ) {
01782:   return getComparableDrawsForTargetContext(draws, lotteryKey, targetHour, targetDow)
01783:     .filter((d) => {
01784:       const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
01785:       const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
01786:       const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
01787:       return Number.isFinite(Number(ts)) && ts < Number(beforeTs);
01788:     })
01789:     .slice(-Math.max(1, Number(count || 8)));
01790: }
01791: 
01792: function computeRecentBichoMetrics(draws, groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
01793:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
01794:   const out = new Map();
01795: 
01796:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
01797:     out.set(g, {
01798:       recentTop5: 0,
01799:       recentFirst: 0,
01800:       recentDupDraws: 0,
01801:       recentLast1Top5: 0,
01802:       recentLast2Top5: 0,
01803:       recentLast3Top5: 0,
01804:     });
01805:   }
01806: 
01807:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
01808:   const last1 = list.length ? [list[list.length - 1]] : [];
01809:   const last2 = list.slice(-2);
01810:   const last3 = list.slice(-3);
01811: 
01812:   for (const d of list) {
01813:     const items = getAllTop5Groups(d);
01814:     const perDrawCounts = new Map();
01815: 
01816:     for (const it of items) {
01817:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
01818:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
01819: 
01820:       const row = out.get(g);
01821:       row.recentTop5 += 1;
01822: 
01823:       if (Number(it.pos) === 1) {
01824:         row.recentFirst += 1;
01825:       }
01826: 
01827:       perDrawCounts.set(g, Number(perDrawCounts.get(g) || 0) + 1);
01828:     }
01829: 
01830:     for (const [g, c] of perDrawCounts.entries()) {
01831:       if (Number(c) >= 2) {
01832:         out.get(Number(g)).recentDupDraws += 1;
01833:       }
01834:     }
01835:   }
01836: 
01837:   for (const d of last1) {
01838:     const seen = new Set();
01839:     for (const it of getAllTop5Groups(d)) {
01840:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
01841:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k || seen.has(g)) continue;
01842:       seen.add(g);
01843:       out.get(g).recentLast1Top5 += 1;
01844:     }
01845:   }
01846: 
01847:   for (const d of last2) {
01848:     const seen = new Set();
01849:     for (const it of getAllTop5Groups(d)) {
01850:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
01851:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k || seen.has(g)) continue;
01852:       seen.add(g);
01853:       out.get(g).recentLast2Top5 += 1;
01854:     }
01855:   }
01856: 
01857:   for (const d of last3) {
01858:     const seen = new Set();
01859:     for (const it of getAllTop5Groups(d)) {
01860:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
01861:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k || seen.has(g)) continue;
01862:       seen.add(g);
01863:       out.get(g).recentLast3Top5 += 1;
01864:     }
01865:   }
01866: 
01867:   return out;
01868: }
01869: 
01870: function getDayDrivenWeights(mode) {
01871:   if (mode === "REPEAT") {
01872:     return {
01873:       transition: 0.25,
01874:       pair: 0.25,
01875:       memory: 0.2,
01876:       recent: 0.15,
01877:       structural: 0.1,
01878:       late: 0.05,
01879:     };
01880:   }
01881: 
01882:   if (mode === "SPREAD") {
01883:     return {
01884:       transition: 0.2,
01885:       pair: 0.1,
01886:       memory: 0.1,
01887:       recent: 0.2,
01888:       structural: 0.3,
01889:       late: 0.1,
01890:     };
01891:   }
01892: 
01893:   return {
01894:     transition: 0.3,
01895:     pair: 0.15,
01896:     memory: 0.15,
01897:     recent: 0.15,
01898:     structural: 0.2,
01899:     late: 0.05,
01900:   };
01901: }
01902: 
01903: function normalizeWeights(weights) {
01904:   const safe = {
01905:     transition: Number(weights?.transition || 0),
01906:     pair: Number(weights?.pair || 0),
01907:     memory: Number(weights?.memory || 0),
01908:     recent: Number(weights?.recent || 0),
01909:     structural: Number(weights?.structural || 0),
01910:     late: Number(weights?.late || 0),
01911:   };
01912: 
01913:   const total = Object.values(safe).reduce((acc, n) => acc + n, 0);
01914:   if (!Number.isFinite(total) || total <= 0) {
01915:     return {
01916:       transition: 0.3,
01917:       pair: 0.15,
01918:       memory: 0.15,
01919:       recent: 0.15,
01920:       structural: 0.2,
01921:       late: 0.05,
01922:     };
01923:   }
01924: 
01925:   return {
01926:     transition: safe.transition / total,
01927:     pair: safe.pair / total,
01928:     memory: safe.memory / total,
01929:     recent: safe.recent / total,
01930:     structural: safe.structural / total,
01931:     late: safe.late / total,
01932:   };
01933: }
01934: 
01935: function computeComparableLastSeenByGrupo(
01936:   draws,
01937:   lotteryKey,
01938:   targetHour,
01939:   targetDow,
01940:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
01941: ) {
01942:   const list = getComparableDrawsForTargetContext(
01943:     draws,
01944:     lotteryKey,
01945:     targetHour,
01946:     targetDow
01947:   );
01948: 
01949:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
01950:   const last = new Map();
01951: 
01952:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
01953:     last.set(g, Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY);
01954:   }
01955: 
01956:   for (const d of list) {
01957:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
01958:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
01959:     const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
01960:     const items = getAllTop5Groups(d);
01961: 
01962:     for (const it of items) {
01963:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
01964:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
01965: 
01966:       const prev = Number(last.get(g));
01967:       if (!Number.isFinite(prev) || ts > prev) {
01968:         last.set(g, ts);
01969:       }
01970:     }
01971:   }
01972: 
01973:   return last;
01974: }
01975: 
01976: function buildDayContext(drawsToday = []) {
01977:   const list = sortDrawsAsc(Array.isArray(drawsToday) ? drawsToday : []);
01978:   const freq = new Map();
01979:   const firstFreq = new Map();
01980:   const seq = [];
01981: 
01982:   for (const d of list) {
01983:     const g = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(d);
01984:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(g)) || Number(g) < 1 || Number(g) > 25) continue;
01985: 
01986:     const gg = Number(g);
01987:     seq.push(gg);
01988:     freq.set(gg, Number(freq.get(gg) || 0) + 1);
01989: 
01990:     const items = getAllTop5Groups(d);
01991:     if (items.some((it) => Number(it.grupo) === gg && Number(it.pos) === 1)) {
01992:       firstFreq.set(gg, Number(firstFreq.get(gg) || 0) + 1);
01993:     }
01994:   }
01995: 
01996:   const total = seq.length;
01997:   const unique = new Set(seq).size;
01998:   const diversidade = total > 0 ? unique / total : 0;
01999: 
02000:   let dominante = null;
02001:   let dominanteCount = -1;
02002:   for (const [g, n] of freq.entries()) {
02003:     if (Number(n) > dominanteCount) {
02004:       dominante = Number(g);
02005:       dominanteCount = Number(n);
02006:     }
02007:   }
02008: 
02009:   const last1 = total >= 1 ? seq[total - 1] : null;
02010:   const last2 = total >= 2 ? seq[total - 2] : null;
02011:   const repeatNow =
02012:     Number.isFinite(Number(last1)) &&
02013:     Number.isFinite(Number(last2)) &&
02014:     Number(last1) === Number(last2);
02015: 
02016:   return {
02017:     total,
02018:     unique,
02019:     diversidade,
02020:     dominante,
02021:     dominanteCount: Math.max(0, dominanteCount),
02022:     repeatNow,
02023:     freq,
02024:     firstFreq,
02025:     seq,
02026:   };
02027: }
02028: 
02029: function buildPairSequenceDistribution({
02030:   drawsRange,
02031:   lotteryKey,
02032:   targetHour,
02033:   targetDow,
02034:   targetDayOfMonth,
02035:   prevPair,
02036:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
02037:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
02038:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
02039:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K,
02040: }) {
02041:   const seq = buildComparableFirstSequence(
02042:     Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? drawsRange : [],
02043:     lotteryKey,
02044:     targetHour,
02045:     targetDow
02046:   );
02047: 
02048:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
02049:   const outFreq = new Map();
02050: 
02051:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) outFreq.set(g, 0);
02052: 
02053:   const pair = Array.isArray(prevPair) ? prevPair : [];
02054:   if (pair.length < 2) {
02055:     return {
02056:       prob: buildZeroProbMap(k),
02057:       samples: 0,
02058:       weightedSamples: 0,
02059:       exactDomSamples: 0,
02060:       freq: outFreq,
02061:     };
02062:   }
02063: 
02064:   const pA = Number(pair[0]);
02065:   const pB = Number(pair[1]);
02066:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
02067: 
02068:   let matchedSamples = 0;
02069:   let weightedSamples = 0;
02070:   let exactDomSamples = 0;
02071: 
02072:   for (let i = 2; i < seq.length; i += 1) {
02073:     const a = Number(seq[i - 2]?.grupo);
02074:     const b = Number(seq[i - 1]?.grupo);
02075:     const g = Number(seq[i]?.grupo);
02076:     const y = safeStr(seq[i]?.ymd);
02077: 
02078:     if (!Number.isFinite(a) || !Number.isFinite(b) || !Number.isFinite(g)) continue;
02079:     if (a !== pA || b !== pB) continue;
02080:     if (!isYMD(y)) continue;
02081: 
02082:     matchedSamples += 1;
02083: 
02084:     let weight = 1;
02085: 
02086:     if (key !== "FEDERAL") {
02087:       const dom = Number(getDayOfMonth(y));
02088:       if (Number.isFinite(dom) && Number(dom) === Number(targetDayOfMonth)) {
02089:         weight = 1.35;
02090:         exactDomSamples += 1;
02091:       }
02092:     }
02093: 
02094:     weightedSamples += weight;
02095:     outFreq.set(g, Number(outFreq.get(g) || 0) + weight);
02096:   }
02097: 
02098:   const prob = new Map();
02099:   const denom = Math.max(1, weightedSamples);
02100: 
02101:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
02102:     prob.set(g, Number(outFreq.get(g) || 0) / denom);
02103:   }
02104: 
02105:   return {
02106:     prob,
02107:     samples: matchedSamples,
02108:     weightedSamples,
02109:     exactDomSamples,
02110:     freq: outFreq,
02111:   };
02112: }
02113: 
02114: function buildSeq2FirstDistribution(
02115:   comparableSeq,
02116:   currentState,
02117:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
02118: ) {
02119:   const seq = Array.isArray(comparableSeq) ? comparableSeq : [];
02120:   const state = Array.isArray(currentState) ? currentState : [];
02121:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
02122: 
02123:   const freq = new Map();
02124:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) freq.set(g, 0);
02125: 
02126:   if (state.length < 2) {
02127:     const prob = new Map();
02128:     for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) prob.set(g, 0);
02129:     return { prob, freq, samples: 0, prev2: null, prev1: null };
02130:   }
02131: 
02132:   const prev1 = Number(state[0]);
02133:   const prev2 = Number(state[1]);
02134: 
02135:   let samples = 0;
02136: 
02137:   for (let i = 2; i < seq.length; i += 1) {
02138:     const gA = Number(seq[i - 2]?.grupo);
02139:     const gB = Number(seq[i - 1]?.grupo);
02140:     const gN = Number(seq[i]?.grupo);
02141: 
02142:     if (!Number.isFinite(gA) || !Number.isFinite(gB) || !Number.isFinite(gN)) continue;
02143:     if (gA !== prev2 || gB !== prev1) continue;
02144:     if (gN < 1 || gN > k) continue;
02145: 
02146:     samples += 1;
02147:     freq.set(gN, Number(freq.get(gN) || 0) + 1);
02148:   }
02149: 
02150:   const prob = new Map();
02151:   const denom = Math.max(1, samples);
02152: 
02153:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
02154:     prob.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) / denom);
02155:   }
02156: 
02157:   return { prob, freq, samples, prev2, prev1 };
02158: }
02159: 
02160: function buildRecentRepeatBoostMap(
02161:   comparableSeq,
02162:   targetTs,
02163:   windowSize = 4,
02164:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
02165: ) {
02166:   const seq = getComparableRecentDrawsBeforeTs(
02167:     Array.isArray(comparableSeq) ? comparableSeq : [],
02168:     targetTs,
02169:     windowSize
02170:   );
02171: 
02172:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
02173:   const counts = new Map();
02174:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) counts.set(g, 0);
02175: 
02176:   for (const item of seq) {
02177:     const g = Number(item?.grupo);
02178:     if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
02179:     counts.set(g, Number(counts.get(g) || 0) + 1);
02180:   }
02181: 
02182:   const boost = new Map();
02183:   const maxCount = Math.max(1, ...Array.from(counts.values()).map((n) => Number(n || 0)));
02184: 
02185:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
02186:     const c = Number(counts.get(g) || 0);
02187:     boost.set(g, c >= 2 ? c / maxCount : 0);
02188:   }
02189: 
02190:   return {
02191:     boost,
02192:     counts,
02193:     windowSize,
02194:     considered: Array.isArray(seq) ? seq.length : 0,
02195:   };
02196: }
02197: 
02198: /* =========================
02199:    Motor principal V2 (FOCO = BICHO)
02200: ========================= */
02201: 
02202: export function computeConditionalNextTop3V2({
02203:   lotteryKey,
02204:   drawsRange,
02205:   drawLast,
02206:   drawsToday,
02207:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
02208:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
02209:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
02210:   topN = 3,
02211:   targetYmdOverride = "",
02212:   targetHourOverride = "",
02213: }) {
02214:   const list = Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? drawsRange : [];
02215:   if (!list.length || !drawLast) {
02216:     return { top: [], meta: null };
02217:   }
02218: 
02219:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
02220: 
02221:   const lastY = pickDrawYMD(drawLast);
02222:   const lastH = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(drawLast));
02223:   const prevGrupo = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(drawLast);
02224: 
02225:   if (
02226:     !isYMD(lastY) ||
02227:     !lastH ||
02228:     !Number.isFinite(Number(prevGrupo)) ||
02229:     Number(prevGrupo) <= 0
02230:   ) {
02231:     return { top: [], meta: null };
02232:   }
02233: 
02234:   const nextSlot = getNextSlotForLottery({
02235:     lotteryKey,
02236:     ymd: lastY,
02237:     hourBucket: lastH,
02238:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
02239:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
02240:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
02241:   });
02242: 
02243:   const forcedTargetY = safeStr(targetYmdOverride);
02244:   const forcedTargetH = toHourBucket(targetHourOverride);
02245: 
02246:   const targetY = isYMD(forcedTargetY) && forcedTargetH
02247:     ? forcedTargetY
02248:     : safeStr(nextSlot?.ymd);
02249: 
02250:   const targetH = isYMD(forcedTargetY) && forcedTargetH
02251:     ? forcedTargetH
02252:     : toHourBucket(nextSlot?.hour);
02253:   const targetDow = getDowKey(targetY);
02254:   const targetDayOfMonth = getDayOfMonth(targetY);
02255: 
02256:   if (!isYMD(targetY) || !targetH || !Number.isFinite(Number(targetDow))) {
02257:     return { top: [], meta: null };
02258:   }
02259: 
02260:   const targetTs = ymdHourToTs(targetY, targetH);
02261: 
02262:   const dayContext = buildDayContext(drawsToday);
02263:   const prevPair =
02264:     Array.isArray(dayContext?.seq) && dayContext.seq.length >= 2
02265:       ? dayContext.seq.slice(-2)
02266:       : [];
02267: 
02268:   const pairOut = buildPairSequenceDistribution({
02269:     drawsRange: list,
02270:     lotteryKey: key,
02271:     targetHour: targetH,
02272:     targetDow,
02273:     targetDayOfMonth,
02274:     prevPair,
02275:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
02276:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
02277:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
02278:     groupsK: TOP3_GROUPS_K,
02279:   });
02280: 
02281:   const pairConfidence = clamp01(
02282:     (confidenceFromSamples(pairOut?.samples || 0, 6) * 0.7) +
02283:     (confidenceFromSamples(pairOut?.exactDomSamples || 0, 3) * 0.3)
02284:   );
02285: 
02286:   const layerOut = buildConditionalLayerDistribution({
02287:     lotteryKey,
02288:     drawsRange: list,
02289:     targetHour: targetH,
02290:     targetDow,
02291:     targetDayOfMonth,
02292:     prevHour: lastH,
02293:     prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
02294:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
02295:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
02296:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
02297:   });
02298: 
02299:   const chosen = layerOut?.chosen || null;
02300:   const condSamples = Number(chosen?.samples || 0);
02301:   const condFirstFreq = chosen?.firstFreq || new Map();
02302: 
02303:   const pTransitionFirst = new Map();
02304:   for (let g = 1; g <= safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25); g += 1) {
02305:     pTransitionFirst.set(
02306:       g,
02307:       Number(condFirstFreq.get(g) || 0) / Math.max(1, condSamples)
02308:     );
02309:   }
02310: 
02311:   const structuralFirst = computeStructuralFirstDistribution(
02312:     list,
02313:     key,
02314:     targetH,
02315:     targetDow,
02316:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
02317:   );
02318:   const pStructuralFirst = structuralFirst?.prob || new Map();
02319: 
02320:   const structuralTop5 = computeComparableTop5Distribution(
02321:     list,
02322:     key,
02323:     targetH,
02324:     targetDow,
02325:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
02326:   );
02327:   const pStructuralTop5 = structuralTop5?.prob || new Map();
02328: 
02329:   const duplication = computeComparableDuplicationDistribution(
02330:     list,
02331:     key,
02332:     targetH,
02333:     targetDow,
02334:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
02335:   );
02336:   const pDuplication = duplication?.prob || new Map();
02337: 
02338:   const comparableSeq = buildComparableFirstSequence(
02339:     list,
02340:     key,
02341:     targetH,
02342:     targetDow
02343:   );
02344: 
02345:   const currentState = getStateFromComparableSequenceBeforeTs(
02346:     comparableSeq,
02347:     targetTs,
02348:     3
02349:   );
02350: 
02351:   const memoryOut = buildMemoryStateDistribution({
02352:     drawsRange: list,
02353:     lotteryKey: key,
02354:     targetHour: targetH,
02355:     targetDow,
02356:     currentState,
02357:     groupsK: TOP3_GROUPS_K,
02358:   });
02359: 
02360:   const pMemory = memoryOut?.prob || new Map();
02361: 
02362:   const seq2Out = buildSeq2FirstDistribution(
02363:     comparableSeq,
02364:     currentState,
02365:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
02366:   );
02367:   const pSeq2 = seq2Out?.prob || new Map();
02368: 
02369:   const repeatBoostOut = buildRecentRepeatBoostMap(
02370:     comparableSeq,
02371:     targetTs,
02372:     4,
02373:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
02374:   );
02375:   const repeatBoostMap = repeatBoostOut?.boost || new Map();
02376: 
02377:   const regimeInfo = detectRegimeFromComparableSequence(
02378:     getComparableRecentDrawsBeforeTs(comparableSeq, targetTs, 6)
02379:   );
02380: 
02381:   const regime = safeStr(regimeInfo?.regime || "neutral");
02382: 
02383:   const mode =
02384:     dayContext.repeatNow || Number(dayContext?.diversidade || 0) < 0.5
02385:       ? "REPEAT"
02386:       : Number(dayContext?.diversidade || 0) > 0.75
02387:         ? "SPREAD"
02388:         : "NEUTRAL";
02389: 
02390:   const weights = getDayDrivenWeights(mode);
02391: 
02392:   const recentContextDraws = getComparableContextDrawsBeforeTs(
02393:     list,
02394:     key,
02395:     targetH,
02396:     targetDow,
02397:     targetTs,
02398:     8
02399:   );
02400:   const recentMetrics = computeRecentBichoMetrics(recentContextDraws, TOP3_GROUPS_K);
02401: 
02402:   const recentMaxTop5 = Math.max(
02403:     1,
02404:     ...Array.from(recentMetrics.values()).map((x) => Number(x.recentTop5 || 0))
02405:   );
02406:   const recentMaxFirst = Math.max(
02407:     1,
02408:     ...Array.from(recentMetrics.values()).map((x) => Number(x.recentFirst || 0))
02409:   );
02410:   const recentMaxDup = Math.max(
02411:     1,
02412:     ...Array.from(recentMetrics.values()).map((x) => Number(x.recentDupDraws || 0))
02413:   );
02414: 
02415:   const lateSeen = computeComparableLastSeenByGrupo(
02416:     list,
02417:     key,
02418:     targetH,
02419:     targetDow,
02420:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
02421:   );
02422: 
02423:   const finiteSeen = Array.from(lateSeen.values()).filter((x) =>
02424:     Number.isFinite(Number(x))
02425:   );
02426:   const minSeenTs = finiteSeen.length ? Math.min(...finiteSeen) : targetTs;
02427:   const maxGapMsBase =
02428:     Number.isFinite(targetTs) && Number.isFinite(minSeenTs)
02429:       ? Math.max(1, targetTs - minSeenTs)
02430:       : 1;
02431: 
02432:   const dayFlowConfidence = confidenceFromSamples(dayContext?.total || 0, 4);
02433:   const pairStrong = Number(pairOut?.samples || 0) >= 4;
02434: 
02435:   const confidence =
02436:     (pairConfidence * 0.3) +
02437:     (confidenceFromSamples(condSamples, 20) * 0.4) +
02438:     (confidenceFromSamples(memoryOut?.matchedSamples || 0, 15) * 0.3);
02439: 
02440:   const adjustedWeights = normalizeWeights({
02441:     transition: pairStrong ? weights.transition * 0.7 : weights.transition,
02442:     pair: pairStrong ? weights.pair * 1.4 : weights.pair,
02443:     memory: weights.memory,
02444:     recent: weights.recent,
02445:     structural: weights.structural,
02446:     late: weights.late,
02447:   });
02448: 
02449:   const ranked = Array.from({ length: safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25) }, (_, idx) => {
02450:     const grupo = idx + 1;
02451: 
02452:     const pT = Number(pTransitionFirst.get(grupo) || 0);
02453:     const pSF = Number(pStructuralFirst.get(grupo) || 0);
02454:     const pST = Number(pStructuralTop5.get(grupo) || 0);
02455:     const pM = Number(pMemory.get(grupo) || 0);
02456:     const pD = Number(pDuplication.get(grupo) || 0);
02457:     const pSeq = Number(pSeq2.get(grupo) || 0);
02458:     const repeatBoost = Number(repeatBoostMap.get(grupo) || 0);
02459:     const pPair = Number(pairOut?.prob?.get?.(grupo) || 0);
02460: 
02461:     const rm = recentMetrics.get(grupo) || {
02462:       recentTop5: 0,
02463:       recentFirst: 0,
02464:       recentDupDraws: 0,
02465:       recentLast1Top5: 0,
02466:       recentLast2Top5: 0,
02467:       recentLast3Top5: 0,
02468:     };
02469: 
02470:     const recentTop5Norm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentTop5, recentMaxTop5);
02471:     const recentFirstNorm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentFirst, recentMaxFirst);
02472:     const recentDupNorm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentDupDraws, recentMaxDup);
02473:     const recentLast1Norm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentLast1Top5, 1);
02474:     const recentLast2Norm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentLast2Top5, 2);
02475: 
02476:     const recentComposite =
02477:       (recentFirstNorm * 0.34) +
02478:       (recentTop5Norm * 0.24) +
02479:       (recentDupNorm * 0.18) +
02480:       (recentLast2Norm * 0.14) +
02481:       (recentLast1Norm * 0.1);
02482:

02484:       (pT * 0.42) +
02485:       (pSF * 0.26) +
02486:       (pST * 0.18) +
02487:       (pD * 0.14);
02488:

02490:     const gapMs =
02491:       Number.isFinite(targetTs) && Number.isFinite(seenTs)
02492:         ? Math.max(0, targetTs - seenTs)
02493:         : maxGapMsBase;
02494: 
02495:     const lateNorm = Math.max(
02496:       0,
02497:       Math.min(1, gapMs / Math.max(1, maxGapMsBase))
02498:     );
02499: 
02500:     const dayFreq = Number(dayContext?.freq?.get?.(grupo) || 0);
02501:     const dayFirstFreq = Number(dayContext?.firstFreq?.get?.(grupo) || 0);
02502:     const isDominantToday =
02503:       Number.isFinite(Number(dayContext?.dominante)) &&
02504:       Number(dayContext.dominante) === Number(grupo);
02505: 
02506:     let dayFlowRaw = 0;
02507: 
02508:     if (dayFreq >= 2) dayFlowRaw += 32;
02509:     if (dayFreq >= 3) dayFlowRaw += 18;
02510:     if (dayFirstFreq >= 1) dayFlowRaw += 20;
02511:     if (dayFirstFreq >= 2) dayFlowRaw += 14;
02512:     if (isDominantToday) dayFlowRaw += 24;
02513: 
02514:     if (Number(dayContext?.diversidade || 0) < 0.5) {
02515:       dayFlowRaw += dayFreq > 0 ? 12 : -8;
02516:     } else if (Number(dayContext?.diversidade || 0) > 0.75) {
02517:       dayFlowRaw += dayFreq === 0 ? 10 : 0;
02518:     }
02519: 
02520:     if (dayContext?.repeatNow && dayFreq > 0) {
02521:       dayFlowRaw += 10;
02522:     }
02523: 
02524:     const dayFlowScore = dayFlowRaw * dayFlowConfidence;
02525: 
02526:     const pairSequenceScore =
02527:       Number(pairOut?.samples || 0) > 0
02528:         ? ((pPair * 260) + Math.min(70, Number(pairOut.samples || 0) * 8)) * pairConfidence
02529:         : 0;
02530: 
02531:     const structuralBlend =
02532:       (pSF * 0.45) +
02533:       (pST * 0.35) +
02534:       (pD * 0.2);
02535: 
02536:     const memoryBlend =
02537:       (pM * 0.75) +
02538:       (pSeq * 0.25);
02539: 
02540:     const recentBlend =
02541:       (recentComposite * 0.85) +
02542:       (repeatBoost * 0.15);
02543: 
02544:     const prob =
02545:       (pT * adjustedWeights.transition) +
02546:       (pPair * adjustedWeights.pair * pairConfidence) +
02547:       (memoryBlend * adjustedWeights.memory) +
02548:       (recentBlend * adjustedWeights.recent) +
02549:       (structuralBlend * adjustedWeights.structural) +
02550:       (lateNorm * adjustedWeights.late);
02551: 
02552:     const score =
02553:       (prob * 1000) +
02554:       (dominanceScore * 180) +
02555:       (pairSequenceScore * 0.6) +
02556:       (dayFlowScore * 0.8) +
02557:       (confidence * 50);
02558: 
02559:     return {

02563:       score,

02602:       if (b.score !== a.score) return b.score - a.score;
02603:       if (b.scoreProb !== a.scoreProb) return b.scoreProb - a.scoreProb;
02604:       if (b.dominanceScore !== a.dominanceScore) return b.dominanceScore - a.dominanceScore;
02605:       if (b.probTransition !== a.probTransition) return b.probTransition - a.probTransition;
02606:       if (b.probPair !== a.probPair) return b.probPair - a.probPair;
02607:       if (b.probDuplication !== a.probDuplication) return b.probDuplication - a.probDuplication;
02608:       if (b.probStructuralFirst !== a.probStructuralFirst) return b.probStructuralFirst - a.probStructuralFirst;
02609:       if (b.probStructuralTop5 !== a.probStructuralTop5) return b.probStructuralTop5 - a.probStructuralTop5;
