01009: const LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES = {
01010:   DOM_DOW_PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET: 3,
01011:   DOW_PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET: 5,
01012:   PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET: 8,
01013:   DOM_TARGET: 8,
01014:   DOW_TARGET: 12,
01015:   TARGET_ONLY: 20,
01016: 
01017:   FED_DOW_PREVG: 3,
01018:   FED_PREVG: 4,
01019:   FED_DOW_ONLY: 6,
01020:   FED_ONLY: 10,
01021: };
01022: 
01023: function buildLayerConfigs({
01024:   lotteryKey,
01025:   targetDayOfMonth,
01026:   targetDow,
01027:   prevHour,
01028:   prevGrupo,
01029:   targetHour,
01030: }) {
01031:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
01032: 
01033:   if (key === "FEDERAL") {
01034:     return [
01035:       {
01036:         key: "FED_DOW_PREVG",
01037:         label: "Federal: dia da semana + grupo anterior",
01038:         minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.FED_DOW_PREVG,
01039:         match: (ctx) =>
01040:           ctx.targetDow === targetDow &&
01041:           ctx.prevGrupo === prevGrupo,
01042:       },
01043:       {
01044:         key: "FED_PREVG",
01045:         label: "Federal: grupo anterior",
01046:         minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.FED_PREVG,
01047:         match: (ctx) => ctx.prevGrupo === prevGrupo,
01048:       },
01049:       {
01050:         key: "FED_DOW_ONLY",
01051:         label: "Federal: dia da semana",
01052:         minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.FED_DOW_ONLY,
01053:         match: (ctx) => ctx.targetDow === targetDow,
01054:       },
01055:       {
01056:         key: "FED_ONLY",
01057:         label: "Federal: histórico geral",
01058:         minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.FED_ONLY,
01059:         match: () => true,
01060:       },
01061:     ];
01062:   }
01063: 
01064:   return [
01065:     {
01066:       key: "DOM_DOW_PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET",
01067:       label: "dia do mês + dia da semana + hora anterior + grupo anterior -> alvo",
01068:       minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.DOM_DOW_PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET,
01069:       match: (ctx) =>
01070:         ctx.targetDayOfMonth === targetDayOfMonth &&
01071:         ctx.targetDow === targetDow &&
01072:         ctx.prevHour === prevHour &&
01073:         ctx.prevGrupo === prevGrupo &&
01074:         ctx.targetHour === targetHour,
01075:     },
01076:     {
01077:       key: "DOW_PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET",
01078:       label: "dia da semana + hora anterior + grupo anterior -> alvo",
01079:       minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.DOW_PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET,
01080:       match: (ctx) =>
01081:         ctx.targetDow === targetDow &&
01082:         ctx.prevHour === prevHour &&
01083:         ctx.prevGrupo === prevGrupo &&
01084:         ctx.targetHour === targetHour,
01085:     },
01086:     {
01087:       key: "PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET",
01088:       label: "hora anterior + grupo anterior -> alvo",
01089:       minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET,
01090:       match: (ctx) =>
01091:         ctx.prevHour === prevHour &&
01092:         ctx.prevGrupo === prevGrupo &&
01093:         ctx.targetHour === targetHour,
01094:     },
01095:     {
01096:       key: "DOM_TARGET",
01097:       label: "dia do mês + alvo",
01098:       minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.DOM_TARGET,
01099:       match: (ctx) =>
01100:         ctx.targetDayOfMonth === targetDayOfMonth &&
01101:         ctx.targetHour === targetHour,
01102:     },
01103:     {
01104:       key: "DOW_TARGET",
01105:       label: "dia da semana + alvo",
01106:       minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.DOW_TARGET,
01107:       match: (ctx) =>
01108:         ctx.targetDow === targetDow &&
01109:         ctx.targetHour === targetHour,
01110:     },
01111:     {
01112:       key: "TARGET_ONLY",
01113:       label: "alvo geral",
01114:       minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.TARGET_ONLY,
01115:       match: (ctx) => ctx.targetHour === targetHour,
01116:     },
01117:   ];
01118: }
01119: 
01120: function buildConditionalLayerDistribution({
01121:   lotteryKey,
01122:   drawsRange,
01123:   targetHour,
01124:   targetDow,
01125:   targetDayOfMonth,
01126:   prevHour,
01127:   prevGrupo,
01128:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
01129:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
01130:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
01131: }) {
01132:   const list = Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? drawsRange : [];
01133:   const drawsIndex = indexDrawsByYmdHour(list);
01134: 
01135:   const layers = buildLayerConfigs({
01136:     lotteryKey,
01137:     targetDayOfMonth,
01138:     targetDow,
01139:     prevHour,
01140:     prevGrupo,
01141:     targetHour,
01142:   });
01143: 
01144:   const layerResults = layers.map((layer) => ({
01145:     key: layer.key,
01146:     label: layer.label,
01147:     minSamples: layer.minSamples,
01148:     samples: 0,
01149:     freq: new Map(),
01150:     firstFreq: new Map(),
01151:     matchingDraws: [],
01152:   }));
01153: 
01154:   for (const d of list) {
01155:     const prevY = pickDrawYMD(d);
01156:     const prevH = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
01157:     if (!isYMD(prevY) || !prevH) continue;
01158: 
01159:     const prevG = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(d);
01160:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(prevG)) || Number(prevG) <= 0) continue;
01161: 
01162:     const ns = getNextSlotForLottery({
01163:       lotteryKey,
01164:       ymd: prevY,
01165:       hourBucket: prevH,
01166:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
01167:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
01168:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
01169:     });
01170: 
01171:     if (!ns?.ymd || !ns?.hour) continue;
01172: 
01173:     const found = findNextExistingDrawFromSlot({
01174:       lotteryKey,
01175:       startSlot: { ymd: ns.ymd, hour: ns.hour },
01176:       drawsIndex,
01177:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
01178:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
01179:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
01180:     });
01181: 
01182:     const nextDraw = found?.draw || null;
01183:     const nextSlot = found?.slot || null;
01184:     if (!nextDraw || !nextSlot?.ymd || !nextSlot?.hour) continue;
01185: 
01186:     const ctx = {
01187:       prevYmd: prevY,
01188:       prevHour: prevH,
01189:       prevGrupo: Number(prevG),
01190:       targetYmd: safeStr(nextSlot.ymd),
01191:       targetHour: toHourBucket(nextSlot.hour),
01192:       targetDow: getDowKey(nextSlot.ymd),
01193:       targetDayOfMonth: getDayOfMonth(nextSlot.ymd),
01194:     };
01195: 
01196:     const nextMaps = buildFreqAndFirstMaps(nextDraw);
01197: 
01198:     for (let i = 0; i < layers.length; i += 1) {
01199:       const layer = layers[i];
01200:       if (!layer.match(ctx)) continue;
01201: 
01202:       layerResults[i].samples += 1;
01203:       layerResults[i].matchingDraws.push(nextDraw);
01204:       mergeMapsAdd(layerResults[i].freq, nextMaps.freq);
01205:       mergeMapsAdd(layerResults[i].firstFreq, nextMaps.firstMap);
01206:     }
01207:   }
01208: 
01209:   let chosen =
01210:     layerResults.find((x) => x.samples >= x.minSamples && x.freq.size > 0) ||
01211:     layerResults.find((x) => x.samples > 0 && x.freq.size > 0) ||
01212:     null;
01213: 
01214:   if (!chosen) {
01215:     chosen = {
01216:       key: "NONE",
01217:       label: "sem amostra",
01218:       minSamples: 0,
01219:       samples: 0,
01220:       freq: new Map(),
01221:       firstFreq: new Map(),
01222:       matchingDraws: [],
01223:     };
01224:   }
01225: 
01226:   return {
01227:     chosen,
01228:     layers: layerResults,
01229:   };
01230: }
01231: 
01232: /* =========================
01233:    Motor principal V1
01234: ========================= */
01235: 
01236: export function computeConditionalNextTop3({
01237:   lotteryKey,
01238:   drawsRange,
01239:   drawLast,
01240:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
01241:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
01242:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
01243:   topN = 3,
01244:   targetYmdOverride = "",
01245:   targetHourOverride = "",
01246: }) {
01247:   const list = Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? drawsRange : [];
01248:   if (!list.length || !drawLast) {
01249:     return { top: [], meta: null };
01250:   }
01251: 
01252:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
01253: 
01254:   const lastY = pickDrawYMD(drawLast);
01255:   const lastH = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(drawLast));
01256:   const prevGrupo = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(drawLast);
01257: 
01258:   if (
01259:     !isYMD(lastY) ||
01260:     !lastH ||
01261:     !Number.isFinite(Number(prevGrupo)) ||
01262:     Number(prevGrupo) <= 0
01263:   ) {
01264:     return { top: [], meta: null };
01265:   }
01266: 
01267:   const nextSlot = getNextSlotForLottery({
01268:     lotteryKey,
01269:     ymd: lastY,
01270:     hourBucket: lastH,
01271:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
01272:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
01273:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
01274:   });
01275: 
01276:   const forcedTargetY = safeStr(targetYmdOverride);
01277:   const forcedTargetH = toHourBucket(targetHourOverride);
01278: 
01279:   const targetY = isYMD(forcedTargetY) && forcedTargetH
01280:     ? forcedTargetY
01281:     : safeStr(nextSlot?.ymd);
01282: 
01283:   const targetH = isYMD(forcedTargetY) && forcedTargetH
01284:     ? forcedTargetH
01285:     : toHourBucket(nextSlot?.hour);
01286:   const targetDow = getDowKey(targetY);
01287:   const targetDayOfMonth = getDayOfMonth(targetY);
01288:   const transition = `${lastH}->${targetH}`;
01289: 
01290:   const useFirstFocusedRanking =
01291:     key !== "FEDERAL" &&
01292:     (transition === "11h->14h" || transition === "14h->16h");
01293: 
01294:   if (!isYMD(targetY) || !targetH || !Number.isFinite(targetDow)) {
01295:     return { top: [], meta: null };
01296:   }
01297: 
01298:   const layerOut = buildConditionalLayerDistribution({
01299:     lotteryKey,
01300:     drawsRange: list,
01301:     targetHour: targetH,
01302:     targetDow,
01303:     targetDayOfMonth,
01304:     prevHour: lastH,
01305:     prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
01306:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
01307:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
01308:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
01309:   });
01310: 
01311:   const chosen = layerOut.chosen;
01312:   const samples = Number(chosen?.samples || 0);
01313:   const freq = chosen?.freq || new Map();
01314:   const firstFreq = chosen?.firstFreq || new Map();
01315:   const probCond = probFromFreq(freq, samples, TOP3_GROUPS_K);
01316: 
01317:   const structural = computeStructuralBaseDistribution(
01318:     list,
01319:     key,
01320:     targetH,
01321:     targetDow,
01322:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
01323:   );
01324:   const probBase = structural?.scoreProb || new Map();
01325: 
01326:   const condWeight =
01327:     key === "FEDERAL"
01328:       ? samples >= 8
01329:         ? 0.55
01330:         : samples >= 4
01331:           ? 0.4
01332:           : 0.25
01333:       : samples >= 12
01334:         ? 0.65
01335:         : samples >= 6
01336:           ? 0.55
01337:           : 0.45;
01338: 
01339:   const baseWeight = 1 - condWeight;
01340: 
01341:   const lastSeen = computeLastSeenByGrupo(list);
01342:   const nowTs = ymdHourToTs(lastY, lastH);
01343: 
01344:   const finiteLastSeen = Array.from(lastSeen.values()).filter((v) =>
01345:     Number.isFinite(Number(v))
01346:   );
01347:   const minLastSeenTs = finiteLastSeen.length ? Math.min(...finiteLastSeen) : nowTs;
01348:   const maxGapMsBase =
01349:     Number.isFinite(nowTs) && Number.isFinite(minLastSeenTs)
01350:       ? Math.max(1, nowTs - minLastSeenTs)
01351:       : 1;
01352: 
01353:   const recentDraws = getRecentDrawsBefore(list, drawLast, 6);
01354:   const recentMetrics = computeRecentPressureMetrics(recentDraws, TOP3_GROUPS_K);
01355: 
01356:   const recentMaxPrizePresence = Math.max(
01357:     1,
01358:     ...Array.from(recentMetrics.values()).map((x) => Number(x.recentPrizePresence || 0))
01359:   );
01360:   const recentMaxIndirect = Math.max(
01361:     1,
01362:     ...Array.from(recentMetrics.values()).map((x) => Number(x.recentIndirect || 0))
01363:   );
01364:   const recentMaxFirst = Math.max(
01365:     1,
01366:     ...Array.from(recentMetrics.values()).map((x) => Number(x.recentFirst || 0))
01367:   );
01368: 
01369:   const ranked = Array.from({ length: safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25) }, (_, idx) => {
01370:     const grupo = idx + 1;
01371:     const aparicoes = Number(freq.get(grupo) || 0);
01372:     const primeiros = Number(firstFreq.get(grupo) || 0);
01373:     const pCond = Number(probCond.get(grupo) || 0);
01374:     const pBase = Number(probBase.get(grupo) || 0);
01375:     const pFinal = (pCond * condWeight) + (pBase * baseWeight);
01376: 
01377:     const ls = lastSeen.get(grupo);
01378:     const lastSeenTs = Number.isFinite(ls) ? ls : Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
01379: 
01380:     const gapMs =
01381:       Number.isFinite(nowTs) &&
01382:       Number.isFinite(lastSeenTs) &&
01383:       lastSeenTs !== Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY
01384:         ? Math.max(0, nowTs - lastSeenTs)
01385:         : maxGapMsBase;
01386: 
01387:     const taxaPrimeiro = samples > 0 ? primeiros / samples : 0;
01388:     const lateBonusRaw = Math.max(0, gapMs) / Math.max(1, maxGapMsBase);
01389:     const lateBonus = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, lateBonusRaw));
01390: 
01391:     const rm = recentMetrics.get(grupo) || {
01392:       recentPrizePresence: 0,
01393:       recentIndirect: 0,
01394:       recentFirst: 0,
01395:       recentLast1First: 0,
01396:       recentLast2PrizePresence: 0,
01397:     };
01398: 
01399:     const recentPrizePresenceNorm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentPrizePresence, recentMaxPrizePresence);
01400:     const recentIndirectNorm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentIndirect, recentMaxIndirect);
01401:     const recentFirstNorm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentFirst, recentMaxFirst);
01402:     const recentLast1FirstNorm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentLast1First, 1);
01403:     const recentLast2PrizePresenceNorm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentLast2PrizePresence, 2);
01404: 
01405:     const recencyCoolingFactor =
01406:       rm.recentLast1First > 0
01407:         ? 0.72
01408:         : rm.recentFirst >= 2
01409:           ? 0.84
01410:           : 1.0;
01411: 
01412:     const smartLateBoost =
01413:       lateBonus *
01414:       (0.45 + (recentIndirectNorm * 0.35) + (recentLast2PrizePresenceNorm * 0.2)) *
01415:       recencyCoolingFactor;
01416: 
01417:     const recentComposite =
01418:       (recentPrizePresenceNorm * 0.45) +
01419:       (recentIndirectNorm * 0.35) +
01420:       (recentFirstNorm * 0.2);
01421: 
01422:     const baseScore =
01423:       key === "FEDERAL"
01424:         ? (pFinal * 145) +
01425:           (pBase * 85) +
01426:           (primeiros * 42) +
01427:           (aparicoes * 18) +
01428:           (recentComposite * 26) +
01429:           (smartLateBoost * 10)
01430:         : useFirstFocusedRanking
01431:           ? (pFinal * 125) +
01432:             (aparicoes * 155) +
01433:             (primeiros * 70) +
01434:             (taxaPrimeiro * 34) +
01435:             (recentComposite * 18) +
01436:             (recentLast1FirstNorm * 10) +
01437:             (smartLateBoost * 16)
01438:           : (pFinal * 125) +
01439:             (aparicoes * 135) +
01440:             (primeiros * 62) +
01441:             (recentComposite * 24) +
01442:             (recentLast1FirstNorm * 8) +
01443:             (smartLateBoost * 18);
01444: 
01445:     return {
01446:       grupo,
01447:       aparicoes,
01448:       primeiros,
01449:       taxaPrimeiro,
01450:       scoreProb: pFinal,
01451:       probCond: pCond,
01452:       probBase: pBase,
01453:       score: baseScore,
01454:       lateBonus,
01455:       smartLateBoost,
01456:       recentPrizePresence: rm.recentPrizePresence,
01457:       recentIndirect: rm.recentIndirect,
01458:       recentFirst: rm.recentFirst,
01459:       recentLast1First: rm.recentLast1First,
01460:       recentLast2PrizePresence: rm.recentLast2PrizePresence,
01461:       recentComposite,
01462:       lastSeenTs,
01463:       gapMs,
01464:     };
01465:   })
01466:     .sort((a, b) => {
01467:       if (b.score !== a.score) return b.score - a.score;
01468:       if (b.scoreProb !== a.scoreProb) return b.scoreProb - a.scoreProb;
01469:       if (b.primeiros !== a.primeiros) return b.primeiros - a.primeiros;
01470:       if (b.aparicoes !== a.aparicoes) return b.aparicoes - a.aparicoes;
01471:       return a.grupo - b.grupo;
01472:     })
01473:     .slice(0, Math.max(1, Number(topN || 3)));
01474: 
01475:   const reasonsBase = [
01476:     `Camada usada: ${chosen?.label || "—"}`,
01477:     `Amostra histórica condicional: ${samples}`,
01478:     `Amostra estrutural: ${Number(structural?.totalSamples || 0)}`,
01479:     `Pesos: condicional=${(condWeight * 100).toFixed(0)}% | estrutural=${(baseWeight * 100).toFixed(0)}%`,
01480:     `Estado atual: prev=${String(prevGrupo).padStart(2, "0")} @ ${lastH} → alvo ${targetH}`,
01481:     `Data alvo: ${targetY} | DOW=${targetDow} | dia=${String(targetDayOfMonth).padStart(2, "0")}`,
01482:   ];
01483: 
01484:   const top = ranked.map((x, idx) => {
01485:     const g2 = String(x.grupo).padStart(2, "0");
01486:     const pct = (x.scoreProb * 100).toFixed(2);
01487:     const pctCond = (x.probCond * 100).toFixed(2);
01488:     const pctBase = (x.probBase * 100).toFixed(2);
01489: 
01490:     return {
01491:       rank: idx + 1,
01492:       title:
01493:         idx === 0
01494:           ? "Mais provável"
01495:           : idx === 1
01496:             ? "2º mais provável"
01497:             : "3º mais provável",
01498:       grupo: x.grupo,
01499:       scoreProb: Number(x.scoreProb || 0),
01500:       probCond: Number(x.probCond || 0),
01501:       probBase: Number(x.probBase || 0),
01502:       lateBonus: Number(x.lateBonus || 0),
01503:       freq: x.aparicoes,
01504:       freqCond: x.aparicoes,
01505:       freqBase: 0,
01506:       freqZeroWhy:
01507:         x.aparicoes <= 0
01508:           ? `Sem ocorrência nesta camada (${chosen?.label || "—"}).`
01509:           : "",
01510:       reasons: [
01511:         ...reasonsBase,
01512:         `Grupo G${g2}: aparições=${x.aparicoes} em ${samples} amostras do próximo sorteio`,
01513:         `Grupo G${g2}: primeiros lugares=${x.primeiros}`,
01514:         `Probabilidade final estimada no TOP3: ${pct}%`,
01515:         `Composição da probabilidade: condicional=${pctCond}% | estrutural=${pctBase}%`,
01516:         `Presença indireta recente (2º-4º): ${x.recentIndirect}`,
01517:         `Persistência curta recente: ${x.recentPrizePresence}`,
01518:         `Bônus de atraso ajustado: ${(Number(x.smartLateBoost || 0) * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
01519:       ],
01520:       meta: {
01521:         trigger: {
01522:           ymd: lastY,
01523:           hour: lastH,
01524:           grupo: Number(prevGrupo),
01525:         },
01526:         next: {
01527:           ymd: safeStr(targetY),
01528:           hour: safeStr(targetH),
01529:         },
01530:         samples,
01531:         scenario: chosen?.key || "NONE",
01532:         explain: {
01533:           layerKey: chosen?.key || "NONE",
01534:           layerLabel: chosen?.label || "—",
01535:           layerSamples: samples,
01536:           baseSamples: Number(structural?.totalSamples || 0),
01537:           condWeight,
01538:           baseWeight,
01539:           targetDow,
01540:           targetDayOfMonth,
01541:           prevHour: lastH,
01542:           prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
01543:           recentPrizePresence: x.recentPrizePresence,
01544:           recentIndirect: x.recentIndirect,
01545:           recentFirst: x.recentFirst,
01546:           recentLast1First: x.recentLast1First,
01547:           recentLast2PrizePresence: x.recentLast2PrizePresence,
01548:           allLayers: (layerOut?.layers || []).map((layer) => ({
01549:             key: layer.key,
01550:             label: layer.label,
01551:             samples: layer.samples,
01552:             minSamples: layer.minSamples,
01553:           })),
01554:         },
01555:       },
01556:     };
01557:   });
01558: 
01559:   return {
01560:     top,
01561:     meta: {
01562:       trigger: {
01563:         ymd: lastY,
01564:         hour: lastH,
01565:         grupo: Number(prevGrupo),
01566:       },
01567:       next: {
01568:         ymd: safeStr(targetY),
01569:         hour: safeStr(targetH),
01570:       },
01571:       samples,
01572:       scenario: chosen?.key || "NONE",
01573:       explain: {
01574:         layerKey: chosen?.key || "NONE",
01575:         layerLabel: chosen?.label || "—",
01576:         layerSamples: samples,
01577:         baseSamples: Number(structural?.totalSamples || 0),
01578:         condWeight,
01579:         baseWeight,
01580:         targetDow,
01581:         targetDayOfMonth,
01582:         prevHour: lastH,
01583:         prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
01584:         allLayers: (layerOut?.layers || []).map((layer) => ({
01585:           key: layer.key,
01586:           label: layer.label,
01587:           samples: layer.samples,
01588:           minSamples: layer.minSamples,
01589:         })),
01590:       },
01591:     },
01592:   };
01593: }
01594: 
01595: /* =========================
01596:    V2 — helpers focados em BICHO
01597: ========================= */
01598: 
01599: function computeStructuralFirstDistribution(
01600:   draws,
01601:   lotteryKey,
01602:   targetHour,
01603:   targetDow,
01604:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
01605: ) {
01606:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
01607:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
01608:   const target = toHourBucket(targetHour);
01609:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
01610: 
01611:   const firstFreq = new Map();
01612:   let totalSamples = 0;
01613: 
01614:   for (const d of list) {
01615:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
01616:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
01617:     if (!isYMD(y) || !h) continue;
01618: 
01619:     if (key === "FEDERAL") {
01620:       if (!isFederalDrawDay(y)) continue;
01621:       if (Number(getDowKey(y)) !== Number(targetDow)) continue;
01622:     } else {
01623:       if (h !== target) continue;
01624:     }
01625: 
01626:     const g = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(d);
01627:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(g)) || Number(g) < 1 || Number(g) > k) continue;
01628: 
01629:     totalSamples += 1;
01630:     firstFreq.set(Number(g), Number(firstFreq.get(Number(g)) || 0) + 1);
01631:   }
01632: 
01633:   const prob = new Map();
01634:   const denom = Math.max(1, totalSamples);
01635: 
01636:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
01637:     prob.set(g, Number(firstFreq.get(g) || 0) / denom);
01638:   }
01639: 
01640:   return { prob, firstFreq, totalSamples };
01641: }
01642: 
01643: function getComparableDrawsForTargetContext(
01644:   draws,
01645:   lotteryKey,
01646:   targetHour,
01647:   targetDow
01648: ) {
01649:   const list = sortDrawsAsc(draws);
01650:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
01651:   const target = toHourBucket(targetHour);
01652: 
01653:   return list.filter((d) => {
01654:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
01655:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
01656:     if (!isYMD(y) || !h) return false;
01657: 
01658:     if (key === "FEDERAL") {
01659:       return isFederalDrawDay(y) && Number(getDowKey(y)) === Number(targetDow);
01660:     }
01661: 
01662:     return h === target;
01663:   });
01664: }
01665: 
01666: function buildComparableFirstSequence(
01667:   draws,
01668:   lotteryKey,
01669:   targetHour,
01670:   targetDow
01671: ) {
01672:   return getComparableDrawsForTargetContext(
01673:     draws,
01674:     lotteryKey,
01675:     targetHour,
01676:     targetDow
01677:   )
01678:     .map((d) => {
01679:       const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
01680:       const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
01681:       const grupo = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(d);
01682:       return {
01683:         draw: d,
01684:         ymd: y,
01685:         hour: h,
01686:         ts: ymdHourToTs(y, h),
01687:         grupo: Number(grupo),
01688:       };
01689:     })
01690:     .filter((x) =>
01691:       isYMD(x.ymd) &&
01692:       x.hour &&
01693:       Number.isFinite(Number(x.ts)) &&
01694:       Number.isFinite(Number(x.grupo)) &&
01695:       Number(x.grupo) >= 1 &&
01696:       Number(x.grupo) <= 25
01697:     );
01698: }
01699: 
01700: function getStateFromComparableSequenceBeforeTs(seq, beforeTs, count = 3) {
01701:   const list = Array.isArray(seq) ? seq : [];
01702:   const n = Math.max(1, Number(count || 3));
01703: 
01704:   return list
01705:     .filter((x) => Number.isFinite(Number(x.ts)) && Number(x.ts) < Number(beforeTs))
01706:     .slice(-n)
01707:     .reverse()
01708:     .map((x) => Number(x.grupo))
01709:     .filter((g) => Number.isFinite(g) && g >= 1 && g <= 25);
01710: }
01711: 
01712: function getComparableRecentDrawsBeforeTs(seq, beforeTs, count = 6) {
01713:   const list = Array.isArray(seq) ? seq : [];
01714:   return list
01715:     .filter((x) => Number.isFinite(Number(x.ts)) && Number(x.ts) < Number(beforeTs))
01716:     .slice(-Math.max(1, Number(count || 6)));
01717: }
01718: 
01719: function countLeadingStateMatches(currentState, histState) {
01720:   const a = Array.isArray(currentState) ? currentState : [];
01721:   const b = Array.isArray(histState) ? histState : [];
01722:   const len = Math.min(a.length, b.length);
01723: 
01724:   let matched = 0;
01725:   for (let i = 0; i < len; i += 1) {
01726:     if (Number(a[i]) !== Number(b[i])) break;
01727:     matched += 1;
01728:   }
01729:   return matched;
01730: }
01731: 
01732: function buildMemoryStateDistribution({
01733:   drawsRange,
01734:   lotteryKey,
01735:   targetHour,
01736:   targetDow,
01737:   currentState,
01738:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K,
01739: }) {
01740:   const seq = buildComparableFirstSequence(
01741:     drawsRange,
01742:     lotteryKey,
01743:     targetHour,
01744:     targetDow
01745:   );
01746: 
01747:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
01748:   const freq = new Map();
01749: 
01750:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
01751:     freq.set(g, 0);
01752:   }
01753: 
01754:   let totalWeight = 0;
01755:   let matchedSamples = 0;
01756: 
01757:   for (let i = 0; i < seq.length; i += 1) {
01758:     const target = seq[i];
01759:     const histState = [];
01760: 
01761:     for (let j = i - 1; j >= 0 && histState.length < 3; j -= 1) {
01762:       histState.push(Number(seq[j].grupo));
01763:     }
01764: 
01765:     const matchDepth = countLeadingStateMatches(currentState, histState);
01766:     if (matchDepth <= 0) continue;
01767: 
01768:     const weight =
01769:       matchDepth >= 3 ? 4.0 :
01770:       matchDepth === 2 ? 2.5 :
01771:       1.0;
01772: 
01773:     const g = Number(target.grupo);
01774:     if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
01775: 
01776:     freq.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) + weight);
01777:     totalWeight += weight;
01778:     matchedSamples += 1;
01779:   }
01780: 
01781:   const prob = new Map();
01782:   const denom = Math.max(1, totalWeight);
01783: 
01784:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
01785:     prob.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) / denom);
01786:   }
01787: 
01788:   return {
01789:     prob,
01790:     freq,
01791:     totalWeight,
01792:     matchedSamples,
01793:     state: Array.isArray(currentState) ? currentState : [],
01794:     sequenceSamples: seq.length,
01795:   };
01796: }
01797: 
01798: function detectRegimeFromComparableSequence(seqRecent) {
01799:   const list = Array.isArray(seqRecent) ? seqRecent : [];
01800:   const groups = list
01801:     .map((x) => Number(x?.grupo))
01802:     .filter((g) => Number.isFinite(g) && g >= 1 && g <= 25);
01803: 
01804:   if (groups.length <= 1) {
01805:     return {
01806:       regime: "neutral",
01807:       repeatRate: 0,
01808:       uniqueRate: groups.length ? 1 : 0,
01809:       samples: groups.length,
01810:     };
01811:   }
01812: 
01813:   let repeats = 0;
01814:   for (let i = 1; i < groups.length; i += 1) {
01815:     if (groups[i] === groups[i - 1]) repeats += 1;
01816:   }
01817: 
01818:   const repeatRate = repeats / Math.max(1, groups.length - 1);
01819:   const uniqueRate = new Set(groups).size / Math.max(1, groups.length);
01820: 
01821:   let regime = "neutral";
01822: 
01823:   if (repeatRate >= 0.34 || uniqueRate <= 0.5) {
01824:     regime = "repeat";
01825:   } else if (repeatRate === 0 && uniqueRate >= 0.84) {
01826:     regime = "spread";
01827:   }
01828: 
01829:   return {
01830:     regime,
01831:     repeatRate,
01832:     uniqueRate,
01833:     samples: groups.length,
01834:   };
01835: }

01837: function computeComparablePrizePresenceDistribution(
01838:   draws,
01839:   lotteryKey,
01840:   targetHour,
01841:   targetDow,
01842:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
01843: ) {
01844:   const list = getComparableDrawsForTargetContext(
01845:     draws,
01846:     lotteryKey,
01847:     targetHour,
01848:     targetDow
01849:   );
01850: 
01851:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
01852:   const freq = new Map();
01853:   let totalSamples = 0;
01854: 
01855:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
01856:     freq.set(g, 0);
01857:   }
01858: 
01859:   for (const d of list) {
01860:     const items = getAllPrizePresenceGroups(d);
01861:     if (!items.length) continue;
01862: 
01863:     totalSamples += 1;
01864: 
01865:     for (const it of items) {
01866:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
01867:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
01868:       freq.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) + 1);
01869:     }
01870:   }
01871: 
01872:   const prob = new Map();
01873:   const denom = Math.max(1, totalSamples * 5);
01874: 
01875:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
01876:     prob.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) / denom);
01877:   }
01878: 
01879:   return { prob, freq, totalSamples };
01880: }

01882: function computeComparableDuplicationDistribution(
01883:   draws,
01884:   lotteryKey,
01885:   targetHour,
01886:   targetDow,
01887:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
01888: ) {
01889:   const list = getComparableDrawsForTargetContext(
01890:     draws,
01891:     lotteryKey,
01892:     targetHour,
01893:     targetDow
01894:   );
01895: 
01896:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
01897:   const freq = new Map();
01898:   let totalSamples = 0;
01899: 
01900:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
01901:     freq.set(g, 0);
01902:   }
01903: 
01904:   for (const d of list) {
01905:     const counts = countAparicoesByGrupoInDraw(d);
01906:     if (!counts.size) continue;
01907: 
01908:     totalSamples += 1;
01909: 
01910:     for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
01911:       const c = Number(counts.get(g) || 0);
01912:       if (c >= 2) {
01913:         freq.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) + 1);
01914:       }
01915:     }
01916:   }
01917: 
01918:   const prob = new Map();
01919:   const denom = Math.max(1, totalSamples);
01920: 
01921:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
01922:     prob.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) / denom);
01923:   }
01924: 
01925:   return { prob, freq, totalSamples };
01926: }

01929:   draws,

01933:   beforeTs,
01934:   count = 8
01935: ) {

01943:     .slice(-Math.max(1, Number(count || 8)));

01946: function computeRecentBichoMetrics(draws, groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
01947:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
01948:   const out = new Map();
01949: 
01950:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
01951:     out.set(g, {
01952:       recentPrizePresence: 0,
01953:       recentFirst: 0,
01954:       recentDupDraws: 0,
01955:       recentLast1PrizePresence: 0,
01956:       recentLast2PrizePresence: 0,
01957:       recentLast3PrizePresence: 0,
01958:     });
01959:   }
01960: 
01961:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
01962:   const last1 = list.length ? [list[list.length - 1]] : [];
01963:   const last2 = list.slice(-2);
01964:   const last3 = list.slice(-3);
01965: 
01966:   for (const d of list) {
01967:     const items = getAllPrizePresenceGroups(d);
01968:     const perDrawCounts = new Map();
01969: 
01970:     for (const it of items) {
01971:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
01972:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
01973: 
01974:       const row = out.get(g);
01975:       row.recentPrizePresence += 1;
01976: 
01977:       if (Number(it.pos) === 1) {
01978:         row.recentFirst += 1;
01979:       }
01980: 
01981:       perDrawCounts.set(g, Number(perDrawCounts.get(g) || 0) + 1);
01982:     }
01983: 
01984:     for (const [g, c] of perDrawCounts.entries()) {
01985:       if (Number(c) >= 2) {
01986:         out.get(Number(g)).recentDupDraws += 1;
01987:       }
01988:     }
01989:   }
01990: 
01991:   for (const d of last1) {
01992:     const seen = new Set();
01993:     for (const it of getAllPrizePresenceGroups(d)) {
01994:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
01995:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k || seen.has(g)) continue;
01996:       seen.add(g);
01997:       out.get(g).recentLast1PrizePresence += 1;
01998:     }
01999:   }
02000: 
02001:   for (const d of last2) {
02002:     const seen = new Set();
02003:     for (const it of getAllPrizePresenceGroups(d)) {
02004:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
02005:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k || seen.has(g)) continue;
02006:       seen.add(g);
02007:       out.get(g).recentLast2PrizePresence += 1;
02008:     }
02009:   }
02010: 
02011:   for (const d of last3) {
02012:     const seen = new Set();
02013:     for (const it of getAllPrizePresenceGroups(d)) {
02014:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
02015:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k || seen.has(g)) continue;
02016:       seen.add(g);
02017:       out.get(g).recentLast3PrizePresence += 1;
02018:     }
02019:   }
02020: 
02021:   return out;
02022: }
02023: 
02024: function getDayDrivenWeights(mode) {
02025:   if (mode === "REPEAT") {
02026:     return {
02027:       transition: 0.25,
02028:       pair: 0.25,
02029:       memory: 0.2,
02030:       recent: 0.15,
02031:       structural: 0.1,
02032:       late: 0.05,
02033:     };
02034:   }
02035: 
02036:   if (mode === "SPREAD") {
02037:     return {
02038:       transition: 0.2,
02039:       pair: 0.1,
02040:       memory: 0.1,
02041:       recent: 0.2,
02042:       structural: 0.3,
02043:       late: 0.1,
02044:     };
02045:   }
02046: 
02047:   return {
02048:     transition: 0.3,
02049:     pair: 0.15,
02050:     memory: 0.15,
02051:     recent: 0.15,
02052:     structural: 0.2,
02053:     late: 0.05,
02054:   };
02055: }
02056: 
02057: function normalizeWeights(weights) {
02058:   const safe = {
02059:     transition: Number(weights?.transition || 0),
02060:     pair: Number(weights?.pair || 0),
02061:     memory: Number(weights?.memory || 0),
02062:     recent: Number(weights?.recent || 0),
02063:     structural: Number(weights?.structural || 0),
02064:     late: Number(weights?.late || 0),
02065:   };
02066: 
02067:   const total = Object.values(safe).reduce((acc, n) => acc + n, 0);
02068:   if (!Number.isFinite(total) || total <= 0) {
02069:     return {
02070:       transition: 0.3,
02071:       pair: 0.15,
02072:       memory: 0.15,
02073:       recent: 0.15,
02074:       structural: 0.2,
02075:       late: 0.05,
02076:     };
02077:   }
02078: 
02079:   return {
02080:     transition: safe.transition / total,
02081:     pair: safe.pair / total,
02082:     memory: safe.memory / total,
02083:     recent: safe.recent / total,
02084:     structural: safe.structural / total,
02085:     late: safe.late / total,
02086:   };
02087: }
02088: 
02089: function computeComparableLastSeenByGrupo(
02090:   draws,
02091:   lotteryKey,
02092:   targetHour,
02093:   targetDow,
02094:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
02095: ) {
02096:   const list = getComparableDrawsForTargetContext(
02097:     draws,
02098:     lotteryKey,
02099:     targetHour,
02100:     targetDow
02101:   );
02102: 
02103:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
02104:   const last = new Map();
02105: 
02106:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
02107:     last.set(g, Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY);
02108:   }
02109: 
02110:   for (const d of list) {
02111:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
02112:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
02113:     const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
02114:     const items = getAllPrizePresenceGroups(d);
02115: 
02116:     for (const it of items) {
02117:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
02118:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
02119: 
02120:       const prev = Number(last.get(g));
02121:       if (!Number.isFinite(prev) || ts > prev) {
02122:         last.set(g, ts);
02123:       }
02124:     }
02125:   }
02126: 
02127:   return last;
02128: }
02129: 
02130: function buildDayContext(drawsToday = []) {
02131:   const list = sortDrawsAsc(Array.isArray(drawsToday) ? drawsToday : []);
02132:   const freq = new Map();
02133:   const firstFreq = new Map();
02134:   const seq = [];
02135: 
02136:   for (const d of list) {
02137:     const g = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(d);
02138:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(g)) || Number(g) < 1 || Number(g) > 25) continue;
02139: 
02140:     const gg = Number(g);
02141:     seq.push(gg);
02142:     freq.set(gg, Number(freq.get(gg) || 0) + 1);
02143: 
02144:     const items = getAllPrizePresenceGroups(d);
02145:     if (items.some((it) => Number(it.grupo) === gg && Number(it.pos) === 1)) {
02146:       firstFreq.set(gg, Number(firstFreq.get(gg) || 0) + 1);
02147:     }
02148:   }
02149: 
02150:   const total = seq.length;
02151:   const unique = new Set(seq).size;
02152:   const diversidade = total > 0 ? unique / total : 0;
02153: 
02154:   let dominante = null;
02155:   let dominanteCount = -1;
02156:   for (const [g, n] of freq.entries()) {
02157:     if (Number(n) > dominanteCount) {
02158:       dominante = Number(g);
02159:       dominanteCount = Number(n);
02160:     }
02161:   }
02162: 
02163:   const last1 = total >= 1 ? seq[total - 1] : null;
02164:   const last2 = total >= 2 ? seq[total - 2] : null;
02165:   const repeatNow =
02166:     Number.isFinite(Number(last1)) &&
02167:     Number.isFinite(Number(last2)) &&
02168:     Number(last1) === Number(last2);
02169: 
02170:   return {
02171:     total,
02172:     unique,
02173:     diversidade,
02174:     dominante,
02175:     dominanteCount: Math.max(0, dominanteCount),
02176:     repeatNow,
02177:     freq,
02178:     firstFreq,
02179:     seq,
02180:   };
02181: }
02182: 
02183: function buildPairSequenceDistribution({
02184:   drawsRange,
02185:   lotteryKey,
02186:   targetHour,
02187:   targetDow,
02188:   targetDayOfMonth,
02189:   prevPair,
02190:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
02191:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
02192:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
02193:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K,
02194: }) {
02195:   const seq = buildComparableFirstSequence(
02196:     Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? drawsRange : [],
02197:     lotteryKey,
02198:     targetHour,
02199:     targetDow
02200:   );
02201: 
02202:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
02203:   const outFreq = new Map();
02204: 
02205:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) outFreq.set(g, 0);
02206: 
02207:   const pair = Array.isArray(prevPair) ? prevPair : [];
02208:   if (pair.length < 2) {
02209:     return {
02210:       prob: buildZeroProbMap(k),
02211:       samples: 0,
02212:       weightedSamples: 0,
02213:       exactDomSamples: 0,
02214:       freq: outFreq,
02215:     };
02216:   }
02217: 
02218:   const pA = Number(pair[0]);
02219:   const pB = Number(pair[1]);
02220:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
02221: 
02222:   let matchedSamples = 0;
02223:   let weightedSamples = 0;
02224:   let exactDomSamples = 0;
02225: 
02226:   for (let i = 2; i < seq.length; i += 1) {
02227:     const a = Number(seq[i - 2]?.grupo);
02228:     const b = Number(seq[i - 1]?.grupo);
02229:     const g = Number(seq[i]?.grupo);
02230:     const y = safeStr(seq[i]?.ymd);
02231: 
02232:     if (!Number.isFinite(a) || !Number.isFinite(b) || !Number.isFinite(g)) continue;
02233:     if (a !== pA || b !== pB) continue;
02234:     if (!isYMD(y)) continue;
02235: 
02236:     matchedSamples += 1;
02237: 
02238:     let weight = 1;
02239: 
02240:     if (key !== "FEDERAL") {
02241:       const dom = Number(getDayOfMonth(y));
02242:       if (Number.isFinite(dom) && Number(dom) === Number(targetDayOfMonth)) {
02243:         weight = 1.35;
02244:         exactDomSamples += 1;
02245:       }
02246:     }
02247: 
02248:     weightedSamples += weight;
02249:     outFreq.set(g, Number(outFreq.get(g) || 0) + weight);
02250:   }
02251: 
02252:   const prob = new Map();
02253:   const denom = Math.max(1, weightedSamples);
02254: 
02255:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
02256:     prob.set(g, Number(outFreq.get(g) || 0) / denom);
02257:   }
02258: 
02259:   return {
02260:     prob,
02261:     samples: matchedSamples,
02262:     weightedSamples,
02263:     exactDomSamples,
02264:     freq: outFreq,
02265:   };
02266: }
02267: 
02268: function buildSeq2FirstDistribution(
02269:   comparableSeq,
02270:   currentState,
02271:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
02272: ) {
02273:   const seq = Array.isArray(comparableSeq) ? comparableSeq : [];
02274:   const state = Array.isArray(currentState) ? currentState : [];
02275:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
02276: 
02277:   const freq = new Map();
02278:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) freq.set(g, 0);
02279: 
02280:   if (state.length < 2) {
02281:     const prob = new Map();
02282:     for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) prob.set(g, 0);
02283:     return { prob, freq, samples: 0, prev2: null, prev1: null };
02284:   }
02285: 
02286:   const prev1 = Number(state[0]);
02287:   const prev2 = Number(state[1]);
02288: 
02289:   let samples = 0;
02290: 
02291:   for (let i = 2; i < seq.length; i += 1) {
02292:     const gA = Number(seq[i - 2]?.grupo);
02293:     const gB = Number(seq[i - 1]?.grupo);
02294:     const gN = Number(seq[i]?.grupo);
02295: 
02296:     if (!Number.isFinite(gA) || !Number.isFinite(gB) || !Number.isFinite(gN)) continue;
02297:     if (gA !== prev2 || gB !== prev1) continue;
02298:     if (gN < 1 || gN > k) continue;
02299: 
02300:     samples += 1;
02301:     freq.set(gN, Number(freq.get(gN) || 0) + 1);
02302:   }
02303: 
02304:   const prob = new Map();
02305:   const denom = Math.max(1, samples);
02306: 
02307:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
02308:     prob.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) / denom);
02309:   }
02310: 
02311:   return { prob, freq, samples, prev2, prev1 };
02312: }
02313: 
02314: function buildRecentRepeatBoostMap(
02315:   comparableSeq,
02316:   targetTs,
02317:   windowSize = 4,
02318:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
02319: ) {
02320:   const seq = getComparableRecentDrawsBeforeTs(
02321:     Array.isArray(comparableSeq) ? comparableSeq : [],
02322:     targetTs,
02323:     windowSize
02324:   );
02325: 
02326:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
02327:   const counts = new Map();
02328:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) counts.set(g, 0);
02329: 
02330:   for (const item of seq) {
02331:     const g = Number(item?.grupo);
02332:     if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
02333:     counts.set(g, Number(counts.get(g) || 0) + 1);
02334:   }
02335: 
02336:   const boost = new Map();
02337:   const maxCount = Math.max(1, ...Array.from(counts.values()).map((n) => Number(n || 0)));
02338: 
02339:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
02340:     const c = Number(counts.get(g) || 0);
02341:     boost.set(g, c >= 2 ? c / maxCount : 0);
02342:   }
02343: 
02344:   return {
02345:     boost,
02346:     counts,
02347:     windowSize,
02348:     considered: Array.isArray(seq) ? seq.length : 0,
02349:   };
02350: }
02351: 
02352: /* =========================
02353:    Motor principal V2 (FOCO = BICHO)
02354: ========================= */
02355: 
02356: export function computeConditionalNextTop3V2({
02357:   lotteryKey,
02358:   drawsRange,
02359:   drawLast,
02360:   drawsToday,
02361:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
02362:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
02363:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
02364:   topN = 3,
02365:   targetYmdOverride = "",
02366:   targetHourOverride = "",
02367: }) {
02368:   const list = Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? drawsRange : [];
02369:   if (!list.length || !drawLast) {
02370:     return { top: [], meta: null };
02371:   }
02372: 
02373:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
02374: 
02375:   const lastY = pickDrawYMD(drawLast);
02376:   const lastH = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(drawLast));
02377:   const prevGrupo = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(drawLast);
02378: 
02379:   if (
02380:     !isYMD(lastY) ||
02381:     !lastH ||
02382:     !Number.isFinite(Number(prevGrupo)) ||
02383:     Number(prevGrupo) <= 0
02384:   ) {
02385:     return { top: [], meta: null };
02386:   }
02387: 
02388:   const nextSlot = getNextSlotForLottery({
02389:     lotteryKey,
02390:     ymd: lastY,
02391:     hourBucket: lastH,
02392:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
02393:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
02394:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
02395:   });
02396: 
02397:   const forcedTargetY = safeStr(targetYmdOverride);
02398:   const forcedTargetH = toHourBucket(targetHourOverride);
02399: 
02400:   const targetY = isYMD(forcedTargetY) && forcedTargetH
02401:     ? forcedTargetY
02402:     : safeStr(nextSlot?.ymd);
02403: 
02404:   const targetH = isYMD(forcedTargetY) && forcedTargetH
02405:     ? forcedTargetH
02406:     : toHourBucket(nextSlot?.hour);
02407:   const targetDow = getDowKey(targetY);
02408:   const targetDayOfMonth = getDayOfMonth(targetY);
02409: 
02410:   if (!isYMD(targetY) || !targetH || !Number.isFinite(Number(targetDow))) {
02411:     return { top: [], meta: null };
02412:   }
02413: 
02414:   const targetTs = ymdHourToTs(targetY, targetH);
02415: 
02416:   const dayContext = buildDayContext(drawsToday);
02417:   const prevPair =
02418:     Array.isArray(dayContext?.seq) && dayContext.seq.length >= 2
02419:       ? dayContext.seq.slice(-2)
02420:       : [];
02421: 
02422:   const pairOut = buildPairSequenceDistribution({
02423:     drawsRange: list,
02424:     lotteryKey: key,
02425:     targetHour: targetH,
02426:     targetDow,
02427:     targetDayOfMonth,
02428:     prevPair,
02429:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
02430:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
02431:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
02432:     groupsK: TOP3_GROUPS_K,
02433:   });
02434: 
02435:   const pairConfidence = clamp01(
02436:     (confidenceFromSamples(pairOut?.samples || 0, 6) * 0.7) +
02437:     (confidenceFromSamples(pairOut?.exactDomSamples || 0, 3) * 0.3)
02438:   );
02439: 
02440:   const layerOut = buildConditionalLayerDistribution({
02441:     lotteryKey,
02442:     drawsRange: list,
02443:     targetHour: targetH,
02444:     targetDow,
02445:     targetDayOfMonth,
02446:     prevHour: lastH,
02447:     prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
02448:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
02449:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
02450:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
02451:   });
02452: 
02453:   const chosen = layerOut?.chosen || null;
02454:   const condSamples = Number(chosen?.samples || 0);
02455:   const condFirstFreq = chosen?.firstFreq || new Map();
02456: 
02457:   const pTransitionFirst = new Map();
02458:   for (let g = 1; g <= safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25); g += 1) {
02459:     pTransitionFirst.set(
02460:       g,
02461:       Number(condFirstFreq.get(g) || 0) / Math.max(1, condSamples)
02462:     );
02463:   }
02464: 
02465:   const structuralFirst = computeStructuralFirstDistribution(
02466:     list,
02467:     key,
02468:     targetH,
02469:     targetDow,
02470:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
02471:   );
02472:   const pStructuralFirst = structuralFirst?.prob || new Map();
02473: 
02474:   const structuralPrizePresence = computeComparablePrizePresenceDistribution(
02475:     list,
02476:     key,
02477:     targetH,
02478:     targetDow,
02479:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
02480:   );
02481:   const pStructuralPrizePresence = structuralPrizePresence?.prob || new Map();
02482: 
02483:   const duplication = computeComparableDuplicationDistribution(
02484:     list,
02485:     key,
02486:     targetH,
02487:     targetDow,
02488:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
02489:   );
02490:   const pDuplication = duplication?.prob || new Map();
02491: 
02492:   const comparableSeq = buildComparableFirstSequence(
02493:     list,
02494:     key,
02495:     targetH,
02496:     targetDow
02497:   );
02498: 
02499:   const currentState = getStateFromComparableSequenceBeforeTs(
02500:     comparableSeq,
02501:     targetTs,
02502:     3
02503:   );
02504: 
02505:   const memoryOut = buildMemoryStateDistribution({
02506:     drawsRange: list,
02507:     lotteryKey: key,
02508:     targetHour: targetH,
02509:     targetDow,
02510:     currentState,
02511:     groupsK: TOP3_GROUPS_K,
02512:   });
02513: 
02514:   const pMemory = memoryOut?.prob || new Map();
02515: 
02516:   const seq2Out = buildSeq2FirstDistribution(
02517:     comparableSeq,
02518:     currentState,
02519:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
02520:   );
02521:   const pSeq2 = seq2Out?.prob || new Map();
02522: 
02523:   const repeatBoostOut = buildRecentRepeatBoostMap(
02524:     comparableSeq,
02525:     targetTs,
02526:     4,
02527:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
02528:   );
02529:   const repeatBoostMap = repeatBoostOut?.boost || new Map();
02530: 
02531:   const regimeInfo = detectRegimeFromComparableSequence(
02532:     getComparableRecentDrawsBeforeTs(comparableSeq, targetTs, 6)
02533:   );
02534: 
02535:   const regime = safeStr(regimeInfo?.regime || "neutral");
02536: 
02537:   const mode =
02538:     dayContext.repeatNow || Number(dayContext?.diversidade || 0) < 0.5
02539:       ? "REPEAT"
02540:       : Number(dayContext?.diversidade || 0) > 0.75
02541:         ? "SPREAD"
02542:         : "NEUTRAL";
02543: 
02544:   const weights = getDayDrivenWeights(mode);
02545: 
02546:   const recentContextDraws = getComparableContextDrawsBeforeTs(
02547:     list,
02548:     key,
02549:     targetH,
02550:     targetDow,
02551:     targetTs,
02552:     8
02553:   );
02554:   const recentMetrics = computeRecentBichoMetrics(recentContextDraws, TOP3_GROUPS_K);
02555: 
02556:   const recentMaxPrizePresence = Math.max(
02557:     1,
02558:     ...Array.from(recentMetrics.values()).map((x) => Number(x.recentPrizePresence || 0))
02559:   );
02560:   const recentMaxFirst = Math.max(
02561:     1,
02562:     ...Array.from(recentMetrics.values()).map((x) => Number(x.recentFirst || 0))
02563:   );
02564:   const recentMaxDup = Math.max(
02565:     1,
02566:     ...Array.from(recentMetrics.values()).map((x) => Number(x.recentDupDraws || 0))
02567:   );
02568: 
02569:   const lateSeen = computeComparableLastSeenByGrupo(
02570:     list,
02571:     key,
02572:     targetH,
02573:     targetDow,
02574:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
02575:   );
02576: 
02577:   const finiteSeen = Array.from(lateSeen.values()).filter((x) =>
02578:     Number.isFinite(Number(x))
02579:   );
02580:   const minSeenTs = finiteSeen.length ? Math.min(...finiteSeen) : targetTs;
02581:   const maxGapMsBase =
02582:     Number.isFinite(targetTs) && Number.isFinite(minSeenTs)
02583:       ? Math.max(1, targetTs - minSeenTs)
02584:       : 1;
02585: 
02586:   const dayFlowConfidence = confidenceFromSamples(dayContext?.total || 0, 4);
02587:   const pairStrong = Number(pairOut?.samples || 0) >= 4;
02588: 
02589:   const confidence =
02590:     (pairConfidence * 0.3) +
02591:     (confidenceFromSamples(condSamples, 20) * 0.4) +
02592:     (confidenceFromSamples(memoryOut?.matchedSamples || 0, 15) * 0.3);
02593: 
02594:   const adjustedWeights = normalizeWeights({
02595:     transition: pairStrong ? weights.transition * 0.7 : weights.transition,
02596:     pair: pairStrong ? weights.pair * 1.4 : weights.pair,
02597:     memory: weights.memory,
02598:     recent: weights.recent,
02599:     structural: weights.structural,
02600:     late: weights.late,
02601:   });
02602: 
02603:   const ranked = Array.from({ length: safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25) }, (_, idx) => {
02604:     const grupo = idx + 1;
02605: 
02606:     const pT = Number(pTransitionFirst.get(grupo) || 0);
02607:     const pSF = Number(pStructuralFirst.get(grupo) || 0);
02608:     const pST = Number(pStructuralPrizePresence.get(grupo) || 0);
02609:     const pM = Number(pMemory.get(grupo) || 0);
02610:     const pD = Number(pDuplication.get(grupo) || 0);
02611:     const pSeq = Number(pSeq2.get(grupo) || 0);
02612:     const repeatBoost = Number(repeatBoostMap.get(grupo) || 0);
02613:     const pPair = Number(pairOut?.prob?.get?.(grupo) || 0);
02614: 
02615:     const rm = recentMetrics.get(grupo) || {
02616:       recentPrizePresence: 0,
02617:       recentFirst: 0,
02618:       recentDupDraws: 0,
02619:       recentLast1PrizePresence: 0,
02620:       recentLast2PrizePresence: 0,
02621:       recentLast3PrizePresence: 0,
02622:     };
02623: 
02624:     const recentPrizePresenceNorm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentPrizePresence, recentMaxPrizePresence);
02625:     const recentFirstNorm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentFirst, recentMaxFirst);
02626:     const recentDupNorm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentDupDraws, recentMaxDup);
02627:     const recentLast1Norm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentLast1PrizePresence, 1);
02628:     const recentLast2Norm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentLast2PrizePresence, 2);
02629: 
02630:     const recentComposite =
02631:       (recentFirstNorm * 0.52) +
02632:       (recentPrizePresenceNorm * 0.08) +
02633:       (recentDupNorm * 0.18) +
02634:       (recentLast2Norm * 0.14) +
02635:       (recentLast1Norm * 0.1);
02636: 
02637:     const dominanceScore =
02638:       (pT * 0.42) +
02639:       (pSF * 0.26) +
02640:       (pST * 0.18) +
02641:       (pD * 0.14);
02642: 
02643:     const seenTs = Number(lateSeen.get(grupo));
02644:     const gapMs =
02645:       Number.isFinite(targetTs) && Number.isFinite(seenTs)
02646:         ? Math.max(0, targetTs - seenTs)
02647:         : maxGapMsBase;
02648: 
02649:     const lateNorm = Math.max(
02650:       0,
02651:       Math.min(1, gapMs / Math.max(1, maxGapMsBase))
02652:     );
02653: 
02654:     const dayFreq = Number(dayContext?.freq?.get?.(grupo) || 0);
02655:     const dayFirstFreq = Number(dayContext?.firstFreq?.get?.(grupo) || 0);
02656:     const isDominantToday =
02657:       Number.isFinite(Number(dayContext?.dominante)) &&
02658:       Number(dayContext.dominante) === Number(grupo);
02659: 
02660:     let dayFlowRaw = 0;
02661: 
02662:     if (dayFreq >= 2) dayFlowRaw += 32;
02663:     if (dayFreq >= 3) dayFlowRaw += 18;
02664:     if (dayFirstFreq >= 1) dayFlowRaw += 20;
02665:     if (dayFirstFreq >= 2) dayFlowRaw += 14;
02666:     if (isDominantToday) dayFlowRaw += 24;
02667: 
02668:     if (Number(dayContext?.diversidade || 0) < 0.5) {
02669:       dayFlowRaw += dayFreq > 0 ? 12 : -8;
02670:     } else if (Number(dayContext?.diversidade || 0) > 0.75) {
02671:       dayFlowRaw += dayFreq === 0 ? 10 : 0;
02672:     }
02673: 
02674:     if (dayContext?.repeatNow && dayFreq > 0) {
02675:       dayFlowRaw += 10;
02676:     }
02677: 
02678:     const dayFlowScore = dayFlowRaw * dayFlowConfidence;
02679: 
02680:     const pairSequenceScore =
02681:       Number(pairOut?.samples || 0) > 0
02682:         ? ((pPair * 260) + Math.min(70, Number(pairOut.samples || 0) * 8)) * pairConfidence
02683:         : 0;
02684: 
02685:     const structuralBlend =
02686:       (pSF * 0.45) +
02687:       (pST * 0.35) +
02688:       (pD * 0.2);
02689: 
02690:     const memoryBlend =
02691:       (pM * 0.75) +
02692:       (pSeq * 0.25);
02693: 
02694:     const recentBlend =
02695:       (recentComposite * 0.85) +
02696:       (repeatBoost * 0.15);
02697: 
02698:     const prob =
02699:       (pT * adjustedWeights.transition) +
02700:       (pPair * adjustedWeights.pair * pairConfidence) +
02701:       (memoryBlend * adjustedWeights.memory) +
02702:       (recentBlend * adjustedWeights.recent) +
02703:       (structuralBlend * adjustedWeights.structural) +
02704:       (lateNorm * adjustedWeights.late);
02705: 
02706:     const score =
02707:       (prob * 1000) +
02708:       (dominanceScore * 180) +
02709:       (pairSequenceScore * 0.6) +
02710:       (dayFlowScore * 0.8) +
02711:       (confidence * 50);
02712: 
02713:     return {
02714:       grupo,
02715:       prob,
02716:       scoreProb: prob,
02717:       score,
02718:       probTransition: pT,
02719:       probStructuralFirst: pSF,
02720:       probStructuralPrizePresence: pST,
02721:       probMemory: pM,
02722:       probDuplication: pD,
02723:       probSeq2: pSeq,
02724:       probPair: pPair,
02725:       pairProb: pPair,
02726:       pairConfidence,
02727:       repeatBoost,
02728:       dominanceScore,
02729:       recentComposite,
02730:       recentPrizePresence: rm.recentPrizePresence,
02731:       recentFirst: rm.recentFirst,
02732:       recentDupDraws: rm.recentDupDraws,
02733:       recentLast1PrizePresence: rm.recentLast1PrizePresence,
02734:       recentLast2PrizePresence: rm.recentLast2PrizePresence,
02735:       recentLast3PrizePresence: rm.recentLast3PrizePresence,
02736:       lateNorm,
02737:       gapMs,
02738:       condFirstCount: Number(condFirstFreq.get(grupo) || 0),
02739:       structuralFirstCount: Number(structuralFirst?.firstFreq?.get(grupo) || 0),
02740:       structuralPrizePresenceCount: Number(structuralPrizePresence?.freq?.get(grupo) || 0),
02741:       duplicationCount: Number(duplication?.freq?.get(grupo) || 0),
02742:       memoryWeight: Number(memoryOut?.freq?.get(grupo) || 0),
02743:       dayFreq,
02744:       dayFirstFreq,
02745:       isDominantToday,
02746:       dayFlowConfidence,
02747:       dayFlowScore,
02748:       pairSamples: Number(pairOut?.samples || 0),
02749:       pairWeightedSamples: Number(pairOut?.weightedSamples || 0),
02750:       pairExactDomSamples: Number(pairOut?.exactDomSamples || 0),
02751:       pairCount: Number(pairOut?.freq?.get?.(grupo) || 0),
02752:       pairSequenceScore,
02753:     };
02754:   })
02755:     .sort((a, b) => {
02756:       if (b.score !== a.score) return b.score - a.score;
02757:       if (b.scoreProb !== a.scoreProb) return b.scoreProb - a.scoreProb;
02758:       if (b.dominanceScore !== a.dominanceScore) return b.dominanceScore - a.dominanceScore;
02759:       if (b.probTransition !== a.probTransition) return b.probTransition - a.probTransition;
02760:       if (b.probPair !== a.probPair) return b.probPair - a.probPair;
02761:       if (b.probDuplication !== a.probDuplication) return b.probDuplication - a.probDuplication;
02762:       if (b.probStructuralFirst !== a.probStructuralFirst) return b.probStructuralFirst - a.probStructuralFirst;
02763:       if (b.probStructuralPrizePresence !== a.probStructuralPrizePresence) return b.probStructuralPrizePresence - a.probStructuralPrizePresence;
02764:       return a.grupo - b.grupo;
02765:     })
02766:     .slice(0, Math.max(1, Number(topN || 3)));
02767: 
02768: 
02769: 
02770:   const reasonsBase = [
02771:     `Motor: V2_BICHO`,
02772:     `Regime detectado: ${regime}`,
02773:     `RepeatRate=${Number(regimeInfo?.repeatRate || 0).toFixed(2)} | UniqueRate=${Number(regimeInfo?.uniqueRate || 0).toFixed(2)}`,
02774:     `Pesos: mode=${mode} | transição=${(adjustedWeights.transition * 100).toFixed(0)}% | par=${(adjustedWeights.pair * 100).toFixed(0)}% | memória=${(adjustedWeights.memory * 100).toFixed(0)}% | recência=${(adjustedWeights.recent * 100).toFixed(0)}% | estrutural=${(adjustedWeights.structural * 100).toFixed(0)}% | atraso=${(adjustedWeights.late * 100).toFixed(0)}%`,
02775:     `Estado atual: prev=${String(prevGrupo).padStart(2, "0")} @ ${lastH} → alvo ${targetH}`,
02776:     `Estado curto comparável: [${currentState.map((g) => String(g).padStart(2, "0")).join(", ")}]`,
02777:     `Camada condicional: ${chosen?.label || "—"} | amostras=${condSamples}`,
02778:     `Amostra estrutural 1º=${Number(structuralFirst?.totalSamples || 0)} | PRIZE_PRESENCE=${Number(structuralPrizePresence?.totalSamples || 0)} | duplicação=${Number(duplication?.totalSamples || 0)}`,
02779:     `Amostra memória: matches=${Number(memoryOut?.matchedSamples || 0)} | peso=${Number(memoryOut?.totalWeight || 0).toFixed(2)}`,
02780:     `Par do dia: ${Array.isArray(prevPair) && prevPair.length === 2 ? prevPair.map((n) => String(n).padStart(2, "0")).join("→") : "--"} | matches=${Number(pairOut?.samples || 0)} | matches DOM=${Number(pairOut?.exactDomSamples || 0)} | confiança=${(pairConfidence * 100).toFixed(0)}%`,
02781:     `Fluxo do dia: draws=${Number(dayContext?.total || 0)} | confiança=${(dayFlowConfidence * 100).toFixed(0)}%`,
02782:     `Data alvo: ${targetY} | DOW=${targetDow} | dia=${String(targetDayOfMonth).padStart(2, "0")}`,
02783:   ];
02784: 
02785:   const top = ranked.map((x, idx) => {
02786:     const g2 = String(x.grupo).padStart(2, "0");
02787: 
02788:     return {
02789:       rank: idx + 1,
02790:       title:
02791:         idx === 0
02792:           ? "Mais provável"
02793:           : idx === 1
02794:             ? "2º mais provável"
02795:             : "3º mais provável",
02796:       grupo: x.grupo,
02797:       scoreProb: Number(x.scoreProb || 0),
02798:       probCond: Number(x.probTransition || 0),
02799:       probBase: Number(x.probStructuralFirst || 0),
02800:       lateBonus: Number(x.lateNorm || 0),
02801:       freq: x.condFirstCount,
02802:       freqCond: x.condFirstCount,
02803:       freqBase: x.structuralFirstCount,
02804:       freqZeroWhy:
02805:         x.condFirstCount <= 0
02806:           ? `Sem 1º lugar nesta camada (${chosen?.label || "—"}).`
02807:           : "",
02808:       reasons: [
02809:         ...reasonsBase,
02810:         `Grupo G${g2}: transição para 1º=${(x.probTransition * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
02811:         `Grupo G${g2}: estrutural de 1º=${(x.probStructuralFirst * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
02812:         `Grupo G${g2}: estrutural de TOP3=${(x.probStructuralPrizePresence * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
02813:         `Grupo G${g2}: duplicação histórica=${(x.probDuplication * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
02814:         `Grupo G${g2}: sequência ordem 2=${(x.probSeq2 * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
02815:         `Grupo G${g2}: par do dia=${(x.probPair * 100).toFixed(2)}% | confiança=${(x.pairConfidence * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
02816:         `Grupo G${g2}: boost de repetição=${(x.repeatBoost * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
02817:         `Grupo G${g2}: memória curta=${(x.probMemory * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
02818:         `Grupo G${g2}: recência composta=${(x.recentComposite * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
02819:         `Grupo G${g2}: atraso normalizado=${(x.lateNorm * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
02820:         `Grupo G${g2}: 1ºs na camada=${x.condFirstCount}`,
02821:         `Grupo G${g2}: 1ºs estruturais=${x.structuralFirstCount}`,
02822:         `Grupo G${g2}: TOP3 estruturais=${x.structuralPrizePresenceCount}`,
02823:         `Grupo G${g2}: draws com duplicação=${x.duplicationCount}`,
02824:         `Grupo G${g2}: peso de memória=${Number(x.memoryWeight || 0).toFixed(2)}`,
02825:         `Grupo G${g2}: frequência no dia=${x.dayFreq}`,
02826:         `Grupo G${g2}: 1ºs no dia=${x.dayFirstFreq}`,
02827:         `Grupo G${g2}: dominante no dia=${x.isDominantToday ? "sim" : "não"}`,
02828:         `Grupo G${g2}: score do fluxo do dia=${Number(x.dayFlowScore || 0).toFixed(2)} | confiança=${(Number(x.dayFlowConfidence || 0) * 100).toFixed(0)}%`,
02829:         `Grupo G${g2}: sequência ${Array.isArray(prevPair) && prevPair.length === 2 ? prevPair.map((n) => String(n).padStart(2, "0")).join("→") : "--"} | amostras=${x.pairSamples} | amostras ponderadas=${Number(x.pairWeightedSamples || 0).toFixed(2)} | DOM exato=${x.pairExactDomSamples} | ocorrências=${Number(x.pairCount || 0).toFixed(2)} | prob=${(Number(x.pairProb || 0) * 100).toFixed(2)}% | score=${Number(x.pairSequenceScore || 0).toFixed(2)}`,
02830:         `Probabilidade final BICHO=${(x.scoreProb * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
02831:       ],
02832:       meta: {
02833:         trigger: {
02834:           ymd: lastY,
02835:           hour: lastH,
02836:           grupo: Number(prevGrupo),
02837:         },
02838:         next: {
02839:           ymd: safeStr(targetY),
02840:           hour: safeStr(targetH),
02841:         },
02842:         samples: condSamples,
02843:         scenario: `V2_BICHO_${safeStr(regime).toUpperCase()}`,
02844:         explain: {
02845:           engine: "V2_BICHO",
02846:           mode,
02847:           regime,
02848:           repeatRate: Number(regimeInfo?.repeatRate || 0),
02849:           uniqueRate: Number(regimeInfo?.uniqueRate || 0),
02850:           weights: adjustedWeights,
02851:           layerKey: chosen?.key || "NONE",
02852:           layerLabel: chosen?.label || "—",
02853:           layerSamples: condSamples,
02854:           structuralFirstSamples: Number(structuralFirst?.totalSamples || 0),
02855:           structuralPrizePresenceSamples: Number(structuralPrizePresence?.totalSamples || 0),
02856:           duplicationSamples: Number(duplication?.totalSamples || 0),
02857:           memoryMatchedSamples: Number(memoryOut?.matchedSamples || 0),
02858:           memoryTotalWeight: Number(memoryOut?.totalWeight || 0),
02859:           seq2Samples: Number(seq2Out?.samples || 0),
02860:           seq2Prev2: Number(seq2Out?.prev2 || 0),
02861:           seq2Prev1: Number(seq2Out?.prev1 || 0),
02862:           pairSamples: Number(pairOut?.samples || 0),
02863:           pairWeightedSamples: Number(pairOut?.weightedSamples || 0),
02864:           pairExactDomSamples: Number(pairOut?.exactDomSamples || 0),
02865:           pairConfidence,
02866:           repeatWindowSize: Number(repeatBoostOut?.windowSize || 0),
02867:           repeatWindowConsidered: Number(repeatBoostOut?.considered || 0),
02868:           currentState,
02869:           targetDow,
02870:           targetDayOfMonth,
02871:           prevHour: lastH,
02872:           prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
02873:           dayContextTotal: Number(dayContext?.total || 0),
02874:           dayFlowConfidence,
02875:           dominanceScore: x.dominanceScore,
02876:           recentComposite: x.recentComposite,
02877:           recentPrizePresence: x.recentPrizePresence,
02878:           recentFirst: x.recentFirst,
02879:           recentDupDraws: x.recentDupDraws,
02880:           allLayers: (layerOut?.layers || []).map((layer) => ({
02881:             key: layer.key,
02882:             label: layer.label,
02883:             samples: layer.samples,
02884:             minSamples: layer.minSamples,
02885:           })),
02886:         },
02887:       },
02888:     };
02889:   });
02890: 
02891:   return {
02892:     top,
02893:     meta: {
02894:       trigger: {
02895:         ymd: lastY,
02896:         hour: lastH,
02897:         grupo: Number(prevGrupo),
02898:       },
02899:       next: {
02900:         ymd: safeStr(targetY),
02901:         hour: safeStr(targetH),
02902:       },
02903:       samples: condSamples,
02904:       scenario: `V2_BICHO_${safeStr(regime).toUpperCase()}`,
02905:       explain: {
02906:         engine: "V2_BICHO",
02907:         mode,
02908:         regime,
02909:         repeatRate: Number(regimeInfo?.repeatRate || 0),
02910:         uniqueRate: Number(regimeInfo?.uniqueRate || 0),
02911:         weights: adjustedWeights,
02912:         layerKey: chosen?.key || "NONE",
02913:         layerLabel: chosen?.label || "—",
02914:         layerSamples: condSamples,
02915:         structuralFirstSamples: Number(structuralFirst?.totalSamples || 0),
02916:         structuralPrizePresenceSamples: Number(structuralPrizePresence?.totalSamples || 0),
02917:         duplicationSamples: Number(duplication?.totalSamples || 0),
02918:         memoryMatchedSamples: Number(memoryOut?.matchedSamples || 0),
02919:         memoryTotalWeight: Number(memoryOut?.totalWeight || 0),
02920:         pairSamples: Number(pairOut?.samples || 0),
02921:         pairWeightedSamples: Number(pairOut?.weightedSamples || 0),
02922:         pairExactDomSamples: Number(pairOut?.exactDomSamples || 0),
02923:         pairConfidence,
02924:         currentState,
02925:         targetDow,
02926:         targetDayOfMonth,
02927:         prevHour: lastH,
02928:         prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
02929:         dayContextTotal: Number(dayContext?.total || 0),
02930:         dayFlowConfidence,
02931:         allLayers: (layerOut?.layers || []).map((layer) => ({
02932:           key: layer.key,
02933:           label: layer.label,
02934:           samples: layer.samples,
02935:           minSamples: layer.minSamples,
02936:         })),
02937:       },
02938:     },
02939:   };
02940: }
02941: 
02942: 
02943: /* =========================
02944:    Motor principal V3 (ESTATISTICO AUDITAVEL)
02945: ========================= */
02946: 
02947: function incMap(map, key, value = 1) {
02948:   const k = Number(key);
02949:   if (!Number.isFinite(k) || k < 1 || k > TOP3_GROUPS_K) return;
02950:   map.set(k, Number(map.get(k) || 0) + Number(value || 0));
02951: }
02952: 
02953: function emptyGroupMap() {
02954:   const out = new Map();
02955:   for (let g = 1; g <= safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25); g += 1) out.set(g, 0);
02956:   return out;
02957: }
02958: 
02959: function getPrizePresenceGroupSet(draw) {
02960:   return new Set(
02961:     getAllPrizePresenceGroups(draw)
02962:       .map((x) => Number(x.grupo))
02963:       .filter((g) => Number.isFinite(g) && g >= 1 && g <= TOP3_GROUPS_K)
02964:   );
02965: }
02966: 
02967: function layerProbability(freqMap, samples, alpha = TOP3_SMOOTH_ALPHA) {
02968:   const out = new Map();
02969:   const k = safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25);
02970:   const a = Math.max(0, Number(alpha || 0));
02971:   const base = Math.max(0, Number(samples || 0));
02972:   const denom = Math.max(1, base + (a * k));
02973: 
02974:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
02975:     const count = Math.max(0, Number(freqMap?.get?.(g) || 0));
02976:     out.set(g, (count + a) / denom);
02977:   }
02978: 
02979:   return out;
02980: }
02981: 
02982: function sampleConfidence(samples, fullAt = 30) {
02983:   const n = Number(samples || 0);
02984:   const lim = Math.max(1, Number(fullAt || 30));
02985:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, n / lim));
02986: }
02987: 
02988: export function computeStatisticalTop3V3({
02989:   lotteryKey,
02990:   drawsRange,
02991:   drawLast,
02992:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
02993:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
02994:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
02995:   topN = 3,
02996:   targetYmdOverride = "",
02997:   targetHourOverride = "",
02998:   drawsAlreadySorted = false,
02999: }) {
03000:   const sourceList =
03001:     Array.isArray(drawsRange)
03002:       ? drawsRange
03003:       : [];
03004: 
03005:   const list = drawsAlreadySorted
03006:     ? sourceList
03007:     : sortDrawsAsc(sourceList);
03008:   if (!list.length || !drawLast) return { top: [], meta: null };
03009: 
03010:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
03011: 
03012:   const lastY = pickDrawYMD(drawLast);
03013:   const lastH = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(drawLast));
03014:   const prevGrupo = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(drawLast);
03015: 
03016:   if (
03017:     !isYMD(lastY) ||
03018:     !lastH ||
03019:     !Number.isFinite(Number(prevGrupo)) ||
03020:     Number(prevGrupo) < 1 ||
03021:     Number(prevGrupo) > TOP3_GROUPS_K
03022:   ) {
03023:     return { top: [], meta: null };
03024:   }
03025: 
03026:   const nextSlot = getNextSlotForLottery({
03027:     lotteryKey: key,
03028:     ymd: lastY,
03029:     hourBucket: lastH,
03030:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
03031:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
03032:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
03033:   });
03034: 
03035:   const forcedTargetY = safeStr(targetYmdOverride);
03036:   const forcedTargetH = toHourBucket(targetHourOverride);
03037: 
03038:   const targetY = isYMD(forcedTargetY) && forcedTargetH
03039:     ? forcedTargetY
03040:     : safeStr(nextSlot?.ymd);
03041: 
03042:   const targetH = isYMD(forcedTargetY) && forcedTargetH
03043:     ? forcedTargetH
03044:     : toHourBucket(nextSlot?.hour);
03045:   const targetDow = getDowKey(targetY);
03046:   const targetDayOfMonth = getDayOfMonth(targetY);
03047:   const targetTs = ymdHourToTs(targetY, targetH);
03048: 
03049:   if (!isYMD(targetY) || !targetH || !Number.isFinite(Number(targetDow))) {
03050:     return { top: [], meta: null };
03051:   }
03052: 
03053:   const history = list.filter((d) => {
03054:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
03055:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
03056:     const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
03057:     return isYMD(y) && h && Number.isFinite(ts) && ts < targetTs;
03058:   });
03059: 
03060:   const layers = {
03061:     hour: { label: `frequência no horário ${targetH}`, samples: 0, first: emptyGroupMap(), prizePresence: emptyGroupMap(), weight: 0.33 },
03062:     dowHour: { label: `frequência no dia da semana + horário`, samples: 0, first: emptyGroupMap(), prizePresence: emptyGroupMap(), weight: 0.27 },
03063:     dayMonth: { label: `frequência no dia ${String(targetDayOfMonth).padStart(2, "0")}`, samples: 0, first: emptyGroupMap(), prizePresence: emptyGroupMap(), weight: 0.04 },
03064:     transition: { label: `transição G${String(prevGrupo).padStart(2, "0")} @ ${lastH} → ${targetH}`, samples: 0, first: emptyGroupMap(), prizePresence: emptyGroupMap(), weight: 0.26 },
03065:     recent: { label: `recência comparável`, samples: 0, first: emptyGroupMap(), prizePresence: emptyGroupMap(), weight: 0.10 },
03066:   };
03067: 
03068:   for (const d of history) {
03069:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
03070:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
03071:     const dow = getDowKey(y);
03072:     const dom = getDayOfMonth(y);
03073:     const g1 = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(d);
03074:     const prizePresenceSet = getPrizePresenceGroupSet(d);
03075: 
03076:     const addToLayer = (layer) => {
03077:       layer.samples += 1;
03078:       incMap(layer.first, g1, 1);
03079:       for (const g of prizePresenceSet) incMap(layer.prizePresence, g, 1);
03080:     };
03081: 
03082:     if (h === targetH) addToLayer(layers.hour);
03083:     if (h === targetH && Number(dow) === Number(targetDow)) addToLayer(layers.dowHour);
03084:     if (Number(dom) === Number(targetDayOfMonth)) addToLayer(layers.dayMonth);
03085:   }
03086: 
03087:   const indexed = indexDrawsByYmdHour(history);
03088: 
03089:   for (const prev of history) {
03090:     const py = pickDrawYMD(prev);
03091:     const ph = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(prev));
03092:     const pg = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(prev);
03093: 
03094:     if (!isYMD(py) || !ph || Number(pg) !== Number(prevGrupo)) continue;
03095: 
03096:     const ns = getNextSlotForLottery({
03097:       lotteryKey: key,
03098:       ymd: py,
03099:       hourBucket: ph,
03100:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
03101:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
03102:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
03103:     });
03104: 
03105:     if (toHourBucket(ns?.hour) !== targetH) continue;
03106:     if (ph !== lastH) continue;
03107: 
03108:     const nextDraw = indexed.get(`${safeStr(ns?.ymd)}|${toHourBucket(ns?.hour)}`) || null;
03109:     if (!nextDraw) continue;
03110: 
03111:     const g1 = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(nextDraw);
03112:     const prizePresenceSet = getPrizePresenceGroupSet(nextDraw);
03113: 
03114:     layers.transition.samples += 1;
03115:     incMap(layers.transition.first, g1, 1);
03116:     for (const g of prizePresenceSet) incMap(layers.transition.prizePresence, g, 1);
03117:   }
03118: 
03119:   const recentComparable = history
03120:     .filter((d) => {
03121:       const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
03122:       const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
03123:       return h === targetH && Number(getDowKey(y)) === Number(targetDow);
03124:     })
03125:     .slice(-20);
03126: 
03127:   for (const d of recentComparable) {
03128:     const g1 = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(d);
03129:     const prizePresenceSet = getPrizePresenceGroupSet(d);
03130: 
03131:     layers.recent.samples += 1;
03132:     incMap(layers.recent.first, g1, 1);
03133:     for (const g of prizePresenceSet) incMap(layers.recent.prizePresence, g, 1);
03134:   }
03135: 
03136:   const currentScene = buildSceneFromDraw(drawLast);
03137:   const sceneRanking = buildHistoricalSceneRanking(history, currentScene, 80);
03138:   const sceneHypothesis = buildSceneHypothesisDistribution(sceneRanking, TOP3_GROUPS_K);
03139:   const sceneWeight =
03140:     sampleConfidence(sceneHypothesis?.samples || 0, TOP3_SCENE_SAMPLE_TARGET) *
03141:     TOP3_SCENE_WEIGHT;
03142: 
03143: 
03144:   const activeWeights = {};
03145:   let totalWeight = 0;
03146: 
03147:   for (const [keyLayer, layer] of Object.entries(layers)) {
03148:     const conf =
03149:       keyLayer === "transition"
03150:         ? sampleConfidence(layer.samples, 8)
03151:         : keyLayer === "recent"
03152:           ? sampleConfidence(layer.samples, 12)
03153:           : sampleConfidence(layer.samples, 30);
03154: 
03155:     const w = layer.samples > 0 ? layer.weight * conf : 0;
03156:     activeWeights[keyLayer] = w;
03157:     totalWeight += w;
03158:   }
03159: 
03160:   if (totalWeight <= 0) {
03161:     activeWeights.hour = 1;
03162:     totalWeight = 1;
03163:   }
03164: 
03165:   for (const keyLayer of Object.keys(activeWeights)) {
03166:     activeWeights[keyLayer] = activeWeights[keyLayer] / totalWeight;
03167:   }
03168: 
03169:   const layerProbabilities = Object.fromEntries(
03170:     Object.entries(layers).map(([keyLayer, layer]) => [
03171:       keyLayer,
03172:       {
03173:         first: layerProbability(layer.first, layer.samples),
03174:         prizePresence: layerProbability(layer.prizePresence, layer.samples),
03175:       },
03176:     ])
03177:   );
03178: 
03179:   const ranked = Array.from({ length: safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25) }, (_, idx) => {
03180:     const grupo = idx + 1;
03181: 
03182:     let scoreProb = 0;
03183:     const details = {};
03184: 
03185:     for (const [keyLayer, layer] of Object.entries(layers)) {
03186:       const probabilities = layerProbabilities[keyLayer];
03187: 
03188:       const pFirst = Number(probabilities.first.get(grupo) || 0);
03189:       const pPrizePresence = Number(probabilities.prizePresence.get(grupo) || 0);
03190: 
03191:       const pLayer = (pFirst * 0.92) + (pPrizePresence * 0.08);
03192:       const w = Number(activeWeights[keyLayer] || 0);
03193: 
03194:       scoreProb += pLayer * w;
03195: 
03196:       details[keyLayer] = {
03197:         label: layer.label,
03198:         samples: layer.samples,
03199:         firstCount: Number(layer.first.get(grupo) || 0),
03200:         top3Count: Number(layer.prizePresence.get(grupo) || 0),
03201:         probability: pLayer,
03202:         weight: w,
03203:       };
03204:     }
03205: 
03206:     const pSceneRaw = Number(sceneHypothesis?.prob?.get?.(grupo) || 0);
03207:     const pScene =
03208:       (pSceneRaw * TOP3_SCENE_BLEND_SCENE) +
03209:       ((1 / TOP3_GROUPS_K) * TOP3_SCENE_BLEND_UNIFORM);
03210: 
03211:     if (sceneWeight > 0) {
03212:       scoreProb = (scoreProb * (1 - sceneWeight)) + (pScene * sceneWeight);
03213:     }
03214: 
03215:     details.scene = {
03216:       label: "analogia histórica de cena",
03217:       samples: Number(sceneHypothesis?.samples || 0),
03218:       firstCount: Number(sceneHypothesis?.freq?.get?.(grupo) || 0),
03219:       top3Count: 0,
03220:       probability: pScene,
03221:       weight: sceneWeight,
03222:     };
03223: 
03224:     return {
03225:       grupo,
03226:       scoreProb,
03227:       score: scoreProb * 1000,
03228:       details,
03229:     };
03230:   })
03231:     .sort((a, b) => {
03232:       if (b.score !== a.score) return b.score - a.score;
03233:       return a.grupo - b.grupo;
03234:     })
03235:     .slice(0, Math.max(1, Number(topN || 3)));
03236: 
03237:   const rankedScored = scoreRanking(
03238:     ranked,
03239:     {
03240:       lotteryKey,
03241:       targetYmd: targetY,
03242:       targetHour: targetH,
03243:       previousGroup: prevGrupo,
03244:       totalDraws: history.length,
03245:     }
03246:   );
03247: 
03248:   const top = rankedScored.map((x, idx) => {
03249:     const g2 = String(x.grupo).padStart(2, "0");
03250:     const rawScoreProb = Number(x.scoreProb || 0);
03251: 
03252:     const strongest = Object.values(x.details)
03253:       .filter((d) => Number(d.probability || 0) > 0)
