03254:       .sort((a, b) => (b.probability * b.weight) - (a.probability * a.weight))
03255:       .slice(0, 3);
03256: 
03257:     return {
03258:       rank: idx + 1,
03259:       title:
03260:         idx === 0
03261:           ? "Mais provável"
03262:           : idx === 1
03263:             ? "2º mais provável"
03264:             : "3º mais provável",
03265:       grupo: x.grupo,
03266:       scoreProb: Number(rawScoreProb || 0),
03267:       rawScoreProb: Number(rawScoreProb || 0),
03268:       probCond: Number(x.details.transition?.probability || 0),
03269:       probBase: Number(x.details.hour?.probability || 0),
03270:       lateBonus: 0,
03271:       freq: Number(x.details.hour?.firstCount || 0),
03272:       freqCond: Number(x.details.transition?.firstCount || 0),
03273:       freqBase: Number(x.details.hour?.firstCount || 0),
03274:       freqZeroWhy: "",
03275:       reasons: [
03276:         `Motor: V3_STATISTICAL`,
03277:         `Alvo: ${targetY} ${targetH} | DOW=${targetDow} | dia=${String(targetDayOfMonth).padStart(2, "0")}`,
03278:         `Base: G${String(prevGrupo).padStart(2, "0")} @ ${lastH}`,
03279:         `Probabilidade final G${g2}: ${(Number(rawScoreProb || 0) * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
03280:         ...strongest.map((d) =>
03281:           `${d.label}: ${d.firstCount}x em 1º | ${d.top3Count}x no TOP3 | amostras=${d.samples} | peso=${(d.weight * 100).toFixed(0)}%`
03282:         ),
03283:       ],
03284:       meta: {
03285:         trigger: {
03286:           ymd: lastY,
03287:           hour: lastH,
03288:           grupo: Number(prevGrupo),
03289:         },
03290:         next: {
03291:           ymd: safeStr(targetY),
03292:           hour: safeStr(targetH),
03293:         },
03294:         samples: history.length,
03295:         period: {
03296:           from: pickDrawYMD(history[0]) || "",
03297:           to: pickDrawYMD(history[history.length - 1]) || "",
03298:         },
03299:         scenario: "V3_STATISTICAL",
03300:         explain: {
03301:           engine: "V3_STATISTICAL",
03302:           targetDow,
03303:           targetDayOfMonth,
03304:           prevHour: lastH,
03305:           prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
03306:           activeWeights,
03307:           scene: {
03308:             samples: Number(sceneHypothesis?.samples || 0),
03309:             totalWeight: Number(sceneHypothesis?.totalWeight || 0),
03310:             weight: Number(sceneWeight || 0),
03311:             currentSignature: currentScene?.signature || "",
03312:           },
03313:           layers: Object.fromEntries(
03314:             Object.entries(layers).map(([k, v]) => [
03315:               k,
03316:               {
03317:                 label: v.label,
03318:                 samples: v.samples,
03319:                 weight: activeWeights[k],
03320:               },
03321:             ])
03322:           ),
03323:           details: x.details,
03324:         },
03325:       },
03326:     };
03327:   });
03328: 
03329:   return {
03330:     top,
03331:     meta: {
03332:       trigger: {
03333:         ymd: lastY,
03334:         hour: lastH,
03335:         grupo: Number(prevGrupo),
03336:       },
03337:       next: {
03338:         ymd: safeStr(targetY),
03339:         hour: safeStr(targetH),
03340:       },
03341:       samples: history.length,
03342:       scenario: "V3_STATISTICAL",
03343:       explain: {
03344:         engine: "V3_STATISTICAL",
03345:         targetDow,
03346:         targetDayOfMonth,
03347:         prevHour: lastH,
03348:         prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
03349:         activeWeights,
03350:         layers: Object.fromEntries(
03351:           Object.entries(layers).map(([k, v]) => [
03352:             k,
03353:             {
03354:               label: v.label,
03355:               samples: v.samples,
03356:               weight: activeWeights[k],
03357:             },
03358:           ])
03359:         ),
03360:       },
03361:     },
03362:   };
03363: }
03364: 
03365: 
03366: 
03367: /* =========================
03368:    TOP3 SCENE READER
03369: ========================= */
03370: 
03371: function scenePickMilhar4(prize) {
03372:   const candidates = [
03373:     prize?.milhar,
03374:     prize?.milhar4,
03375:     prize?.numero,
03376:     prize?.number,
03377:     prize?.value,
03378:     prize?.result,
03379:     prize?.resultado,
03380:     prize?.premio,
03381:   ];
03382: 
03383:   for (const v of candidates) {
03384:     const dig = String(v ?? "").replace(/\D+/g, "");
03385:     if (dig) return dig.length >= 4 ? dig.slice(-4) : dig.padStart(4, "0");
03386:   }
03387: 
03388:   return "";
03389: }
03390: 
03391: function sceneGrupoFromMilhar4(m4) {
03392:   const s = String(m4 || "").replace(/\D+/g, "").padStart(4, "0").slice(-4);
03393:   if (!/^\d{4}$/.test(s)) return null;
03394: 
03395:   const dzRaw = Number(s.slice(-2));
03396:   if (!Number.isFinite(dzRaw)) return null;
03397: 
03398:   const dz = dzRaw === 0 ? 100 : dzRaw;
03399:   const grupo = Math.ceil(dz / 4);
03400: 
03401:   return grupo >= 1 && grupo <= 25 ? grupo : null;
03402: }
03403: 
03404: function buildSceneFromDraw(draw) {
03405:   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
03406: 
03407:   const rows = prizes
03408:     .map((p) => {
03409:       const pos = Number(guessPrizePos(p));
03410:       const milhar = scenePickMilhar4(p);
03411:       const grupo = sceneGrupoFromMilhar4(milhar);
03412: 
03413:       return {
03414:         pos,
03415:         milhar: String(milhar || "").replace(/\D+/g, "").padStart(4, "0").slice(-4),
03416:         centena: getCentena3(milhar),
03417:         dezena: getDezena2(milhar),
03418:         grupo,
03419:       };
03420:     })
03421:     .filter((x) => Number.isFinite(x.pos) && x.pos >= 1 && x.pos <= 7)
03422:     .sort((a, b) => a.pos - b.pos);
03423: 
03424:   const grupos = rows.map((x) => x.grupo).filter((x) => Number.isFinite(x));
03425:   const dezenas = rows.map((x) => x.dezena).filter(Boolean);
03426:   const centenas = rows.map((x) => x.centena).filter(Boolean);
03427:   const milhares = rows.map((x) => x.milhar).filter(Boolean);
03428: 
03429:   const countMap = (arr) => {
03430:     const m = new Map();
03431:     for (const x of arr) m.set(String(x), (m.get(String(x)) || 0) + 1);
03432:     return Array.from(m.entries())
03433:       .filter(([, n]) => n > 1)
03434:       .map(([value, count]) => ({ value, count }));
03435:   };
03436: 
03437:   return {
03438:     ymd: pickDrawYMD(draw),
03439:     hour: toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw)),
03440:     firstGrupo: grupos[0] ?? null,
03441:     grupos,
03442:     dezenas,
03443:     centenas,
03444:     milhares,
03445:     repeatedGroups: countMap(grupos),
03446:     repeatedDezenas: countMap(dezenas),
03447:     repeatedCentenas: countMap(centenas),
03448:     rows,
03449:     signature: grupos.map((g) => String(g).padStart(2, "0")).join("-"),
03450:   };
03451: }
03452: 
03453: 
03454: 
03455: function compareScenes(a, b, explain = false) {
03456:   if (!a || !b) return explain ? { score: 0, details: {} } : 0;
03457: 
03458:   let score = 0;
03459:   const details = {};
03460: 
03461:   const add = (key, value) => {
03462:     if (!value) return;
03463:     score += value;
03464:     details[key] = value;
03465:   };
03466: 
03467:   const ag = Array.isArray(a.grupos) ? a.grupos : [];
03468:   const bg = Array.isArray(b.grupos) ? b.grupos : [];
03469: 
03470:   const ad = Array.isArray(a.dezenas) ? a.dezenas : [];
03471:   const bd = Array.isArray(b.dezenas) ? b.dezenas : [];
03472: 
03473:   const ac = Array.isArray(a.centenas) ? a.centenas : [];
03474:   const bc = Array.isArray(b.centenas) ? b.centenas : [];
03475: 
03476:   if (Number(a.firstGrupo) === Number(b.firstGrupo))
03477:     add("firstGrupo",45);
03478: 
03479:   for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(ag.length,bg.length,7); i++) {
03480:     if (Number(ag[i]) === Number(bg[i]))
03481:       add("order_"+i, i===0 ? 20 : 10);
03482:   }
03483: 
03484:   add(
03485:     "groupOverlap",
03486:     new Set(ag.filter(g => bg.includes(g))).size * 6
03487:   );
03488: 
03489:   add(
03490:     "dezenaOverlap",
03491:     new Set(ad.filter(d => bd.includes(d))).size * 3
03492:   );
03493: 
03494:   add(
03495:     "centenaOverlap",
03496:     new Set(ac.filter(c => bc.includes(c))).size * 1.5
03497:   );
03498: 
03499:   const repA = Array.isArray(a.repeatedGroups) ? a.repeatedGroups.length : 0;
03500:   const repB = Array.isArray(b.repeatedGroups) ? b.repeatedGroups.length : 0;
03501: 
03502:   if (repA > 0 && repB > 0)
03503:     add("repeatExists",8);
03504: 
03505:   if (repA === repB && repA > 0)
03506:     add("repeatEqual",6);
03507: 
03508:   if (String(a.signature||"") === String(b.signature||""))
03509:     add("signature",60);
03510: 
03511:   if (a.hour === b.hour)
03512:     add("hour",8);
03513: 
03514:   return explain ? { score, details } : score;
03515: }
03516: 
03517: 
03518: 
03519: 
03520: function buildHistoricalSceneRanking(draws, currentScene, limit = 100) {
03521:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
03522:   if (!currentScene) return [];
03523: 
03524:   const ordered = list
03525:     .map((draw) => ({
03526:       draw,
03527:       scene: buildSceneFromDraw(draw),
03528:     }))
03529:     .filter((x) => x.scene)
03530:     .sort((a, b) => {
03531:       const ta = ymdHourToTs(a.scene?.ymd, a.scene?.hour);
03532:       const tb = ymdHourToTs(b.scene?.ymd, b.scene?.hour);
03533:       return Number(ta || 0) - Number(tb || 0);
03534:     });
03535: 
03536:   return ordered
03537:     .map((item, idx) => {
03538:       const next = ordered[idx + 1] || null;
03539:       const score = compareScenes(currentScene, item.scene);
03540: 
03541:       return {
03542:         draw: item.draw,
03543:         scene: item.scene,
03544:         nextDraw: next?.draw || null,
03545:         nextScene: next?.scene || null,
03546:         score,
03547:       };
03548:     })
03549:     .filter((x) => x.scene && x.nextDraw && Number(x.score) > 0)
03550:     .sort((a, b) => {
03551:       if (Number(b.score) !== Number(a.score)) {
03552:         return Number(b.score) - Number(a.score);
03553:       }
03554: 
03555:       const ta = ymdHourToTs(a.scene?.ymd, a.scene?.hour);
03556:       const tb = ymdHourToTs(b.scene?.ymd, b.scene?.hour);
03557:       return Number(tb || 0) - Number(ta || 0);
03558:     })
03559:     .slice(0, Math.max(1, Number(limit || 100)));
03560: }
03561: 
03562: 
03563: 
03564: 
03565: function buildSceneHypothesisDistribution(sceneRanking, groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
03566:   const ranking = Array.isArray(sceneRanking) ? sceneRanking : [];
03567:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
03568: 
03569:   const freq = new Map();
03570:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) freq.set(g, 0);
03571: 
03572:   let totalWeight = 0;
03573: 
03574:   for (const item of ranking) {
03575:     const score = Math.max(0, Number(item?.score || 0));
03576:     const nextGrupo = Number(item?.nextDraw ? pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(item.nextDraw) : null);
03577: 
03578:     if (!Number.isFinite(nextGrupo) || nextGrupo < 1 || nextGrupo > k) continue;
03579:     if (score <= 0) continue;
03580: 
03581:     freq.set(nextGrupo, Number(freq.get(nextGrupo) || 0) + score);
03582:     totalWeight += score;
03583:   }
03584: 
03585:   const prob = new Map();
03586:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
03587:     prob.set(g, totalWeight > 0 ? Number(freq.get(g) || 0) / totalWeight : 0);
03588:   }
03589: 
03590:   return {
03591:     freq,
03592:     prob,
03593:     samples: ranking.length,
03594:     totalWeight,
03595:   };
03596: }
03597: 
03598: 
03599: /* =========================
03600:    16/20 milhares (por grupo) — POR TERMINAÇÃO (CORRETO)
03601: ========================= */
03602: 
03603: function getDezenasFixasFromGrupo(grupo2) {
03604:   const g = Number(grupo2);
03605:   if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > 25) return [];
03606: 
03607:   const start = (g - 1) * 4 + 1;
03608:   const out = [];
03609: 
03610:   for (let i = 0; i < 4; i += 1) {
03611:     out.push(wrapToDezena2(start + i));
03612:   }
03613: 
03614:   return out.filter(Boolean);
03615: }
03616: 
03617: 
03618: export function buildMilharesForGrupo({
03619:   rangeDraws,
03620:   analysisHourBucket,
03621:   schedule,
03622:   grupo2,
03623:   count = 20,
03624:   targetYmd = "",
03625: }) {
03626:   const list = Array.isArray(rangeDraws) ? rangeDraws : [];
03627:   const target = toHourBucket(analysisHourBucket);
03628:   const schSet = scheduleSet(schedule);
03629:   const targetDay = safeStr(targetYmd);
03630:   const targetDow = isYMD(targetDay) ? getDowKey(targetDay) : null;
03631: 
03632:   const N = Number.isFinite(Number(count))
03633:     ? Math.max(4, Math.trunc(Number(count)))
03634:     : 20;
03635: 
03636:   const grupoNum = Number(grupo2);
03637: 
03638:   if (!Number.isFinite(grupoNum) || grupoNum < 1 || grupoNum > 25 || !list.length) {
03639:     return { dezenas: [], slots: [] };
03640:   }
03641: 
03642:   const dezenasFixas = getDezenasFixasFromGrupo(grupoNum);
03643:   if (!dezenasFixas.length) return { dezenas: [], slots: [] };
03644: 
03645:   const perDezena = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(N / dezenasFixas.length));
03646:   const byMilhar = new Map();
03647: 
03648:   for (const d of list) {
03649:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
03650:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
03651:     if (!h) continue;
03652: 
03653:     const isTargetHour = target && h === target;
03654:     const isScheduleHour = schSet.size && schSet.has(h);
03655:     const isSameDow = targetDow !== null && Number(getDowKey(y)) === Number(targetDow);
03656:     const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
03657: 
03658:     const ps = Array.isArray(d?.prizes) ? d.prizes : [];
03659: 
03660:     for (const prize of ps) {
03661:       const pos = Number(guessPrizePos(prize));
03662:       if (!Number.isFinite(pos) || pos < 1 || pos > 7) continue;
03663: 
03664:       const g = Number(guessPrizeGrupo(prize));
03665:       if (g !== grupoNum) continue;
03666: 
03667:       const m4 = String(pickPrizeMilhar4(prize) || "").replace(/\D+/g, "").padStart(4, "0").slice(-4);
03668:       if (!/^\d{4}$/.test(m4)) continue;
03669: 
03670:       const dz = getDezena2(m4);
03671:       const c3 = getCentena3(m4);
03672: 
03673:       if (!dz || !c3 || !dezenasFixas.includes(dz)) continue;
03674: 
03675:       const current = byMilhar.get(m4) || {
03676:         milhar: m4,
03677:         dezena: dz,
03678:         centena: c3,
03679:         freq: 0,
03680:         score: 0,
03681:         targetHits: 0,
03682:         scheduleHits: 0,
03683:         sameDowHits: 0,
03684:         firstPrizeHits: 0,
03685:         lastTs: 0,
03686:       };
03687: 
03688:       let inc = 1;
03689: 
03690:       if (isTargetHour) {
03691:         inc += 8;
03692:         current.targetHits += 1;
03693:       }
03694: 
03695:       if (isScheduleHour) {
03696:         inc += 3;
03697:         current.scheduleHits += 1;
03698:       }
03699: 
03700:       if (isSameDow) {
03701:         inc += 2;
03702:         current.sameDowHits += 1;
03703:       }
03704: 
03705:       if (pos === 1) {
03706:         inc += 3;
03707:         current.firstPrizeHits += 1;
03708:       } else if (pos <= 3) {
03709:         inc += 1;
03710:       }
03711: 
03712:       current.freq += 1;
03713:       current.score += inc;
03714: 
03715:       if (Number.isFinite(ts)) {
03716:         current.lastTs = Math.max(Number(current.lastTs || 0), ts);
03717:       }
03718: 
03719:       byMilhar.set(m4, current);
03720:     }
03721:   }
03722: 
03723:   const ranked = Array.from(byMilhar.values())
03724:     .map((m) => {
03725:       const freq = Number(m.freq || 0);
03726:       const targetHits = Number(m.targetHits || 0);
03727:       const scheduleHits = Number(m.scheduleHits || 0);
03728:       const sameDowHits = Number(m.sameDowHits || 0);
03729:       const firstPrizeHits = Number(m.firstPrizeHits || 0);
03730:       const recencyBoost = Number(m.lastTs || 0) > 0 ? 1 : 0;
03731: 
03732:       return {
03733:         ...m,
03734:         score:
03735:           freq * 100 +
03736:           targetHits * 70 +
03737:           scheduleHits * 35 +
03738:           sameDowHits * 25 +
03739:           firstPrizeHits * 45 +
03740:           recencyBoost * 5,
03741:       };
03742:     })
03743:     .sort((a, b) => {
03744:       if (Number(b.score) !== Number(a.score)) return Number(b.score) - Number(a.score);
03745:       if (Number(b.targetHits) !== Number(a.targetHits)) return Number(b.targetHits) - Number(a.targetHits);
03746:       if (Number(b.freq) !== Number(a.freq)) return Number(b.freq) - Number(a.freq);
03747:       if (Number(b.lastTs) !== Number(a.lastTs)) return Number(b.lastTs) - Number(a.lastTs);
03748:       return milharCompareAsc(a.milhar, b.milhar);
03749:     });
03750: 
03751:   const centenaStats = new Map();
03752: 
03753:   for (const item of ranked) {
03754:     const centena = String(item?.centena || "").trim();
03755:     if (!centena) continue;
03756: 
03757:     const current = centenaStats.get(centena) || {
03758:       centena,
03759:       freq: 0,
03760:       score: 0,
03761:       targetHits: 0,
03762:       sameDowHits: 0,
03763:       firstPrizeHits: 0,
03764:       lastTs: 0,
03765:     };
03766: 
03767:     current.freq += Number(item.freq || 0);
03768:     current.score += Number(item.score || 0);
03769:     current.targetHits += Number(item.targetHits || 0);
03770:     current.sameDowHits += Number(item.sameDowHits || 0);
03771:     current.firstPrizeHits += Number(item.firstPrizeHits || 0);
03772:     current.lastTs = Math.max(Number(current.lastTs || 0), Number(item.lastTs || 0));
03773: 
03774:     centenaStats.set(centena, current);
03775:   }
03776: 
03777:   const centenaScoreMap = new Map();
03778: 
03779:   for (const c of centenaStats.values()) {
03780:     centenaScoreMap.set(
03781:       c.centena,
03782:       Number(c.score || 0) +
03783:         Number(c.freq || 0) * 25 +
03784:         Number(c.targetHits || 0) * 60 +
03785:         Number(c.sameDowHits || 0) * 30 +
03786:         Number(c.firstPrizeHits || 0) * 40 +
03787:         (Number(c.lastTs || 0) > 0 ? 10 : 0)
03788:     );
03789:   }
03790: 
03791:   /*
03792:    * Contexto compartilhado do seletor de milhar.
03793:    *
03794:    * O Top3 continua escolhendo:
03795:    * - grupo;
03796:    * - dezenas;
03797:    * - centenas.
03798:    *
03799:    * O chooseBestMilhar escolhe somente o prefixo vencedor
03800:    * para cada centena selecionada.
03801:    */
03802:   const fallbackMilharPrizes = [];
03803:   const primaryMilharPrizes = [];
03804: 
03805:   for (const draw of list) {
03806:     const drawYmd = pickDrawYMD(draw);
03807:     const drawHour = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
03808:     const drawDow =
03809:       isYMD(drawYmd)
03810:         ? getDowKey(drawYmd)
03811:         : null;
03812: 
03813:     const matchesTargetHour =
03814:       Boolean(target) &&
03815:       drawHour === target;
03816: 
03817:     const matchesTargetDow =
03818:       targetDow === null ||
03819:       Number(drawDow) === Number(targetDow);
03820: 
03821:     const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes)
03822:       ? draw.prizes
03823:       : [];
03824: 
03825:     for (const prize of prizes) {
03826:       const position = Number(guessPrizePos(prize));
03827:       const prizeGroup = Number(guessPrizeGrupo(prize));
03828: 
03829:       if (
03830:         !Number.isFinite(position) ||
03831:         position < 1 ||
03832:         position > 7 ||
03833:         prizeGroup !== grupoNum
03834:       ) {
03835:         continue;
03836:       }
03837: 
03838:       fallbackMilharPrizes.push(prize);
03839: 
03840:       if (matchesTargetHour && matchesTargetDow) {
03841:         primaryMilharPrizes.push(prize);
03842:       }
03843:     }
03844:   }
03845: 
03846:   const usedMilhares = new Set();
03847:   const slots = [];
03848: 
03849:   function pickDiversifiedMilharesForDezena(dz, limit) {
03850:     const candidates = ranked
03851:       .filter((item) => item.dezena === dz)
03852:       .map((item) => {
03853:         const centena = String(
03854:           item?.centena || ""
03855:         )
03856:           .replace(/\D+/g, "")
03857:           .padStart(3, "0")
03858:           .slice(-3);
03859: 
03860:         return {
03861:           ...item,
03862:           centena,
03863:           centenaScore: Number(
03864:             centenaScoreMap.get(centena) || 0
03865:           ),
03866:         };
03867:       })
03868:       .filter((item) => /^\d{3}$/.test(item.centena));
03869: 
03870:     const byCentena = new Map();
03871: 
03872:     for (const item of candidates) {
03873:       const current = byCentena.get(item.centena);
03874: 
03875:       if (
03876:         !current ||
03877:         Number(item.centenaScore || 0) >
03878:           Number(current.centenaScore || 0) ||
03879:         (
03880:           Number(item.centenaScore || 0) ===
03881:             Number(current.centenaScore || 0) &&
03882:           Number(item.score || 0) >
03883:             Number(current.score || 0)
03884:         )
03885:       ) {
03886:         byCentena.set(item.centena, item);
03887:       }
03888:     }
03889: 
03890:     const strongestCentenas = Array.from(
03891:       byCentena.values()
03892:     ).sort((a, b) => {
03893:       if (
03894:         Number(b.centenaScore || 0) !==
03895:         Number(a.centenaScore || 0)
03896:       ) {
03897:         return (
03898:           Number(b.centenaScore || 0) -
03899:           Number(a.centenaScore || 0)
03900:         );
03901:       }
03902: 
03903:       if (
03904:         Number(b.score || 0) !==
03905:         Number(a.score || 0)
03906:       ) {
03907:         return (
03908:           Number(b.score || 0) -
03909:           Number(a.score || 0)
03910:         );
03911:       }
03912: 
03913:       if (
03914:         Number(b.targetHits || 0) !==
03915:         Number(a.targetHits || 0)
03916:       ) {
03917:         return (
03918:           Number(b.targetHits || 0) -
03919:           Number(a.targetHits || 0)
03920:         );
03921:       }
03922: 
03923:       if (
03924:         Number(b.freq || 0) !==
03925:         Number(a.freq || 0)
03926:       ) {
03927:         return (
03928:           Number(b.freq || 0) -
03929:           Number(a.freq || 0)
03930:         );
03931:       }
03932: 
03933:       return String(a.centena).localeCompare(
03934:         String(b.centena)
03935:       );
03936:     });
03937: 
03938:     const picked = [];
03939: 
03940:     for (const centenaItem of strongestCentenas) {
03941:       if (picked.length >= limit) break;
03942: 
03943:       const sharedResult = chooseBestMilhar({
03944:         centena: centenaItem.centena,
03945:         prizes: primaryMilharPrizes,
03946:         fallbackPrizes: fallbackMilharPrizes,
03947:       });
03948: 
03949:       const sharedWinner =
03950:         sharedResult?.winner || null;
03951: 
03952:       const selectedMilhar = String(
03953:         sharedWinner?.milhar || ""
03954:       )
03955:         .replace(/\D+/g, "")
03956:         .padStart(4, "0")
03957:         .slice(-4);
03958: 
03959:       if (!/^\d{4}$/.test(selectedMilhar)) {
03960:         continue;
03961:       }
03962: 
03963:       if (
03964:         selectedMilhar.slice(-3) !==
03965:         centenaItem.centena
03966:       ) {
03967:         continue;
03968:       }
03969: 
03970:       if (
03971:         getDezena2(selectedMilhar) !== dz ||
03972:         usedMilhares.has(selectedMilhar) ||
03973:         picked.some(
03974:           (item) =>
03975:             item.milhar === selectedMilhar
03976:         )
03977:       ) {
03978:         continue;
03979:       }
03980: 
03981:       picked.push({
03982:         ...centenaItem,
03983:         milhar: selectedMilhar,
03984:         prefix:
03985:           sharedWinner?.prefix ??
03986:           selectedMilhar.slice(0, 1),
03987:         adjustedScore: Number(
03988:           sharedWinner?.score ||
03989:           centenaItem.score ||
03990:           0
03991:         ),
03992:         sharedMilharModel:
03993:           sharedResult?.model ||
03994:           "MILHAR_PROBABILITY_V2",
03995:         sharedMilharSampleSize: Number(
03996:           sharedResult?.sampleSize || 0
03997:         ),
03998:       });
03999:     }
04000: 
04001:     return picked.slice(0, limit);
04002:   }
04003: 
04004:   for (const dz of dezenasFixas) {
04005:     const items = pickDiversifiedMilharesForDezena(dz, perDezena);
04006: 
04007:     let pushed = 0;
04008: 
04009:     for (const item of items) {
04010:       usedMilhares.add(item.milhar);
04011:       slots.push({
04012:         dezena: dz,
04013:         milhar: item.milhar,
04014:         score: item.score,
04015:         adjustedScore: item.adjustedScore,
04016:         centena: item.centena,
04017:         centenaScore: item.centenaScore,
04018:         freq: item.freq,
04019:         targetHits: item.targetHits,
04020:         scheduleHits: item.scheduleHits,
04021:         sameDowHits: item.sameDowHits,
04022:         firstPrizeHits: item.firstPrizeHits,
04023:       });
04024:       pushed += 1;
04025:     }
04026: 
04027:     while (pushed < perDezena) {
04028:       slots.push({ dezena: dz, milhar: "" });
04029:       pushed += 1;
04030:     }
04031:   }
04032: 
04033:   while (slots.length < N) slots.push({ dezena: "", milhar: "" });
04034: 
04035:   return { dezenas: dezenasFixas, slots: slots.slice(0, N) };
04036: }
04037: 
04038: export function build16MilharesForGrupo(args) {
04039:   return buildMilharesForGrupo({ ...(args || {}), count: 16 });
04040: }
04041: 
04042: export function build20MilharesForGrupo(args) {
04043:   return buildMilharesForGrupo({ ...(args || {}), count: 20 });
04044: }
04045: 
04046: /* =========================
04047:    TIMELINE DO DIA (CADEIA PREDITIVA)
04048: ========================= */
04049: 
04050: 
04051: 
04052: export function auditTop3Timeline({
04053:   timeline,
04054:   lotteryKey = "",
04055: }) {
04056:   const rows = Array.isArray(timeline) ? timeline : [];
04057: 
04058:   const validated = rows.filter((slot) => {
04059:     const status = String(slot?.status || "").toLowerCase();
04060: 
04061:     return (
04062:       status === "validated" &&
04063:       Number.isFinite(Number(slot?.resultGrupo))
04064:     );
04065:   });
04066: 
04067:   function pct(n, d) {
04068:     if (!d) return 0;
04069: 
04070:     return Number(
04071:       ((Number(n || 0) / Number(d || 1)) * 100).toFixed(2)
04072:     );
04073:   }
04074: 
04075:   function normalizeResultTop3(slot) {
04076:     const source = Array.isArray(slot?.resultTop3Groups)
04077:       ? slot.resultTop3Groups
04078:       : [];
04079: 
04080:     const normalized = source
04081:       .slice(0, 3)
04082:       .map((grupo) => {
04083:         const value = Number(grupo);
04084: 
04085:         return Number.isFinite(value) &&
04086:           value >= 1 &&
04087:           value <= TOP3_GROUPS_K
04088:           ? value
04089:           : null;
04090:       });
04091: 
04092:     while (normalized.length < 3) {
04093:       normalized.push(null);
04094:     }
04095: 
04096:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(normalized[0]))) {
04097:       const fallback = Number(slot?.resultGrupo);
04098: 
04099:       normalized[0] =
04100:         Number.isFinite(fallback) &&
04101:         fallback >= 1 &&
04102:         fallback <= TOP3_GROUPS_K
04103:           ? fallback
04104:           : null;
04105:     }
04106: 
04107:     return normalized;
04108:   }
04109: 
04110:   function evaluateSlot(slot) {
04111:     const picks = (Array.isArray(slot?.top3) ? slot.top3 : [])
04112:       .slice(0, 3)
04113:       .map((item) => Number(item?.grupo))
04114:       .filter(
04115:         (grupo) =>
04116:           Number.isFinite(grupo) &&
04117:           grupo >= 1 &&
04118:           grupo <= TOP3_GROUPS_K
04119:       );
04120: 
04121:     const resultTop3 = normalizeResultTop3(slot);
04122:     const resultGrupo = Number(resultTop3[0]);
04123: 
04124:     const top1Hit =
04125:       picks.length > 0 &&
04126:       Number.isFinite(resultGrupo) &&
04127:       Number(picks[0]) === resultGrupo;
04128: 
04129:     const top3Hit =
04130:       Number.isFinite(resultGrupo) &&
04131:       picks.some(
04132:         (grupo) => Number(grupo) === resultGrupo
04133:       );
04134: 
04135:     const prizePositionHits = resultTop3.map(
04136:       (grupo) =>
04137:         Number.isFinite(Number(grupo)) &&
04138:         picks.some(
04139:           (pick) => Number(pick) === Number(grupo)
04140:         )
04141:     );
04142: 
04143:     const predictionHits = picks.map((grupo) =>
04144:       resultTop3.some(
04145:         (result) =>
04146:           Number.isFinite(Number(result)) &&
04147:           Number(result) === Number(grupo)
04148:       )
04149:     );
04150: 
04151:     const matchedPrizePositions =
04152:       prizePositionHits.filter(Boolean).length;
04153: 
04154:     const matchedPredictions =
04155:       predictionHits.filter(Boolean).length;
04156: 
04157:     return {
04158:       picks,
04159:       resultTop3,
04160:       resultGrupo,
04161: 
04162:       top1Hit,
04163:       top3Hit,
04164: 
04165:       prize1Hit: Boolean(prizePositionHits[0]),
04166:       prize2Hit: Boolean(prizePositionHits[1]),
04167:       prize3Hit: Boolean(prizePositionHits[2]),
04168: 
04169:       top3PrizeHit: matchedPrizePositions > 0,
04170: 
04171:       matchedPrizePositions,
04172:       matchedPredictions,
04173: 
04174:       prizePositionHits,
04175:       predictionHits,
04176:     };
04177:   }
04178: 
04179:   const evaluated = validated.map((slot) => ({
04180:     slot,
04181:     metrics: evaluateSlot(slot),
04182:   }));
04183: 
04184:   function summarize(items) {
04185:     const summary = {
04186:       total: items.length,
04187: 
04188:       top1Hits: 0,
04189:       top3Hits: 0,
04190: 
04191:       prize1Hits: 0,
04192:       prize2Hits: 0,
04193:       prize3Hits: 0,
04194: 
04195:       top3PrizeHits: 0,
04196: 
04197:       matchedPrizePositions: 0,
04198:       matchedPredictions: 0,
04199:     };
04200: 
04201:     for (const item of items) {
04202:       const metrics = item.metrics;
04203: 
04204:       if (metrics.top1Hit) summary.top1Hits += 1;
04205:       if (metrics.top3Hit) summary.top3Hits += 1;
04206: 
04207:       if (metrics.prize1Hit) summary.prize1Hits += 1;
04208:       if (metrics.prize2Hit) summary.prize2Hits += 1;
04209:       if (metrics.prize3Hit) summary.prize3Hits += 1;
04210: 
04211:       if (metrics.top3PrizeHit) {
04212:         summary.top3PrizeHits += 1;
04213:       }
04214: 
04215:       summary.matchedPrizePositions +=
04216:         Number(metrics.matchedPrizePositions || 0);
04217: 
04218:       summary.matchedPredictions +=
04219:         Number(metrics.matchedPredictions || 0);
04220:     }
04221: 
04222:     return {
04223:       ...summary,
04224: 
04225:       top1Rate: pct(summary.top1Hits, summary.total),
04226:       top3Rate: pct(summary.top3Hits, summary.total),
04227: 
04228:       prize1Rate: pct(summary.prize1Hits, summary.total),
04229:       prize2Rate: pct(summary.prize2Hits, summary.total),
04230:       prize3Rate: pct(summary.prize3Hits, summary.total),
04231: 
04232:       top3PrizeRate: pct(
04233:         summary.top3PrizeHits,
04234:         summary.total
04235:       ),
04236: 
04237:       averageMatchedPrizePositions: summary.total
04238:         ? Number(
04239:             (
04240:               summary.matchedPrizePositions /
04241:               summary.total
04242:             ).toFixed(4)
04243:           )
04244:         : 0,
04245: 
04246:       averageMatchedPredictions: summary.total
04247:         ? Number(
04248:             (
04249:               summary.matchedPredictions /
04250:               summary.total
04251:             ).toFixed(4)
04252:           )
04253:         : 0,
04254:     };
04255:   }
04256: 
04257:   function groupBy(keyFn) {
04258:     const map = new Map();
04259: 
04260:     for (const item of evaluated) {
04261:       const key = keyFn(item.slot);
04262: 
04263:       if (!map.has(key)) {
04264:         map.set(key, []);
04265:       }
04266: 
04267:       map.get(key).push(item);
04268:     }
04269: 
04270:     return Array.from(map.entries())
04271:       .map(([key, items]) => ({
04272:         key,
04273:         ...summarize(items),
04274:       }))
04275:       .sort((a, b) => {
04276:         if (b.top3PrizeRate !== a.top3PrizeRate) {
04277:           return b.top3PrizeRate - a.top3PrizeRate;
04278:         }
04279: 
04280:         if (b.top3Rate !== a.top3Rate) {
04281:           return b.top3Rate - a.top3Rate;
04282:         }
04283: 
04284:         return b.total - a.total;
04285:       });
04286:   }
04287: 
04288:   const summary = summarize(evaluated);
04289: 
04290:   return {
04291:     lotteryKey,
04292:     ...summary,
04293: 
04294:     byHour: groupBy(
04295:       (slot) => String(slot?.targetHour || "")
04296:     ),
04297: 
04298:     byDate: groupBy(
04299:       (slot) => String(slot?.targetYmd || "")
04300:     ),
04301: 
04302:     rows: evaluated.map(({ slot, metrics }) => ({
04303:       ymd: slot.targetYmd,
04304:       hour: slot.targetHour,
04305: 
04306:       baseYmd: slot.baseYmd,
04307:       baseHour: slot.baseHour,
04308: 
04309:       resultGrupo: metrics.resultGrupo,
04310:       resultTop3: metrics.resultTop3,
04311: 
04312:       top3: metrics.picks,
04313: 
04314:       top1Hit: metrics.top1Hit,
04315:       top3Hit: metrics.top3Hit,
04316: 
04317:       prize1Hit: metrics.prize1Hit,
04318:       prize2Hit: metrics.prize2Hit,
04319:       prize3Hit: metrics.prize3Hit,
04320: 
04321:       top3PrizeHit: metrics.top3PrizeHit,
04322: 
04323:       matchedPrizePositions:
04324:         metrics.matchedPrizePositions,
04325: 
04326:       matchedPredictions:
04327:         metrics.matchedPredictions,
04328: 
04329:       prizePositionHits:
04330:         metrics.prizePositionHits,
04331: 
04332:       predictionHits:
04333:         metrics.predictionHits,
04334: 
04335:       historyStats: slot.historyStats || null,
04336:     })),
04337:   };
04338: }
04339: 
04340: 
04341: function buildTimelineForDate({
04342:   ymd,
04343:   drawsToday,
04344:   drawsRange,
04345:   lotteryKey,
04346:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
04347:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
04348:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
04349: }) {
04350:   const targetYmd = safeStr(ymd);
04351:   const day = Array.isArray(drawsToday) ? [...drawsToday] : [];
04352:   const range = Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? [...drawsRange] : [];
04353: 
04354:   if (!isYMD(targetYmd) || !range.length) return [];
04355: 
04356:   const schedule = getScheduleForLottery({
04357:     lotteryKey,
04358:     ymd: targetYmd,
04359:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
04360:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
04361:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
04362:   });
04363: 
04364:   const normalizedSchedule = (Array.isArray(schedule) ? schedule : [])
04365:     .map(toHourBucket)
04366:     .filter(Boolean);
04367: 
04368:   if (!normalizedSchedule.length) return [];
04369: 
04370:   const daySorted = day
04371:     .filter((d) => isYMD(pickDrawYMD(d)) && toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d)))
04372:     .sort((a, b) => {
04373:       const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
04374:       const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
04375:       return ta - tb;
04376:     });
04377: 
04378:   const rangeSorted = range
04379:     .filter((d) => isYMD(pickDrawYMD(d)) && toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d)))
04380:     .sort((a, b) => {
04381:       const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
04382:       const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
04383:       return ta - tb;
04384:     });
04385: 
04386:   const realizedTodayMap = new Map();
04387: 
04388:   for (const d of daySorted) {
04389:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
04390:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
04391:     if (!isYMD(y) || !h) continue;
04392:     if (y !== targetYmd) continue;
04393:     realizedTodayMap.set(`${y}|${h}`, d);
04394:   }
04395: 
04396: 
04397:   const chainContextDraws = [];
04398:   const timeline = [];
04399:   let pendingPredictionAlreadyShown = false;
04400: 
04401:   for (const slotHour of normalizedSchedule) {
04402:     const currentDraw = realizedTodayMap.get(`${targetYmd}|${slotHour}`) || null;
04403:     const slotTs = ymdHourToTs(targetYmd, slotHour);
04404: 
04405: 
04406:     if (!currentDraw && pendingPredictionAlreadyShown) {
04407:       timeline.push({
04408:         targetYmd,
04409:         targetHour: slotHour,
04410:         baseYmd: "",
04411:         baseHour: "",
04412:         top3: [],
04413:         resultGrupo: null,
04414:         hit: null,
04415:         status: "pending",
04416:       });
04417:       continue;
04418:     }
04419: 
04420:     let baseDraw = null;
04421: 
04422:     const priorChainDraw = [...chainContextDraws]
04423:       .filter((d) => {
04424:         const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
04425:         const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
04426:         const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
04427:         return isYMD(y) && h && Number.isFinite(ts) && ts < slotTs;
04428:       })
04429:       .sort((a, b) => {
04430:         const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
04431:         const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
04432:         return tb - ta;
04433:       })[0] || null;
04434: 
04435:     if (priorChainDraw) baseDraw = priorChainDraw;
04436: 
04437:     if (!baseDraw) {
04438:       const prevSameDay = daySorted
04439:         .filter((d) => {
04440:           const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
04441:           const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
04442:           const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
04443:           return y === targetYmd && h && Number.isFinite(ts) && ts < slotTs;
04444:         })
04445:         .sort((a, b) => {
04446:           const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
04447:           const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
04448:           return tb - ta;
04449:         })[0] || null;
04450: 
04451:       if (prevSameDay) baseDraw = prevSameDay;
04452:     }
04453: 
04454:     if (!baseDraw) {
04455:       for (let i = rangeSorted.length - 1; i >= 0; i -= 1) {
04456:         const d = rangeSorted[i];
04457:         const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
04458:         const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
04459:         const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
04460: 
04461:         if (!Number.isFinite(ts) || ts >= slotTs) continue;
04462: 
04463:         baseDraw = d;
04464:         break;
04465:       }
04466:     }
04467: 
04468:     if (!baseDraw) {
04469:       baseDraw = findPreviousValidDraw(rangeSorted, targetYmd, slotHour);
04470:     }
04471: 
04472:     if (!baseDraw) {
04473:       timeline.push({
04474:         targetYmd,
04475:         targetHour: slotHour,
04476:         baseYmd: "",
04477:         baseHour: "",
04478:         top3: [],
04479:         resultGrupo: null,
04480:         hit: null,
04481:         status: "pending",
04482:       });
04483:       continue;
04484:     }
04485: 
04486:     const baseTs = ymdHourToTs(
04487:       pickDrawYMD(baseDraw),
04488:       toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(baseDraw))
04489:     );
04490: 
04491:     const usableHistory = rangeSorted.filter((d) => {
04492:       const ts = ymdHourToTs(
04493:         pickDrawYMD(d),
04494:         toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d))
04495:       );
04496:       return Number.isFinite(ts) && ts <= baseTs;
04497:     });
04498: 
04499:     const historyStats = (() => {
04500:       const MS_DAY = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
04501: 
04502:       let d30 = 0;
04503:       let d90 = 0;
04504:       let d180 = 0;
04505:       let d365 = 0;
04506: 
04507:       for (const historicalDraw of usableHistory) {
04508:         const historicalTs = ymdHourToTs(
04509:           pickDrawYMD(historicalDraw),
04510:           toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(historicalDraw))
04511:         );
04512: 
04513:         if (!Number.isFinite(historicalTs)) continue;
04514: 
04515:         const diffDays = (baseTs - historicalTs) / MS_DAY;
04516: 
04517:         if (diffDays >= 0 && diffDays <= 30) d30 += 1;
04518:         if (diffDays >= 0 && diffDays <= 90) d90 += 1;
04519:         if (diffDays >= 0 && diffDays <= 180) d180 += 1;
04520:         if (diffDays >= 0 && diffDays <= 365) d365 += 1;
04521:       }
04522: 
04523:       return {
04524:         total: usableHistory.length,
04525:         d30,
04526:         d90,
04527:         d180,
04528:         d365,
04529:       };
04530:     })();
04531: 
04532:     const usableTodayContext = [...chainContextDraws]
04533:       .filter((d) => {
04534:         const ts = ymdHourToTs(
04535:           pickDrawYMD(d),
04536:           toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d))
04537:         );
04538:         return Number.isFinite(ts) && ts <= baseTs;
04539:       })
04540:       .sort((a, b) => {
04541:         const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
04542:         const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
04543:         return ta - tb;
04544:       });
04545: 
04546:     const computed = computeStatisticalTop3V3({
04547:       lotteryKey,
04548:       drawsRange: [...usableHistory, ...usableTodayContext],
04549:       drawLast: baseDraw,
04550:       drawsToday: usableTodayContext,
04551:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
04552:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
04553:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
04554:       topN: 3,
04555:       targetYmdOverride: targetYmd,
04556:       targetHourOverride: slotHour,
04557:     });
04558: 
04559: 
04560:     const computedNextYmd = safeStr(computed?.meta?.next?.ymd || "");
04561:     const computedNextHour = toHourBucket(computed?.meta?.next?.hour || "");
04562:     const computedForSlot =
04563:       computedNextYmd === targetYmd && computedNextHour === slotHour;
04564: 
04565:     const top3 =
04566:       computedForSlot && Array.isArray(computed?.top)
04567:         ? computed.top.slice(0, 3)
04568:         : [];
04569: 
04570:     const resultTop3Groups = currentDraw
04571:       ? getPrizeGroupsByPosition(currentDraw, 3)
04572:       : [null, null, null];
04573: 
04574:     const resultGrupo = Number(resultTop3Groups[0]);
04575: 
04576:     const normalizedResultGrupo =
04577:       Number.isFinite(resultGrupo) &&
04578:       resultGrupo >= 1 &&
04579:       resultGrupo <= TOP3_GROUPS_K
04580:         ? resultGrupo
04581:         : null;
04582: 
04583:     const hitPrizePositions = resultTop3Groups.map(
04584:       (resultGroup) =>
04585:         Number.isFinite(Number(resultGroup)) &&
04586:         top3.some(
04587:           (item) =>
04588:             Number(item?.grupo) ===
04589:             Number(resultGroup)
04590:         )
04591:     );
04592: 
04593:     const matchedPrizePositions =
04594:       hitPrizePositions.filter(Boolean).length;
04595: 
04596:     const hit =
04597:       top3.length > 0 &&
04598:       resultTop3Groups.some(
04599:         (resultGroup) =>
04600:           Number.isFinite(Number(resultGroup))
04601:       )
04602:         ? matchedPrizePositions > 0
04603:         : null;
04604: 
04605:     timeline.push({
04606:       targetYmd,
04607:       targetHour: slotHour,
04608: 
04609:       baseYmd: pickDrawYMD(baseDraw) || "",
04610:       baseHour: toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(baseDraw)) || "",
04611: 
04612:       top3,
04613: 
04614:       resultGrupo: normalizedResultGrupo,
04615:       resultTop3Groups,
04616: 
04617:       hit,
04618:       top3PrizeHit: hit,
04619:       hitPrizePositions,
04620:       matchedPrizePositions,
04621: 
04622:       status: Number.isFinite(
04623:         Number(normalizedResultGrupo)
04624:       )
04625:         ? "validated"
04626:         : "pending",
04627: 
04628:       historyStats,
04629:     });
04630: 
04631:     if (currentDraw) {
04632:       chainContextDraws.push(currentDraw);
04633:     } else {
04634:       pendingPredictionAlreadyShown = true;
04635:     }
04636:   }
04637: 
04638:   return timeline;
04639: 
04640: }
04641: 
04642: export function buildTimelineTop3(args) {
04643:   return buildTimelineForDate(args || {});
04644: }
04645: 
04646: export function auditTop3Backtest({
04647:   drawsRange,
04648:   lotteryKey,
04649:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
04650:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
04651:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
04652: }) {
04653:   const range = Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? drawsRange : [];
04654:   const dates = Array.from(
04655:     new Set(
04656:       range
04657:         .map((d) => pickDrawYMD(d))
04658:         .filter((ymd) => isYMD(ymd))
04659:     )
04660:   ).sort();
04661: 
04662:   const allTimeline = [];
04663: 
04664:   for (const ymd of dates) {
04665:     const drawsToday = range.filter((d) => pickDrawYMD(d) === ymd);
04666: 
04667:     const dayTimeline = buildTimelineForDate({
04668:       ymd,
04669:       drawsToday,
04670:       drawsRange: range,
04671:       lotteryKey,
04672:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
04673:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
04674:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
04675:     });
04676: 
04677:     allTimeline.push(...(Array.isArray(dayTimeline) ? dayTimeline : []));
04678:   }
04679: 
04680:   return auditTop3Timeline({
04681:     timeline: allTimeline,
04682:     lotteryKey,
04683:   });
04684: }

00090: function emptyAnalytics() {

00094: function drawTs(draw) {
00095:   const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
00096:   const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
00097: 
00098:   if (!isYMD(y) || !h) return Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;
00099: 
00100:   const [Y, M, D] = String(y).split("-").map(Number);
00101:   const mins = hourToInt(h);
00102: 
00103:   if (!Number.isFinite(mins) || mins < 0) {
00104:     return Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;
00105:   }
00106: 
00107:   return Date.UTC(Y, M - 1, D) + mins * 60 * 1000;
00108: }
00109: 
00110: function drawKey(draw) {
00111:   const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
00112:   const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
00113:   return isYMD(y) && h ? `${y}|${h}` : "";
00114: }
00115: 
00116: function hasDrawAtHour(draws, hourBucket) {
00117:   const target = toHourBucket(hourBucket);
00118:   if (!target) return false;
00119: 
00120:   return (Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : []).some((d) => {
00121:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
00122:     return h === target;
00123:   });
00124: }
00125: 
00126: function mergeBaseIntoRange(rangeDraws, baseDraw) {
00127:   const list = Array.isArray(rangeDraws) ? rangeDraws : [];
00128:   const key = drawKey(baseDraw);
00129: 
00130:   if (!key) return list;
00131: 
00132:   const map = new Map();
00133: 
00134:   for (const d of list) {
00135:     const k = drawKey(d);
00136:     if (k) map.set(k, d);
00137:   }
00138: 
00139:   if (!map.has(key)) {
00140:     map.set(key, baseDraw);
00141:   }
00142: 
00143:   return Array.from(map.values()).sort((a, b) => drawTs(a) - drawTs(b));
00144: }

00146: function isDrawValidForLotterySchedule(draw, lotteryKey) {
00147:   const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
00148:   const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
00149: 
00150:   if (!isYMD(y) || !h) return false;
00151: 
00152:   const schedule = getScheduleForLottery({
00153:     lotteryKey,
00154:     ymd: y,
00155:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
00156:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
00157:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
00158:   });
00159: 
00160:   const normalizedSchedule = Array.isArray(schedule)
00161:     ? schedule.map(toHourBucket).filter(Boolean)
00162:     : [];
00163: 
00164:   return normalizedSchedule.includes(h);
00165: }

00167: function findLatestHistoricalBaseDraw({
00168:   draws,
00169:   lotteryKey,
00170:   targetYmd,
00171:   targetHourBucket,
00172: }) {
00173:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
00174:   const targetHour = toHourBucket(targetHourBucket);
00175: 
00176:   if (!isYMD(targetYmd) || !targetHour) {
00177:     return { draw: null, ymd: "", hour: "", source: "none" };
00178:   }
00179: 
00180:   const [Y, M, D] = String(targetYmd).split("-").map(Number);
00181:   const mins = hourToInt(targetHour);
00182:   const targetTs = Date.UTC(Y, M - 1, D) + mins * 60 * 1000;
00183: 
00184:   let best = null;
00185:   let bestTs = Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;
00186: 
00187:   for (const d of list) {
00188:     if (!isDrawValidForLotterySchedule(d, lotteryKey)) continue;
00189: 
00190:     const ts = drawTs(d);
00191:     if (!Number.isFinite(ts)) continue;
00192:     if (ts >= targetTs) continue;
00193: 
00194:     if (ts > bestTs) {
00195:       best = d;
00196:       bestTs = ts;
00197:     }
00198:   }
00199: 
00200:   if (!best) {
00201:     return { draw: null, ymd: "", hour: "", source: "none" };
00202:   }
00203: 
00204:   return {
00205:     draw: best,
00206:     ymd: pickDrawYMD(best) || "",
00207:     hour: toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(best)) || "",
00208:     source: "history_range",
00209:   };
00210: }
00211: 
00212: function sanitizeHistoricalDraws({ draws, lotteryKey, baseDraw }) {
00213:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
00214:   const baseTs = drawTs(baseDraw);
00215: 
00216:   if (!baseDraw || !Number.isFinite(baseTs)) return [];
00217: 
00218:   return list
00219:     .filter((d) => isDrawValidForLotterySchedule(d, lotteryKey))
00220:     .filter((d) => {
00221:       const ts = drawTs(d);
00222:       return Number.isFinite(ts) && ts <= baseTs;
00223:     })
00224:     .sort((a, b) => drawTs(a) - drawTs(b));
00225: }

00227: function parseTargetDate(ymd, hour) {
00228:   if (!isYMD(ymd)) return null;
00229: 
00230:   const h = toHourBucket(hour);
00231:   const m = String(h || "").match(/^(\d{2})h$/);
00232: 
00233:   if (!m) return null;
00234: 
00235:   const [Y, M, D] = String(ymd).split("-").map(Number);
00236:   const hh = Number(m[1]);
00237: 
00238:   const dt = new Date(Y, M - 1, D, hh, 0, 0, 0);
00239:   return Number.isNaN(dt.getTime()) ? null : dt;
00240: }

00242: function isFutureTarget(ymd, hour) {
00243:   const target = parseTargetDate(ymd, hour);
00244:   if (!target) return false;
00245:   return target.getTime() > Date.now();
00246: }

00254: function hydratePersistedTop3(entry) {

00260:     .map((item, index) => {

00264:         !Number.isFinite(grupo) ||
00265:         grupo < 1 ||

00272:         Array.isArray(item?.imgBg) && item.imgBg.length
00273:           ? item.imgBg.filter(Boolean)
00274:           : [getGrupoImgSrc(grupo, 512)].filter(Boolean);

00276:       const imgIcon =
00277:         Array.isArray(item?.imgIcon) && item.imgIcon.length
00278:           ? item.imgIcon.filter(Boolean)
00279:           : buildResultStyleImgVariants(grupo, 96);
00280: 
00281:       return {
00282:         ...item,

00286:           safeStr(item?.animal) ||
00287:           safeStr(getAnimalLabel(grupo)),

00290:         milhares20: Array.isArray(item?.milhares20)

00293:         milharesCols: Array.isArray(item?.milharesCols)

00295:           : [],
00296:         imgBg,
00297:         imgIcon,
00298:         persistedSnapshot: true,
00299:       };
00300:     })
00301:     .filter(Boolean)
00302:     .slice(0, 3);
00303: }

01757:           reconcileRunKey,

01768:       })

00006: function extractPrizeMilharByPosition(slot, position) {
00007:   const prizes = Array.isArray(slot?.prizes)
00008:     ? slot.prizes
00009:     : [];
00010: 
00011:   const prize =
00012:     prizes.find(
00013:       (item) => Number(item?.position) === Number(position)
00014:     ) ||
00015:     prizes[Number(position) - 1] ||
00016:     null;
00017: 
00018:   if (!prize) return "";
00019: 
00020:   const digits = String(
00021:     prize?.numero ??
00022:       prize?.milhar ??
00023:       prize?.number ??
00024:       prize?.valor ??
00025:       ""
00026:   ).replace(/\D/g, "");
00027: 
00028:   return digits
00029:     ? digits.slice(-4).padStart(4, "0")
00030:     : "";
00031: }
00032: 
00033: function extractResultMilhar(slot) {
00034:   const persistedMilhar = normalizeMilharStr(
00035:     slot?.resultMilhar ??
00036:       slot?.milhar ??
00037:       slot?.result?.milhar ??
00038:       slot?.resultado?.milhar ??
00039:       ""
00040:   );
00041: 
00042:   if (persistedMilhar) {
00043:     return persistedMilhar;
00044:   }
00045: 
00046:   const persistedTop3 = Array.isArray(
00047:     slot?.resultTop3Milhares
00048:   )
00049:     ? slot.resultTop3Milhares
00050:     : [];
00051: 
00052:   const persistedPrimeira = normalizeMilharStr(
00053:     persistedTop3[0] ?? ""
00054:   );
00055: 
00056:   if (persistedPrimeira) {
00057:     return persistedPrimeira;
00058:   }
00059: 
00060:   return extractPrizeMilharByPosition(slot, 1);
00061: }

00063: function getPrizeGrupoByPosition(slot, position) {
00064:   const persisted = Array.isArray(
00065:     slot?.resultTop3Groups
00066:   )
00067:     ? Number(slot.resultTop3Groups[position - 1])
00068:     : NaN;
00069: 
00070:   if (
00071:     Number.isFinite(persisted) &&
00072:     persisted >= 1 &&
00073:     persisted <= 25
00074:   ) {
00075:     return persisted;
00076:   }

00079:     ? slot.prizes
00080:     : [];
00081: 
00082:   const prize =
00083:     prizes.find(
00084:       (item) => Number(item?.position) === Number(position)
00085:     ) ||
00086:     prizes[Number(position) - 1] ||
00087:     null;
00088: 
00089:   const direct = Number(
00090:     prize?.grupo ??
00091:       prize?.group ??
00092:       prize?.animal_grupo ??
00093:       prize?.grupo2
00094:   );
00095: 
00096:   if (
00097:     Number.isFinite(direct) &&
00098:     direct >= 1 &&
00099:     direct <= 25
00100:   ) {
00101:     return direct;
00102:   }
00103: 
00104:   const milhar = extractPrizeMilharByPosition(
00105:     slot,
00106:     position
00107:   );
00108: 
00109:   if (!milhar) return NaN;
00110: 
00111:   const dezena = Number(milhar.slice(-2));
00112:   const normalizedDezena = dezena === 0 ? 100 : dezena;
00113:   const grupo = Math.ceil(normalizedDezena / 4);
00114: 
00115:   return grupo >= 1 && grupo <= 25
00116:     ? grupo
00117:     : NaN;
00118: }

00166: function toPercent(score) {
00167:   const n = Number(score);
00168:   if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return 0;
00169:   const pct = n <= 1 ? n * 100 : n;
00170:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(100, pct));
00171: }

00173: function formatGrupo(grupo) {
00174:   const g = Number(grupo);
00175:   if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g <= 0) return "—";
00176:   return String(Math.trunc(g)).padStart(2, "0");
00177: }

00179: function normalizeMilharStr(v) {
00180:   const s = String(v || "").trim();
00181:   if (!s) return "";
00182:   const dig = s.replace(/\D+/g, "");
00183:   if (!dig) return "";
00184:   return dig.length >= 4 ? dig.slice(-4) : dig.padStart(4, "0");
00185: }

00187: function centenaFromMilhar(m4) {

00189:   return s ? s.slice(-3) : "";
00190: }

00192: function dezenaFromMilhar(m4) {
00193:   const s = normalizeMilharStr(m4);
00194:   return s ? s.slice(-2) : "";

00197: function isYMD(s) {
00198:   return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(s || "").trim());
00199: }

00201: function ymdToBR(ymd) {
00202:   const s = String(ymd || "").trim();
00203:   if (!isYMD(s)) return s || "—";
00204:   const [y, m, d] = s.split("-");
00205:   return `${d}/${m}/${y}`;
00206: }

00208: function addDaysYMDLocal(ymd, amount) {
00209:   const s = String(ymd || "").trim();
00210:   if (!isYMD(s)) return "";
00211: 
00212:   const [y, m, d] = s.split("-").map(Number);
00213:   const date = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d));
00214: 
00215:   date.setUTCDate(date.getUTCDate() + Number(amount || 0));
00216: 
00217:   return [
00218:     date.getUTCFullYear(),
00219:     String(date.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0"),
00220:     String(date.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, "0"),
00221:   ].join("-");
00222: }

00234: function formatYmdHour(ymd, hour) {
00235:   const y = String(ymd || "").trim();
00236:   const h = String(hour || "").trim();
00237:   if (y && h) return `${ymdToBR(y)} ${h}`;
00238:   if (y) return ymdToBR(y);
00239:   if (h) return h;
00240:   return "—";

00243: function hourBucketToSortValue(hour) {
00244:   const s = String(hour || "").trim().toLowerCase();
00245:   const m = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})(?::(\d{2}))?h?$/);
00246:   if (!m) return -1;
00247:   const hh = Number(m[1]);
00248:   const mm = Number(m[2] || 0);
00249:   if (!Number.isFinite(hh) || !Number.isFinite(mm)) return -1;
00250:   return hh * 60 + mm;
00251: }

00253: function wrapToDezena2(n) {
00254:   const x = Number(n);
00255:   if (!Number.isFinite(x)) return "";
00256:   const d = ((x % 100) + 100) % 100;
00257:   return String(d).padStart(2, "0");
00258: }

00260: function getDezenasFixasFromGrupo(grupo) {
00261:   const g = Number(grupo);
00262:   if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > 25) return [];
00263: 
00264:   const start = (g - 1) * 4 + 1;
00265:   const out = [];
00266: 
00267:   for (let i = 0; i < 4; i += 1) {
00268:     out.push(wrapToDezena2(start + i));
00269:   }
00270: 
00271:   return out;
00272: }

00274: function clampColsFromItemMilharesCols(milharesCols, expectedCols = 4, perCol = 5) {
00275:   const colsArr = Array.isArray(milharesCols) ? milharesCols : [];
00276:   const out = [];
00277: 
00278:   for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(expectedCols, colsArr.length); i += 1) {
00279:     const dz = String(colsArr[i]?.dezena || "").trim();
00280:     const items0 = Array.isArray(colsArr[i]?.items) ? colsArr[i].items : [];
00281: 
00282:     const items = items0
00283:       .map(normalizeMilharStr)
00284:       .map((m) => (m && /^\d{4}$/.test(m) ? m : ""))
00285:       .slice(0, perCol);
00286: 
00287:     while (items.length < perCol) items.push("");
00288:     out.push({ dezena: dz, items });
00289:   }
00290: 
00291:   while (out.length < expectedCols) {
00292:     out.push({ dezena: "", items: Array(perCol).fill("") });
00293:   }
00294: 
00295:   return out.slice(0, expectedCols);
00296: }
00297: 
00298: function build20ByDezena({ grupo, baseMilhares, perCol = 5 }) {
00299:   const g = Number(grupo);
00300:   const dezenas = getDezenasFixasFromGrupo(g);
00301: 
00302:   if (!dezenas.length) {
00303:     return { dezenas: [], rows: [], flat20: [] };
00304:   }
00305: 
00306:   const input = Array.isArray(baseMilhares) ? baseMilhares : [];
00307:   const normalized = input
00308:     .map(normalizeMilharStr)
00309:     .filter((x) => /^\d{4}$/.test(x));
00310: 
00311:   const byDz = new Map();
00312:   for (const dz of dezenas) byDz.set(dz, []);
00313: 
00314:   for (const m4 of normalized) {
00315:     const dz = dezenaFromMilhar(m4);
00316:     if (byDz.has(dz)) byDz.get(dz).push(m4);
00317:   }
00318: 
00319:   const seenCent = new Set();
00320:   const seenMilhar = new Set();
00321: 
00322:   const cols = {};
00323:   for (const dz of dezenas) cols[dz] = [];
00324: 
00325:   const tryPush = (dz, m4) => {
00326:     const mm = normalizeMilharStr(m4);
00327:     if (!mm) return false;
00328: 
00329:     const c3 = centenaFromMilhar(mm);
00330:     if (!c3) return false;
00331: 
00332:     if (seenCent.has(c3)) return false;
00333:     if (seenMilhar.has(mm)) return false;
00334: 
00335:     cols[dz].push(mm);
00336:     seenCent.add(c3);
00337:     seenMilhar.add(mm);
00338:     return true;
00339:   };
00340: 
00341:   for (const dz of dezenas) {
00342:     const arr = byDz.get(dz) || [];
00343:     for (const m4 of arr) {
00344:       if (cols[dz].length >= perCol) break;
00345:       tryPush(dz, m4);
00346:     }
00347:   }
00348: 
00349:   for (const dz of dezenas) {
00350:     while (cols[dz].length < perCol) cols[dz].push("");
00351:     if (cols[dz].length > perCol) cols[dz] = cols[dz].slice(0, perCol);
00352:   }
00353: 
00354:   const rows = [];
00355:   for (let r = 0; r < perCol; r += 1) {
00356:     rows.push(dezenas.map((dz) => cols[dz][r] || ""));
00357:   }
00358: 
00359:   const flat20 = rows.flat().filter(Boolean);
00360:   return { dezenas, rows, flat20 };
00361: }

00368:     .replace(/\s*[•\-|]\s*Amostras:\s*\d+\s*$/i, "")
00369:     .replace(/\s*Amostras:\s*\d+\s*$/i, "")
00370:     .trim();
00371: 
00372:   return noSamples || "—";
00373: }

00412: function analyzeTop3Hit(top3, resultSource, resultMilhar) {
00413:   if (!Array.isArray(top3) || !top3.length) {
00414:     return {
00415:       type: "none",
00416:       score: 0,
00417:       position: -1,
00418:       predictionPosition: -1,
00419:       resultPosition: -1,
00420:       podiumMedal: "",
00421:       matchedValue: "",
00422:       matchedGrupo: null,
00423:       matchedMilhar: "",
00424:     };
00425:   }
00426: 
00427:   const officialPodium = Array.isArray(resultSource)
00428:     ? resultSource.filter(Boolean).slice(0, 3)
00429:     : [
00430:         {
00431:           position: 1,
00432:           grupo: Number(resultSource),
00433:           milhar: normalizeMilharStr(resultMilhar),
00434:         },
00435:       ].filter(
00436:         (item) =>
00437:           Number.isFinite(item.grupo) &&
00438:           item.grupo >= 1 &&
00439:           item.grupo <= 25
00440:       );
00441: 
00442:   if (!officialPodium.length) {
00443:     return {
00444:       type: "none",
00445:       score: 0,
00446:       position: -1,
00447:       predictionPosition: -1,
00448:       resultPosition: -1,
00449:       podiumMedal: "",
00450:       matchedValue: "",
00451:       matchedGrupo: null,
00452:       matchedMilhar: "",
00453:     };
00454:   }
00455: 
00456:   for (const officialPrize of officialPodium) {
00457:     const grupoNum = Number(officialPrize?.grupo);
00458:     const milhar = normalizeMilharStr(
00459:       officialPrize?.milhar
00460:     );
00461:     const centena = milhar
00462:       ? milhar.slice(-3)
00463:       : "";
00464: 
00465:     for (
00466:       let predictionIndex = 0;
00467:       predictionIndex < top3.length;
00468:       predictionIndex += 1
00469:     ) {
00470:       const item = top3[predictionIndex];
00471:       const grupo = Number(item?.grupo);
00472: 
00473:       const milhares = (
00474:         Array.isArray(item?.milhares20)
00475:           ? item.milhares20
00476:           : Array.isArray(item?.milhares)
00477:             ? item.milhares
00478:             : []
00479:       )
00480:         .map(normalizeMilharStr)
00481:         .filter((value) => /^\d{4}$/.test(value));
00482: 
00483:       const centenas = milhares
00484:         .map((value) => centenaFromMilhar(value))
00485:         .filter((value) => /^\d{3}$/.test(value));
00486: 
00487:       let type = "miss";
00488:       let score = 0;
00489:       let matchedValue = "";
00490: 
00491:       if (milhar && milhares.includes(milhar)) {
00492:         type = "hit_exact";
00493:         score = 100;
00494:         matchedValue = milhar;
00495:       } else if (centena && centenas.includes(centena)) {
00496:         type = "hit_centena";
00497:         score = 66.67;
00498:         matchedValue = centena;
00499:       } else if (
00500:         Number.isFinite(grupoNum) &&
00501:         grupo === grupoNum
00502:       ) {
00503:         type = "hit_grupo";
00504:         score = 33.33;
00505:         matchedValue = milhar
00506:           ? milhar.slice(-2)
00507:           : formatGrupo(grupoNum);
00508:       }
00509: 
00510:       if (type !== "miss") {
00511:         const resultPosition = Number(
00512:           officialPrize?.position
00513:         );
00514: 
00515:         return {
00516:           type,
00517:           score,
00518:           position: predictionIndex + 1,
00519:           predictionPosition: predictionIndex + 1,
00520:           resultPosition,
00521:           podiumMedal:
00522:             podiumMedalFromPosition(resultPosition),
00523:           matchedValue,
00524:           matchedGrupo: grupoNum,
00525:           matchedMilhar: milhar,
00526:         };
00527:       }
00528:     }
00529:   }
00530: 
00531:   return {
00532:     type: "miss",
00533:     score: 0,
00534:     position: -1,
00535:     predictionPosition: -1,
00536:     resultPosition: -1,
00537:     podiumMedal: "",
00538:     matchedValue: "",
00539:     matchedGrupo: null,
00540:     matchedMilhar: "",
00541:   };
00542: }

00544: function ImgWithFallback({ srcs, alt, size = 84, style }) {
00545:   const list = useMemo(
00546:     () => (Array.isArray(srcs) ? srcs.filter(Boolean) : []),
00547:     [srcs]
00548:   );
00549: 
00550:   const [i, setI] = useState(0);
00551:   const listKey = useMemo(() => list.join("|"), [list]);
00552: 
00553:   useEffect(() => {
00554:     setI(0);
00555:   }, [listKey]);
00556: 
00557:   const src = list[i] || "";
00558: 
00559:   const onError = () => {
00560:     setI((prev) => (prev < list.length - 1 ? prev + 1 : prev));
00561:   };
00562: 
00563:   return (
00564:     <div
00565:       style={{
00566:         width: size,
00567:         height: size,
00568:         borderRadius: 16,
00569:         border: "1px solid rgba(201,168,62,0.36)",
00570:         background:
00571:           "linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(255,255,255,0.04), rgba(0,0,0,0.28))",
00572:         display: "grid",
00573:         placeItems: "center",
00574:         overflow: "hidden",
00575:         boxShadow: "inset 0 0 0 1px rgba(255,255,255,0.04)",
00576:         ...style,
00577:       }}
00578:     >
00579:       {src ? (
00580:         <img
00581:           src={src}
00582:           alt={alt || ""}
00583:           width={size}
00584:           height={size}
00585:           onError={onError}
00586:           style={{
00587:             width: "100%",
00588:             height: "100%",
00589:             objectFit: "cover",
00590:             display: "block",
00591:             imageRendering: "auto",
00592:           }}
00593:         />
00594:       ) : (
00595:         <div style={{ color: "rgba(255,255,255,0.55)", fontSize: 12 }}>
00596:           sem imagem
00597:         </div>
00598:       )}
00599:     </div>
00600:   );
00601: }

00623: function Top3Card({
00624:   item,
00625:   idx,
00626:   theme,
00627:   copiedAllKey,
00628:   copiedCellKey,
00629:   setCopiedAllKey,
00630:   setCopiedCellKey,

00636:   const t = theme;
00637: 
00638:   const grupoTxt = formatGrupo(item?.grupo);
00639:   const animal = String(item?.animal || "").trim();
00640: 
00641:   const samplesRaw = Number(item?.meta?.samples ?? item?.samples ?? 0);
00642:   const samples = Number.isFinite(samplesRaw)
00643:     ? Math.max(0, Math.trunc(samplesRaw))
00644:     : 0;
00645: 
00646:   const freqRaw = Number(item?.freq ?? 0);
00647:   const freq = Number.isFinite(freqRaw)
00648:     ? Math.max(0, Math.trunc(freqRaw))
00649:     : 0;
00650: 
00651:   const denom = samples > 0 ? samples * 5 : 0;
00652:   const derivedScore = denom > 0 ? freq / denom : 0;
00653: 
00654:   const pct = toPercent(
00655:     item?.probPct ??
00656:       item?.prob ??
00657:       item?.probCond ??
00658:       item?.score ??
00659:       derivedScore
00660:   );
00661: 
00662:   const iconSrcs =
00663:     Array.isArray(item?.imgIcon) && item.imgIcon.length
00664:       ? item.imgIcon
00665:       : Array.isArray(item?.imgBg)
00666:         ? item.imgBg
00667:         : [];
00668: 
00669:   const hasCols =
00670:     Array.isArray(item?.milharesCols) &&
00671:     item.milharesCols.length >= 4 &&
00672:     Array.isArray(item.milharesCols[0]?.items);
00673: 
00674:   let dezenasHeader = [];
00675:   let gridRows = Array.from({ length: 5 }, () => Array(4).fill(""));
00676:   let flat20 = [];
00677: 
00678:   if (hasCols) {
00679:     const cols4 = clampColsFromItemMilharesCols(item.milharesCols, 4, 5);
00680:     dezenasHeader = cols4.map((c) => String(c.dezena || ""));
00681:     gridRows = Array.from({ length: 5 }, (_, r) =>
00682:       cols4.map((c) => c.items[r] || "")
00683:     );
00684:     flat20 = gridRows.flat().filter(Boolean);
00685:   } else {
00686:     let milharesBase = [];
00687: 
00688:     const m20 = Array.isArray(item?.milhares20) ? item.milhares20 : null;
00689:     const mAny = Array.isArray(item?.milhares) ? item.milhares : null;
00690: 
00691:     if (m20 && m20.length) milharesBase = m20.slice(0);
00692:     else if (mAny && mAny.length) milharesBase = mAny.slice(0);
00693: 
00694:     if (!milharesBase.length) {
00695:       const g = Number(item?.grupo);
00696: 
00697:       if (Number.isFinite(g) && g > 0) {
00698:         if (typeof build20 === "function") {
00699:           const out20 = build20(g);
00700:           const slots20 = Array.isArray(out20?.slots) ? out20.slots : [];
00701:           milharesBase = slots20.map((x) => x?.milhar).filter(Boolean);
00702:         } else if (typeof buildMilhares === "function") {
00703:           const out = buildMilhares(g, 20);
00704:           if (Array.isArray(out)) {
00705:             milharesBase = out.slice(0);
00706:           } else if (out && Array.isArray(out.slots)) {
00707:             milharesBase = out.slots.map((x) => x?.milhar).filter(Boolean);
00708:           }
00709:         } else if (typeof build16 === "function") {
00710:           const out16 = build16(g);
00711:           const slots16 = Array.isArray(out16?.slots) ? out16.slots : [];
00712:           milharesBase = slots16.map((x) => x?.milhar).filter(Boolean);
00713:         }
00714:       }
00715:     }
00716: 
00717:     const grupoNum = Number(item?.grupo);
00718:     const grid = build20ByDezena({
00719:       grupo: grupoNum,
00720:       baseMilhares: milharesBase,
00721:       perCol: 5,
00722:     });
00723: 
00724:     dezenasHeader = grid.dezenas;
00725:     gridRows = grid.rows;
00726:     flat20 = grid.flat20;
00727:   }
00728: 
00729:   const key = `${String(item?.grupo ?? "g")}__${animal || "x"}__${idx}`;
00730:   const rank = idx + 1;
00731:   // TOP3 EQUAL CARD LAYOUT
00732:   // A hierarquia permanece no ranking, mas os três cards usam a mesma estrutura.
00733:   const isHero = false;
00734: 
00735:   const title =
00736:     idx === 0 ? "1º MAIS FORTE" : idx === 1 ? "2º MAIS FORTE" : "3º MAIS FORTE";
00737: 
00738:   const engineScore = Number(item?.score ?? 0);
00739:   const evidenceCount = Number(item?.evidenceCount ?? 0);
00740:   const evidenceModules = Array.isArray(item?.evidenceModules)
00741:     ? item.evidenceModules.filter(Boolean)
00742:     : [];
00743: 
00744:   const explain = item?.meta?.explain || {};
00745:   const detailMap =
00746:     explain?.details && typeof explain.details === "object"
00747:       ? explain.details
00748:       : {};
00749: 
00750:   const transitionDetail = detailMap.transition || null;
00751:   const hourDetail = detailMap.hour || null;
00752:   const dowDetail = detailMap.dow || detailMap.weekday || null;
00753:   const sceneDetail = detailMap.scene || null;
00754: 
00755:   const periodFrom = String(item?.meta?.period?.from || "").trim();
00756:   const periodTo = String(item?.meta?.period?.to || "").trim();
00757: 
00758:   const periodLabel =
00759:     periodFrom && periodTo
00760:       ? `Período analisado: ${ymdToBR(periodFrom)} até ${ymdToBR(periodTo)}`
00761:       : samples > 0
00762:         ? `Amostra analisada: ${samples} sorteios`
00763:         : "";
00764: 
00765:   const baseGrupo = formatGrupo(item?.meta?.trigger?.grupo);
00766:   const baseHour = String(item?.meta?.trigger?.hour || "").trim();
00767:   const targetHour = String(item?.meta?.next?.hour || "").trim();
00768: 
00769:   const smartReasons = [];
00770: 
00771:   if (periodLabel) {
00772:     smartReasons.push(periodLabel);
00773:   }
00774: 
00775:   if (transitionDetail) {
00776:     const first = Number(transitionDetail.firstCount || 0);
00777:     const top5 = Number(transitionDetail.top3Count || 0);
00778:     const amostras = Number(transitionDetail.samples || 0);
00779: 
00780:     smartReasons.push(
00781:       `Após G${baseGrupo} às ${baseHour}, G${grupoTxt} apareceu ${first}x em 1º e ${top5}x no TOP3 para o alvo ${targetHour}.`
00782:     );
00783: 
00784:     if (amostras > 0) {
00785:       smartReasons.push(`Cenários de transição avaliados: ${amostras}.`);
00786:     }
00787:   }
00788: 
00789:   if (hourDetail) {
00790:     const first = Number(hourDetail.firstCount || 0);
00791:     const top5 = Number(hourDetail.top3Count || 0);
00792: 
00793:     smartReasons.push(
00794:       `No horário ${targetHour}, G${grupoTxt} apareceu ${first}x em 1º e ${top5}x no TOP3.`
00795:     );
00796:   }
00797: 
00798:   if (dowDetail) {
00799:     const first = Number(dowDetail.firstCount || 0);
00800:     const top5 = Number(dowDetail.top3Count || 0);
00801: 
00802:     smartReasons.push(
00803:       `No mesmo dia da semana, G${grupoTxt} apareceu ${first}x em 1º e ${top5}x no TOP3.`
00804:     );
00805:   }
00806: 
00807:   if (sceneDetail && Number(sceneDetail.samples || 0) > 0) {
00808:     smartReasons.push(
00809:       `Cenários semelhantes encontrados: ${Number(sceneDetail.samples || 0)}.`
00810:     );
00811:   }
00812: 
00813:   smartReasons.push(`Probabilidade final do G${grupoTxt}: ${pct.toFixed(2)}%.`);
00814: 
00815:   const evidenceReasons = smartReasons
00816:     .concat(Array.isArray(item?.reasons) ? item.reasons.filter(Boolean) : [])
00817:     .filter(Boolean)
00818:     .slice(0, 7);
00819: 
00820:   const visibleEvidenceCount = Math.max(
00821:     evidenceCount || 0,
00822:     evidenceModules.length || 0,
00823:     evidenceReasons.length || 0
00824:   );
00825: 
00826:   const trendLabel =
00827:     engineScore >= pct
00828:       ? "TENDÊNCIA POSITIVA"
00829:       : "TENDÊNCIA NEUTRA";
00830: 
00831:   const displayScore = Math.round(
00832:     Math.max(
00833:       1,
00834:       Math.min(
00835:         99,
00836:         Number.isFinite(engineScore) && engineScore > 0
00837:           ? engineScore
00838:           : pct * 10
00839:       )
00840:     )
00841:   );
00842: 
00843:   const scoreBarPct = Math.max(4, Math.min(100, displayScore));
00844:

00846:     typeof getAnimalLabel === "function"
00847:       ? String(getAnimalLabel(Number(item?.meta?.trigger?.grupo)) || "").trim()
00848:       : "";
00849: 
00850:   const baseLabel = baseAnimalName
00851:     ? `${baseAnimalName} (G${baseGrupo})`
00852:     : `G${baseGrupo}`;

00855:     ? `${animal} (G${grupoTxt})`
00856:     : `G${grupoTxt}`;
00857: 
00858:   const targetWeekdayName = getWeekdayNameBR(item?.meta?.next?.ymd || item?.targetYmd || "");
00859: 
00860:   const transitionFirst = Number(transitionDetail?.firstCount || 0);
00861:   const transitionTop3 = Number(transitionDetail?.top3Count || transitionDetail?.top5Count || 0);
00862:   const transitionSamples = Number(transitionDetail?.samples || 0);
00863: 
00864:   const hourFirst = Number(hourDetail?.firstCount || 0);

00870:       ? "MUITO FORTE"
00871:       : displayScore >= 75
00872:         ? "FORTE"
00873:         : displayScore >= 60
00874:           ? "MODERADA"
00875:           : "BAIXA";

00878:     displayScore >= 85

00881:         ? "MÉDIA"

00883:           ? "EM FORMAÇÃO"
00884:           : "SEM EVIDÊNCIAS";
00885: 
00886:   const doCopyAll = async () => {
00887:     const payload = flat20.join(" ").trim();
00888:     if (!payload) return;
00889: 
00890:     const ok = await copyText(payload);
00891:     if (ok) setCopiedAllKey(key);
00892:   };
00893: 
00894:   const doCopyOne = async (m, rIdx, cIdx) => {
00895:     const mm = normalizeMilharStr(m);
00896:     if (!mm) return;
00897:

00899:     if (ok) setCopiedCellKey(`${key}__${rIdx}__${cIdx}`);
00900:   };
00901: 
00902:   return (
00903:     <article
00904:       className={`top3-card ${isHero ? "top3-card--hero" : "top3-card--secondary"}`}

00909:       </div>

00959:             }}

00998:                   background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.22)",
00999:                 }}
01000:               >
01001:                 <div className="top3-card__confidenceLabel">SCORE</div>

01110:           : `${samples} registros históricos`
01111:     }
01112:               </div>

01118:                 {transitionFirst > 0 && transitionSamples > 0
01119:                   ? `${transitionFirst} ocorrências em ${transitionSamples} transições`
01120:                   : "Em apuração"}
01121:               </div>
01122:             </div>
01123:           </div>

01138:             <div>

01142:                   fontWeight: 900,

01144:                 }}
01145:               >
01146:                 Score — confiança da análise
01147:               </div>

01172:           </button>
01173:         </div>
01174: 
01175:         {dezenasHeader.length ? (

01180:               </div>
01181:             ))}
01182:           </div>
01183:         ) : null}
01184: 
01185:         <div className="pp-gridBox">
01186:           {gridRows.map((row, rIdx) => (

01190:                 const empty = !mm ? "1" : "0";
01191:                 const cKey = `${key}__${rIdx}__${cIdx}`;
01192:                 const isCopied = copiedCellKey === cKey;
01193: 
01194:                 return (
01195:                   <div
01196:                     key={`${rIdx}-${cIdx}`}
01197:                     className="pp-pill"
01198:                     data-empty={empty}
01199:                     title={mm ? "Clique para copiar" : ""}
