01201:                   >
01202:                     {mm || "—"}
01203:                     {isCopied ? <div className="pp-copiedBadge">COPIADO</div> : null}
01204:                   </div>
01205:                 );
01206:               })}
01207:             </div>
01208:           ))}
01209:         </div>
01210:       </div>
01211:     </article>
01212:   );
01213: }
01214: 
01215: function CompactTop3Result({
01216:   slot,
01217:   status,
01218:   statusLabel,
01219:   statusDetail,
01220:   analysis,
01221: }) {
01222:   const slotTop3 = Array.isArray(slot?.top3)
01223:     ? slot.top3.slice(0, 3)
01224:     : [];
01225: 
01226:   const resultGrupo = getSlotResultGrupo(slot);
01227: 
01228:   const hasResult =
01229:     Number.isFinite(resultGrupo) &&
01230:     resultGrupo >= 1 &&
01231:     resultGrupo <= 25;
01232: 
01233:   const resultAnimal = hasResult
01234:     ? String(getAnimalLabel(resultGrupo) || "")
01235:     : "";
01236: 
01237:   const resultImg = hasResult
01238:     ? getImgFromGrupo(resultGrupo, 64)
01239:     : "";
01240: 
01241:   const resultMilhar = extractResultMilhar(slot);
01242: 
01243:   const matchedPredictionIndex =
01244:     Number.isFinite(Number(analysis?.position)) &&
01245:     Number(analysis.position) >= 1
01246:       ? Number(analysis.position) - 1
01247:       : -1;
01248: 
01249:   const resultPosition = Number(
01250:     analysis?.resultPosition
01251:   );
01252: 
01253:   const medal =
01254:     status !== "hit"
01255:       ? ""
01256:       : resultPosition === 1
01257:         ? "🥇"
01258:         : resultPosition === 2
01259:           ? "🥈"
01260:           : resultPosition === 3
01261:             ? "🥉"
01262:             : "";
01263: 
01264:   const dateLabel = formatYmdHour(
01265:     slot?.targetYmd,
01266:     slot?.targetHour
01267:   );
01268: 
01269:   return (
01270:     <div
01271:       className="timeline-compact"
01272:       title={statusDetail || statusLabel || ""}
01273:       style={{
01274:         width: "100%",
01275:         overflowX: "auto",
01276:       }}
01277:     >
01278:       <div
01279:         className="timeline-compact__twoLine"
01280:         style={{
01281:           display: "grid",
01282:           gridTemplateColumns:
01283:             "minmax(112px, 1.35fr) repeat(3, minmax(54px, 0.72fr)) minmax(82px, 0.95fr)",
01284:           alignItems: "center",
01285:           gap: "6px",
01286:           minWidth: "410px",
01287:           padding: "8px 6px",
01288:         }}
01289:       >
01290:         <div
01291:           className="timeline-compact__date"
01292:           style={{
01293:             gridRow: "1 / span 2",
01294:             alignSelf: "center",
01295:             fontSize: "12px",
01296:             fontWeight: 800,
01297:             lineHeight: 1.2,
01298:             whiteSpace: "nowrap",
01299:           }}
01300:         >
01301:           {dateLabel}
01302:         </div>
01303: 
01304:         {slotTop3.map((item, idx) => {
01305:           const grupo = Number(item?.grupo);
01306: 
01307:           const animal = String(
01308:             item?.animal ||
01309:               getAnimalLabel(grupo) ||
01310:               ""
01311:           ).trim();
01312: 
01313:           const img = Number.isFinite(grupo)
01314:             ? getImgFromGrupo(grupo, 64)
01315:             : "";
01316: 
01317:           const isMatched =
01318:             status === "hit" &&
01319:             matchedPredictionIndex === idx;
01320: 
01321:           return (
01322:             <div
01323:               key={`${String(
01324:                 slot?.targetYmd || "y"
01325:               )}_${String(
01326:                 slot?.targetHour || "h"
01327:               )}_${idx}_${grupo}`}
01328:               className={`timeline-compact__pick ${
01329:                 isMatched
01330:                   ? "timeline-compact__pick--matched"
01331:                   : ""
01332:               }`}
01333:               style={{
01334:                 display: "grid",
01335:                 gridTemplateRows: "34px 18px",
01336:                 justifyItems: "center",
01337:                 alignItems: "center",
01338:                 minWidth: 0,
01339:                 padding: 0,
01340:                 border: "none",
01341:                 background: "transparent",
01342:               }}
01343:               title={`${animal || "Animal"} — G${formatGrupo(
01344:                 grupo
01345:               )}`}
01346:             >
01347:               <div
01348:                 style={{
01349:                   position: "relative",
01350:                   display: "flex",
01351:                   alignItems: "center",
01352:                   justifyContent: "center",
01353:                 }}
01354:               >
01355:                 <ImgWithFallback
01356:                   srcs={img ? [img] : []}
01357:                   alt={
01358:                     animal ||
01359:                     `G${formatGrupo(grupo)}`
01360:                   }
01361:                   size={34}
01362:                   style={{
01363:                     borderRadius: 8,
01364:                     outline: isMatched
01365:                       ? "2px solid rgba(201,168,62,0.95)"
01366:                       : "none",
01367:                     outlineOffset: 1,
01368:                   }}
01369:                 />
01370: 
01371:                 {isMatched && medal ? (
01372:                   <span
01373:                     aria-label="Palpite acertado"
01374:                     style={{
01375:                       position: "absolute",
01376:                       top: -8,
01377:                       right: -13,
01378:                       fontSize: "17px",
01379:                       lineHeight: 1,
01380:                       filter:
01381:                         "drop-shadow(0 1px 2px rgba(0,0,0,0.75))",
01382:                     }}
01383:                   >
01384:                     {medal}
01385:                   </span>
01386:                 ) : null}
01387:               </div>
01388: 
01389:               <div
01390:                 style={{
01391:                   fontSize: "12px",
01392:                   fontWeight: 900,
01393:                   lineHeight: 1,
01394:                   whiteSpace: "nowrap",
01395:                 }}
01396:               >
01397:                 G{formatGrupo(grupo)}
01398:               </div>
01399:             </div>
01400:           );
01401:         })}
01402: 
01403:         <div
01404:           className="timeline-compact__official"
01405:           style={{
01406:             display: "grid",
01407:             gridTemplateRows: "34px 18px",
01408:             justifyItems: "center",
01409:             alignItems: "center",
01410:             minWidth: 0,
01411:             paddingLeft: "6px",
01412:             borderLeft:
01413:               "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.14)",
01414:           }}
01415:           title={
01416:             hasResult
01417:               ? `${resultAnimal} — G${formatGrupo(
01418:                   resultGrupo
01419:                 )}${
01420:                   resultMilhar
01421:                     ? ` — ${resultMilhar}`
01422:                     : ""
01423:                 }`
01424:               : "Resultado pendente"
01425:           }
01426:         >
01427:           {hasResult ? (
01428:             <>
01429:               <ImgWithFallback
01430:                 srcs={resultImg ? [resultImg] : []}
01431:                 alt={resultAnimal}
01432:                 size={34}
01433:                 style={{ borderRadius: 8 }}
01434:               />
01435: 
01436:               <div
01437:                 style={{
01438:                   fontSize: "11px",
01439:                   fontWeight: 900,
01440:                   lineHeight: 1,
01441:                   whiteSpace: "nowrap",
01442:                 }}
01443:               >
01444:                 G{formatGrupo(resultGrupo)}
01445:                 {resultMilhar
01446:                   ? ` • ${resultMilhar}`
01447:                   : ""}
01448:               </div>
01449:             </>
01450:           ) : (
01451:             <div
01452:               style={{
01453:                 gridRow: "1 / span 2",
01454:                 alignSelf: "center",
01455:                 fontSize: "10px",
01456:                 fontWeight: 800,
01457:               }}
01458:             >
01459:               PENDENTE
01460:             </div>
01461:           )}
01462:         </div>
01463:       </div>
01464:     </div>
01465:   );
01466: }
01467: 
01468: function TimelineSlot({
01469:   slot,
01470:   theme,
01471:   copiedAllKey,
01472:   copiedCellKey,
01473:   setCopiedAllKey,
01474:   setCopiedCellKey,
01475:   copyText,
01476:   build16,
01477:   buildMilhares,
01478:   build20,
01479: }) {
01480:   const t = theme;
01481:   const slotTop3 = Array.isArray(slot?.top3) ? slot.top3.slice(0, 3) : [];
01482: 
01483:   const resultGrupo = getSlotResultGrupo(slot);
01484:   const hasResult = hasOfficialResult(slot);
01485:   const resultAnimal =
01486:     hasResult && Number.isFinite(resultGrupo)
01487:       ? String(getAnimalLabel(resultGrupo) || "")
01488:       : "";
01489: 
01490:   const officialPodium = getOfficialPodium(slot);
01491: 
01492:   const analysis = analyzeTop3Hit(
01493:     slotTop3,
01494:     officialPodium
01495:   );
01496: 
01497:   const status =
01498:     !hasResult
01499:       ? "pending"
01500:       : analysis.type === "miss"
01501:         ? "miss"
01502:         : "hit";
01503: 
01504:   const statusLabel =
01505:     !hasResult
01506:       ? "⏳ PENDENTE"
01507:       : analysis.type === "hit_exact"
01508:         ? "🎯 MILHAR (100%)"
01509:         : analysis.type === "hit_centena"
01510:           ? "🟡 CENTENA (66,67%)"
01511:           : analysis.type === "hit_grupo"
01512:             ? "✅ DEZENA/GRUPO (33,33%)"
01513:             : "❌ ERRO (0%)";
01514: 
01515:   const statusDetail =
01516:     !hasResult
01517:       ? "Aguardando resultado oficial."
01518:       : analysis.type === "hit_exact"
01519:         ? `Milhar exata acertada no palpite #${analysis.position}.`
01520:         : analysis.type === "hit_centena"
01521:           ? `Centena acertada no palpite #${analysis.position}.`
01522:           : analysis.type === "hit_grupo"
01523:             ? `Grupo acertado no palpite #${analysis.position}.`
01524:             : "Nenhum dos 3 palpites foi sorteado.";
01525: 
01526:   return (
01527:     <section className="top3-shell">
01528:       {status === "pending" ? (
01529:         <>
01530:           <div className="timeline-slot__header">
01531:             <div className="timeline-slot__title">
01532:               {formatYmdHour(
01533:                 slot?.targetYmd,
01534:                 slot?.targetHour
01535:               )}
01536:             </div>
01537: 
01538:             <div
01539:               className={`timeline-slot__badge timeline-slot__badge--${status}`}
01540:             >
01541:               {statusLabel}
01542:             </div>
01543:           </div>
01544: 
01545:           <div className="timeline-slot__metaGrid">
01546:             <div className="top3-metaItem">
01547:               <div className="top3-metaItem__label">
01548:                 Base usada
01549:               </div>
01550: 
01551:               <div className="top3-metaItem__value">
01552:                 {formatYmdHour(
01553:                   slot?.baseYmd,
01554:                   slot?.baseHour
01555:                 )}
01556:               </div>
01557:             </div>
01558: 
01559:             <div className="top3-metaItem">
01560:               <div className="top3-metaItem__label">
01561:                 Resultado real
01562:               </div>
01563: 
01564:               <div className="top3-metaItem__value">
01565:                 {hasResult
01566:                   ? `G${formatGrupo(
01567:                       resultGrupo
01568:                     )} • ${resultAnimal.toUpperCase()}`
01569:                   : "Pendente"}
01570:               </div>
01571:             </div>
01572: 
01573:             <div className="top3-metaItem">
01574:               <div className="top3-metaItem__label">
01575:                 Status
01576:               </div>
01577: 
01578:               <div className="top3-metaItem__value">
01579:                 {statusDetail}
01580:               </div>
01581:             </div>
01582:           </div>
01583:         </>
01584:       ) : null}
01585: 
01586:       {!slotTop3.length ? (
01587:         <div className="top3-empty">Sem Top3 calculado para este horário.</div>
01588:       ) : status === "pending" ? (
01589:         <div className="timeline-slot__cards">
01590:           {slotTop3.map((item, i) => (
01591:             <Top3Card
01592:               key={`${slot?.targetYmd || "y"}_${slot?.targetHour || "h"}_${String(item?.grupo ?? i)}`}
01593:               item={item}
01594:               idx={i}
01595:               theme={t}
01596:               copiedAllKey={copiedAllKey}
01597:               copiedCellKey={copiedCellKey}
01598:               setCopiedAllKey={setCopiedAllKey}
01599:               setCopiedCellKey={setCopiedCellKey}
01600:               copyText={copyText}
01601:               build16={build16}
01602:               buildMilhares={buildMilhares}
01603:               build20={build20}
01604:             />
01605:           ))}
01606:         </div>
01607:       ) : (
01608:         <CompactTop3Result
01609:           slot={slot}
01610:           status={status}
01611:           statusLabel={statusLabel}
01612:           statusDetail={statusDetail}
01613:           analysis={analysis}
01614:         />
01615:       )}
01616:     </section>
01617:   );
01618: }
01619: 
01620: export default function Top3View(props) {
01621:   const {
01622:     loading,
01623:     error,
01624:     timelineTop3,
01625:     persistedTop3History,
01626:     availableHistoryDates: controllerHistoryDates,
01627:     top3,
01628:     layerMetaText,
01629:     lastLabel,
01630:     prevLabel,
01631:     theme,
01632:     LOTTERY_OPTIONS,
01633:     lotteryKeySafe,
01634:     setLotteryKey,
01635:     setYmd,
01636:     build16,
01637:     buildMilhares,
01638:     build20,
01639:     analysisYmd,
01640:     analysisHourBucket,
01641:     loadedYmd,
01642:     ymdSafe,
01643:     lastHourBucket,
01644:   } = props || {};
01645: 
01646:   // TODO_BACKTEST_REAL
01647: // Os cards estatísticos deverão consumir auditTop3Backtest()
01648: // evitando cálculos paralelos na interface.
01649: //
01650: // Fase atual:
01651: // apenas corrigindo nomenclatura para refletir o cálculo existente.
01652: 
01653: const list = Array.isArray(top3)
01654:   ? top3.slice(0, 3)
01655:   : [];
01656:   const timeline = (Array.isArray(timelineTop3) ? timelineTop3 : [])
01657:   .slice()
01658:   .sort((a, b) => {
01659:     const ta = `${a?.targetYmd || ""} ${a?.targetHour || ""}`;
01660:     const tb = `${b?.targetYmd || ""} ${b?.targetHour || ""}`;
01661:     return ta.localeCompare(tb);
01662:   });
01663: 
01664:   const showDetailedTimeline = false;
01665: 
01666:   const meta = useMemo(() => {
01667:     const last = lastLabel || "—";
01668:     const prev = prevLabel || "—";
01669:     const layer = cleanLayerText(layerMetaText || "—");
01670:     return { last, prev, layer };
01671:   }, [lastLabel, prevLabel, layerMetaText]);
01672: 
01673:   const headerBase = useMemo(() => meta.last || "—", [meta.last]);
01674: 
01675:   const headerForecast = useMemo(() => {
01676:     return formatYmdHour(analysisYmd, analysisHourBucket);
01677:   }, [analysisYmd, analysisHourBucket]);
01678: 
01679:   const headerTransition = useMemo(() => {
01680:     const from = String(lastHourBucket || "").trim();
01681:     const to = String(analysisHourBucket || "").trim();
01682:     if (from && to) return `${from} → ${to}`;
01683:     return "—";
01684:   }, [lastHourBucket, analysisHourBucket]);
01685: 
01686:   const t = theme || {
01687:     bg: "#050505",
01688:     panel: "rgba(0,0,0,0.55)",
01689:     border: "rgba(255,255,255,0.18)",
01690:     text: "rgba(255,255,255,0.92)",
01691:     muted: "rgba(255,255,255,0.72)",
01692:     accent: "rgba(201,168,62,0.92)",
01693:   };
01694: 
01695:   const [copiedAllKey, setCopiedAllKey] = useState("");
01696:   const [copiedCellKey, setCopiedCellKey] = useState("");
01697: 
01698: 
01699:   useEffect(() => {
01700:     if (!copiedAllKey) return;
01701:     const id = setTimeout(() => setCopiedAllKey(""), 900);
01702:     return () => clearTimeout(id);
01703:   }, [copiedAllKey]);
01704: 
01705:   useEffect(() => {
01706:     if (!copiedCellKey) return;
01707:     const id = setTimeout(() => setCopiedCellKey(""), 750);
01708:     return () => clearTimeout(id);
01709:   }, [copiedCellKey]);
01710: 
01711:   const copyText = useCallback(async (txt) => {
01712:     const s = String(txt || "").trim();
01713:     if (!s) return false;
01714: 
01715:     try {
01716:       if (typeof navigator !== "undefined" && navigator?.clipboard?.writeText) {
01717:         await navigator.clipboard.writeText(s);
01718:         return true;
01719:       }
01720:     } catch {}
01721: 
01722:     try {
01723:       if (typeof document === "undefined") return false;
01724: 
01725:       const ta = document.createElement("textarea");
01726:       ta.value = s;
01727:       ta.style.position = "fixed";
01728:       ta.style.left = "-9999px";
01729:       ta.style.top = "0";
01730:       document.body.appendChild(ta);
01731:       ta.focus();
01732:       ta.select();
01733:       document.execCommand("copy");
01734:       document.body.removeChild(ta);
01735:       return true;
01736:     } catch {
01737:       return false;
01738:     }
01739:   }, []);
01740: 
01741:   const baseLots = Array.isArray(LOTTERY_OPTIONS) ? LOTTERY_OPTIONS : [];
01742:   const mustHave = [
01743:     { value: "PT_RIO", label: "RJ" },
01744:     { value: "FEDERAL", label: "Federal" },
01745:     { value: "LOOK", label: "LOOK" },
01746:     { value: "NACIONAL", label: "Nacional" },
01747:   ];
01748: 
01749:   const map = new Map();
01750: 
01751:   [...baseLots, ...mustHave].forEach((op) => {
01752:     const rawVal = String(op?.value ?? op?.key ?? "").toUpperCase();
01753:     if (!rawVal) return;
01754: 
01755:     if (!map.has(rawVal)) {
01756:       map.set(rawVal, {
01757:         value: rawVal,
01758:         label:
01759:           rawVal === "PT_RIO"
01760:             ? "RJ"
01761:             : op?.label || rawVal,
01762:       });
01763:     }
01764:   });
01765: 
01766:   const lotOptions = [
01767:     map.get("PT_RIO"),
01768:     map.get("FEDERAL"),
01769:     map.get("LOOK"),
01770:     map.get("NACIONAL"),
01771:     ...[...map.values()].filter(
01772:       (x) =>
01773:         ![
01774:           "PT_RIO",
01775:           "FEDERAL",
01776:           "LOOK",
01777:           "NACIONAL",
01778:         ].includes(
01779:           String(
01780:             x?.value || ""
01781:           ).toUpperCase()
01782:         )
01783:     ),
01784:   ].filter(Boolean);
01785: 
01786:   const curLot = String(lotteryKeySafe || "PT_RIO").toUpperCase();
01787:   const visibleTop3 = list.slice(0, 3);
01788: 
01789:   const historyAnchorYmd = useMemo(() => {
01790:   const loaded = String(loadedYmd || "").trim();
01791:   if (isYMD(loaded)) return loaded;
01792: 
01793:   const selected = String(ymdSafe || "").trim();
01794:   if (isYMD(selected)) return selected;
01795: 
01796:   const realized = (Array.isArray(timeline) ? timeline : [])
01797:     .filter((slot) => String(slot?.status || "").toLowerCase() === "validated")
01798:     .slice()
01799:     .sort((a, b) => {
01800:       const ta = `${a?.targetYmd || ""} ${a?.targetHour || ""}`;
01801:       const tb = `${b?.targetYmd || ""} ${b?.targetHour || ""}`;
01802:       return tb.localeCompare(ta);
01803:     });
01804: 
01805:   const fallback = String(realized?.[0]?.targetYmd || "").trim();
01806:   return isYMD(fallback) ? fallback : "";
01807: }, [loadedYmd, ymdSafe, timeline]);
01808: 
01809:   const historyRows = useMemo(() => {
01810:     const persistedRows = (
01811:       Array.isArray(persistedTop3History)
01812:         ? persistedTop3History
01813:         : []
01814:     )
01815:       .filter(
01816:         (entry) =>
01817:           String(entry?.targetYmd || "").trim() ===
01818:           historyAnchorYmd
01819:       )
01820:       .map((entry) => {
01821:         const snapshot = Array.isArray(entry?.snapshot)
01822:           ? entry.snapshot.slice(0, 3)
01823:           : [];
01824: 
01825:         const resultGrupo = Number(entry?.resultGrupo);
01826:         const hasResult =
01827:           Number.isFinite(resultGrupo) &&
01828:           resultGrupo >= 1 &&
01829:           resultGrupo <= 25;
01830: 
01831:         return {
01832:           targetKey: String(
01833:             entry?.id || entry?.targetKey || ""
01834:           ),
01835:           target: {
01836:             ymd: String(entry?.targetYmd || ""),
01837:             hour: String(entry?.targetHour || ""),
01838:           },
01839:           picks: Array.isArray(entry?.picks)
01840:             ? entry.picks
01841:             : snapshot
01842:                 .map((item) => Number(item?.grupo))
01843:                 .filter(Number.isFinite),
01844:           top3: snapshot,
01845:           result: hasResult ? resultGrupo : null,
01846:           grupo: hasResult ? resultGrupo : null,
01847:           animal: hasResult
01848:             ? String(
01849:                 entry?.resultAnimal ||
01850:                   getAnimalLabel(resultGrupo) ||
01851:                   ""
01852:               )
01853:             : "",
01854:           resultMilhar: String(
01855:             entry?.resultMilhar || ""
01856:           ),
01857:           hit:
01858:             Number(entry?.hitScore || 0) > 0,
01859:           hitType: String(entry?.hitType || ""),
01860:           hitScore: Number(entry?.hitScore || 0),
01861:           hitPosition: Number(
01862:             entry?.hitPosition ?? -1
01863:           ),
01864:           predictionPosition: Number(
01865:             entry?.predictionPosition ??
01866:               entry?.hitPosition ??
01867:               -1
01868:           ),
01869:           resultPosition: Number(
01870:             entry?.resultPosition ?? -1
01871:           ),
01872:           podiumMedal: String(
01873:             entry?.podiumMedal || ""
01874:           ),
01875:           resultTop3Groups: Array.isArray(
01876:             entry?.resultTop3Groups
01877:           )
01878:             ? entry.resultTop3Groups
01879:             : [],
01880:           resultTop3Milhares: Array.isArray(
01881:             entry?.resultTop3Milhares
01882:           )
01883:             ? entry.resultTop3Milhares
01884:             : [],
01885:           matchedGrupo: Number(
01886:             entry?.matchedGrupo ?? NaN
01887:           ),
01888:           matchedMilhar: normalizeMilharStr(
01889:             entry?.matchedMilhar || ""
01890:           ),
01891:           analysis: {
01892:             type: String(entry?.hitType || ""),
01893:             score: Number(entry?.hitScore || 0),
01894:             position: Number(
01895:               entry?.hitPosition ?? -1
01896:             ),
01897:             predictionPosition: Number(
01898:               entry?.predictionPosition ??
01899:                 entry?.hitPosition ??
01900:                 -1
01901:             ),
01902:             resultPosition: Number(
01903:               entry?.resultPosition ?? -1
01904:             ),
01905:             podiumMedal: String(
01906:               entry?.podiumMedal || ""
01907:             ),
01908:             matchedValue: String(
01909:               entry?.matchedValue || ""
01910:             ),
01911:             matchedGrupo: Number(
01912:               entry?.matchedGrupo ?? NaN
01913:             ),
01914:             matchedMilhar: normalizeMilharStr(
01915:               entry?.matchedMilhar || ""
01916:             ),
01917:           },
01918:         };
01919:       });
01920: 
01921:     const timelineRows = (
01922:       Array.isArray(timeline) ? timeline : []
01923:     )
01924:       .filter(
01925:         (slot) =>
01926:           String(slot?.targetYmd || "").trim() ===
01927:           historyAnchorYmd
01928:       )
01929:       .map((slot) => {
01930:         const slotTop3 = Array.isArray(slot?.top3)
01931:           ? slot.top3.slice(0, 3)
01932:           : [];
01933: 
01934:         const resultGrupo = getSlotResultGrupo(slot);
01935: 
01936:         const hasResult =
01937:           Number.isFinite(resultGrupo) &&
01938:           resultGrupo >= 1 &&
01939:           resultGrupo <= 25;
01940: 
01941:         const resultMilhar =
01942:           extractResultMilhar(slot);
01943: 
01944:         const officialPodium =
01945:           getOfficialPodium(slot);
01946: 
01947:         const analysis = analyzeTop3Hit(
01948:           slotTop3,
01949:           officialPodium
01950:         );
01951: 
01952:         return {
01953:           targetKey:
01954:             `${String(slot?.targetYmd || "")}` +
01955:             `__${String(slot?.targetHour || "")}`,
01956:           target: {
01957:             ymd: String(slot?.targetYmd || ""),
01958:             hour: String(slot?.targetHour || ""),
01959:           },
01960:           picks: slotTop3
01961:             .map((item) => Number(item?.grupo))
01962:             .filter(Number.isFinite),
01963:           top3: slotTop3,
01964:           result: hasResult
01965:             ? Number(resultGrupo)
01966:             : null,
01967:           grupo: hasResult
01968:             ? Number(resultGrupo)
01969:             : null,
01970:           animal: hasResult
01971:             ? getAnimalLabel(resultGrupo)
01972:             : "",
01973:           resultMilhar,
01974:           resultTop3Groups: officialPodium.map(
01975:             (item) => Number(item?.grupo)
01976:           ),
01977:           resultTop3Milhares: officialPodium.map(
01978:             (item) => String(item?.milhar || "")
01979:           ),
01980:           prizes: Array.isArray(slot?.prizes)
01981:             ? slot.prizes
01982:             : [],
01983:           analysis,
01984:           hit:
01985:             analysis.type !== "miss" &&
01986:             analysis.type !== "none",
01987:           hitType: analysis.type,
01988:           hitScore: Number(analysis.score || 0),
01989:           hitPosition: Number(
01990:             analysis.position ?? -1
01991:           ),
01992:         };
01993:       });
01994: 
01995:     // A timeline garante todos os horarios oficiais.
01996:     // O registro persistido prevalece quando realmente existe.
01997:     const rowsByTarget = new Map();
01998: 
01999:     for (const row of timelineRows) {
02000:       const key =
02001:         `${String(row?.target?.ymd || "")}` +
02002:         `__${String(row?.target?.hour || "")}`;
02003: 
02004:       rowsByTarget.set(key, row);
02005:     }
02006: 
02007:     for (const row of persistedRows) {
02008:       const key =
02009:         `${String(row?.target?.ymd || "")}` +
02010:         `__${String(row?.target?.hour || "")}`;
02011: 
02012:       const timelineRow = rowsByTarget.get(key);
02013: 
02014:       if (!timelineRow || timelineRow?.result == null) {
02015:         rowsByTarget.set(key, row);
02016:         continue;
02017:       }
02018: 
02019:       const resultGrupo = Number(timelineRow.result);
02020:       const resultMilhar = String(
02021:         timelineRow?.resultMilhar || ""
02022:       );
02023: 
02024:       const officialPodium = getOfficialPodium(timelineRow);
02025: 
02026:       const analysis = analyzeTop3Hit(
02027:         row?.top3,
02028:         officialPodium
02029:       );
02030: 
02031:       rowsByTarget.set(key, {
02032:         ...row,
02033:         result: resultGrupo,
02034:         grupo: resultGrupo,
02035:         animal:
02036:           timelineRow?.animal ||
02037:           getAnimalLabel(resultGrupo) ||
02038:           "",
02039:         resultMilhar,
02040:         resultTop3Groups: Array.isArray(
02041:           timelineRow?.resultTop3Groups
02042:         )
02043:           ? timelineRow.resultTop3Groups
02044:           : row?.resultTop3Groups || [],
02045:         resultTop3Milhares: Array.isArray(
02046:           timelineRow?.resultTop3Milhares
02047:         )
02048:           ? timelineRow.resultTop3Milhares
02049:           : row?.resultTop3Milhares || [],
02050:         matchedGrupo: Number(
02051:           analysis?.matchedGrupo ?? NaN
02052:         ),
02053:         matchedMilhar: normalizeMilharStr(
02054:           analysis?.matchedMilhar || ""
02055:         ),
02056:         analysis,
02057:         hit:
02058:           analysis.type !== "miss" &&
02059:           analysis.type !== "none",
02060:         hitType: analysis.type,
02061:         hitScore: Number(analysis.score || 0),
02062:         hitPosition: Number(
02063:           analysis.position ?? -1
02064:         ),
02065:       });
02066:     }
02067: 
02068:     const todayYmd = todayYMDLocalView();
02069: 
02070:     return Array.from(rowsByTarget.values())
02071:       .filter((row) => {
02072:         const targetYmd = String(
02073:           row?.target?.ymd || ""
02074:         ).trim();
02075: 
02076:         const hasResult = row?.result != null;
02077: 
02078:         // Resultado existente sempre permanece no histórico.
02079:         if (hasResult) return true;
02080: 
02081:         // Linha inválida não deve gerar card pendente.
02082:         if (!isYMD(targetYmd)) return false;
02083: 
02084:         // Pendência só é legítima no dia atual ou em data futura.
02085:         // Datas anteriores sem resultado representam sorteio inexistente,
02086:         // cancelado ou definitivamente não publicado.
02087:         return targetYmd >= todayYmd;
02088:       })
02089:       .sort((a, b) => {
02090:         const ah = hourBucketToSortValue(
02091:           a?.target?.hour
02092:         );
02093:         const bh = hourBucketToSortValue(
02094:           b?.target?.hour
02095:         );
02096: 
02097:         return ah - bh;
02098:       });
02099:   }, [
02100:     timeline,
02101:     historyAnchorYmd,
02102:     persistedTop3History,
02103:   ]);
02104: 
02105:   const historySummary = useMemo(() => {
02106:     const validated = historyRows.filter(
02107:       (item) => item?.result != null
02108:     );
02109: 
02110:     const exact = validated.filter(
02111:       (item) => item?.hitType === "hit_exact"
02112:     ).length;
02113: 
02114:     const centena = validated.filter(
02115:       (item) => item?.hitType === "hit_centena"
02116:     ).length;
02117: 
02118:     const grupo = validated.filter(
02119:       (item) => item?.hitType === "hit_grupo"
02120:     ).length;
02121: 
02122:     const misses = validated.filter(
02123:       (item) =>
02124:         item?.hitType === "miss" ||
02125:         item?.hitType === "none"
02126:     ).length;
02127: 
02128:     const totalScore = validated.reduce(
02129:       (sum, item) => sum + Number(item?.hitScore || 0),
02130:       0
02131:     );
02132: 
02133:     const average =
02134:       validated.length > 0
02135:         ? totalScore / validated.length
02136:         : 0;
02137: 
02138:     return {
02139:       total: historyRows.length,
02140:       validated: validated.length,
02141:       pending: Math.max(0, historyRows.length - validated.length),
02142:       exact,
02143:       centena,
02144:       grupo,
02145:       misses,
02146:       average,
02147:     };
02148:   }, [historyRows]);
02149: 
02150:   const todayForCalendar = todayYMDLocalView();
02151:   const selectedHistoryYmd = isYMD(ymdSafe)
02152:     ? ymdSafe
02153:     : todayForCalendar;
02154: 
02155:   const availableHistoryDates = useMemo(() => {
02156:     const dates = new Set();
02157: 
02158:     (
02159:       Array.isArray(controllerHistoryDates)
02160:         ? controllerHistoryDates
02161:         : []
02162:     ).forEach((date) => {
02163:       const ymd = String(date || "").trim();
02164: 
02165:       if (isYMD(ymd) && ymd <= todayForCalendar) {
02166:         dates.add(ymd);
02167:       }
02168:     });
02169: 
02170:     (Array.isArray(timeline) ? timeline : []).forEach(
02171:       (slot) => {
02172:         const ymd = String(
02173:           slot?.targetYmd || ""
02174:         ).trim();
02175: 
02176:         if (isYMD(ymd) && ymd <= todayForCalendar) {
02177:           dates.add(ymd);
02178:         }
02179:       }
02180:     );
02181: 
02182:     (
02183:       Array.isArray(persistedTop3History)
02184:         ? persistedTop3History
02185:         : []
02186:     ).forEach((entry) => {
02187:       const ymd = String(
02188:         entry?.targetYmd || ""
02189:       ).trim();
02190: 
02191:       if (isYMD(ymd) && ymd <= todayForCalendar) {
02192:         dates.add(ymd);
02193:       }
02194:     });
02195: 
02196:     return Array.from(dates).sort();
02197:   }, [
02198:     controllerHistoryDates,
02199:     timeline,
02200:     persistedTop3History,
02201:     todayForCalendar,
02202:   ]);
02203: 
02204:   const previousAvailableDate = useMemo(() => {
02205:     for (
02206:       let index = availableHistoryDates.length - 1;
02207:       index >= 0;
02208:       index -= 1
02209:     ) {
02210:       if (
02211:         availableHistoryDates[index] <
02212:         selectedHistoryYmd
02213:       ) {
02214:         return availableHistoryDates[index];
02215:       }
02216:     }
02217: 
02218:     return "";
02219:   }, [
02220:     availableHistoryDates,
02221:     selectedHistoryYmd,
02222:   ]);
02223: 
02224:   const nextAvailableDate = useMemo(() => {
02225:     return (
02226:       availableHistoryDates.find(
02227:         (date) => date > selectedHistoryYmd
02228:       ) || ""
02229:     );
02230:   }, [
02231:     availableHistoryDates,
02232:     selectedHistoryYmd,
02233:   ]);
02234: 
02235:   const canGoPrevious =
02236:     isYMD(previousAvailableDate);
02237: 
02238:   const canGoNext =
02239:     isYMD(nextAvailableDate);
02240: 
02241:   const goToHistoryDate = useCallback(
02242:     (nextYmd) => {
02243:       if (!isYMD(nextYmd)) return;
02244:       if (typeof setYmd !== "function") return;
02245:       setYmd(nextYmd);
02246:     },
02247:     [setYmd]
02248:   );
